        if (fence && layout !== "block") fail2();
        return Object.freeze({
          ...r,
          layout,
          content: node2(f.content, budget, 1, fail2, request),
          accessibleText: boundedText(f.accessibleText, budget, fail2, 8192),
          ...f.status === void 0 ? {} : { status: choice2(f.status, ["ready", "error"], fail2) },
          ...f.message === void 0 ? {} : { message: boundedText(f.message, budget, fail2, 1e3) }
        });
      },
      fail2
    );
    const result = Object.freeze({ fragments, complete: boolean(v.complete, fail2) });
    outputSize(result, fail2);
    canonicalSize(
      fragments.map((fragment) => fragment.content),
      fail2
    );
    return result;
  }
  function canonicalSize(nodes, fail2) {
    let bytes = 0;
    const context = { image: 0 };
    for (const node3 of nodes) {
      bytes += new TextEncoder().encode(serializeValidatedNode(node3, context)).byteLength;
      if (bytes > DOCUMENT_PRESENTATION_LIMITS.outputBytes) fail2();
    }
  }
  function serializeDocumentPresentationNode(value, request) {
    return serializeValidatedNode(copyDocumentPresentationNode(value, request), { image: 0 });
  }
  function serializeValidatedNode(validated, context) {
    const escape2 = (text2) => text2.replace(
      /[&<>"']/g,
      (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" })[c]
    );
    const math = (n) => {
      const attrs = Object.entries(n.attributes ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => {
        const names = {
          scriptLevel: "scriptlevel",
          displayStyle: "displaystyle",
          rowSpacingEm: "rowspacing",
          columnSpacingEm: "columnspacing",
          columnAlign: "columnalign",
          widthEm: "width",
          lspaceEm: "lspace",
          rspaceEm: "rspace"
        };
        const formatted = Array.isArray(v) ? v.map((x) => typeof x === "number" ? `${x}em` : x).join(" ") : k.endsWith("Em") ? `${v}em` : String(v);
        return ` ${names[k] ?? k}="${escape2(formatted)}"`;
      }).join("");
      return `<${n.tag}${n.tag === "math" ? ' xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"' : ""}${attrs}>${escape2(n.text ?? "")}${(n.children ?? []).map(math).join("")}</${n.tag}>`;
    };
    const vector = (p) => {
      const attrs = [];
      let text2 = "";
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(p)) {
        if (key === "type") continue;
        if (key === "text") {
          text2 = escape2(String(value));
          continue;
        }
        if (key === "tone") {
          attrs.push(`fill="currentColor"`);
          continue;
        }
        if (key === "fill" || key === "stroke") {
          attrs.push(`${key}="${value === "none" ? "none" : "currentColor"}"`);
          continue;
        }
        if (key === "radius") {
          attrs.push(`rx="${value}"`);
          continue;
        }
        if (key === "points") {
          attrs.push(
            `points="${value.map((p2) => `${p2.x},${p2.y}`).join(" ")}"`
          );
          continue;
        }
        const name = { strokeWidth: "stroke-width", fontSize: "font-size", anchor: "text-anchor" }[key] ?? key;
        attrs.push(`${name}="${escape2(String(value))}"`);
      }
      return `<${p.type} ${attrs.join(" ")}>${text2}</${p.type}>`;
    };
    const render = (n) => {
      if (n.type === "svg-image")
        return serializeDocumentSvgImage(n, { idPrefix: `document-svg-${context.image++}` });
      if (n.type === "text") return escape2(n.text);
      if (n.type === "math") return math(n.tree);
      if (n.type === "vector")
        return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 ${n.width} ${n.height}" width="${n.width}" height="${n.height}">${n.children.map(vector).join("")}</svg>`;
      if (n.type === "reference")
        return `<span data-source-from="${n.target.from}" data-source-to="${n.target.to}">${escape2(n.label)}</span>`;
      const tag = n.type === "group" ? "span" : n.type === "emphasis" ? "em" : n.type;
      return `<${tag}>${n.children.map(render).join("")}</${tag}>`;
    };
    return render(validated);
  }
  function copyDocumentPresentationAggregate(value, request, declarations) {
    return copyDocumentPresentationResult(value, request, declarations);
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/document-svg-image.ts
  var imageDecodeDeadlineMs = 1e4;
  function createOwnedDocumentSvgImage(document, value, changed) {
    const canonical = serializeDocumentPresentationNode(value);
    const [x, y, width, height] = value.rasterBounds;
    const left = Math.floor(x);
    const top = Math.floor(y);
    const right = Math.ceil(x + width);
    const bottom = Math.ceil(y + height);
    const insetLeft = Math.max(0, -left);
    const insetTop = Math.max(0, -top);
    const insetRight = x + width > value.width ? right - value.width : 0;
    const insetBottom = y + height > value.height ? bottom - value.height : 0;
    const element = document.createElement("span");
    element.style.display = "inline-block";
    element.style.position = "relative";
    element.style.width = `${value.width + insetLeft + insetRight}px`;
    element.style.height = `${value.height + insetTop + insetBottom}px`;
    element.style.maxWidth = "none";
    element.style.overflow = "visible";
    element.style.verticalAlign = "middle";
    element.contentEditable = "false";
    const slot = document.createElement("span");
    slot.style.display = "block";
    slot.style.position = "absolute";
    slot.style.left = `${insetLeft}px`;
    slot.style.top = `${insetTop}px`;
    slot.style.width = `${value.width}px`;
    slot.style.height = `${value.height}px`;
    slot.style.overflow = "visible";
    element.append(slot);
    const image2 = document.createElement("img");
    image2.alt = value.title ?? "";
    image2.draggable = false;
    image2.width = right - left;
    image2.height = bottom - top;
    image2.style.position = "absolute";
    image2.style.left = `${left}px`;
    image2.style.top = `${top}px`;
    image2.style.display = "block";
    image2.style.width = `${right - left}px`;
    image2.style.height = `${bottom - top}px`;
    image2.style.maxWidth = "none";
    image2.style.maxHeight = "none";
    slot.append(image2);
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([canonical], { type: "image/svg+xml" }));
    let state = "pending";
    let timer;
    const notify = () => queueMicrotask(() => {
      if (state !== "disposed") changed();
    });
    const failed = () => {
      if (state === "disposed" || state === "failed") return;
      state = "failed";
      clearTimeout(timer);
      notify();
    };
    image2.addEventListener("error", failed);
    timer = setTimeout(failed, imageDecodeDeadlineMs);
    try {
      image2.src = url;
      void image2.decode().then(() => {
        if (state !== "pending") return;
        clearTimeout(timer);
        state = "decoded";
        notify();
      }, failed);
    } catch {
      failed();
    }
    return {
      element,
      get state() {
        return state;
      },
      dispose() {
        if (state === "disposed") return;
        state = "disposed";
        clearTimeout(timer);
        image2.removeEventListener("error", failed);
        image2.removeAttribute("src");
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }
    };
  }

  var mathNS = "http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML";

  var tones2 = {
    foreground: "currentColor",
    muted: "var(--milkup-presentation-muted, currentColor)",
    accent: "var(--milkup-presentation-accent, currentColor)",
    background: "var(--milkup-presentation-background, transparent)",

  function createOwnedPresentationNode(document, value, navigate, image2) {
    const root = copyDocumentPresentationNode(value);
    function math(node3) {
      const element = document.createElementNS(mathNS, node3.tag);
      if (node3.text !== void 0) element.textContent = node3.text;
      const names = {
        display: "display",
        mathvariant: "mathvariant",
        stretchy: "stretchy",
        fence: "fence",
        separator: "separator",
        accent: "accent",
        scriptLevel: "scriptlevel",
        displayStyle: "displaystyle",
        rowSpacingEm: "rowspacing",
        columnSpacingEm: "columnspacing",
        columnAlign: "columnalign",
        widthEm: "width",
        lspaceEm: "lspace",
        rspaceEm: "rspace"
      };
      for (const [key, value2] of Object.entries(node3.attributes ?? {})) {
        const name = names[key];
        if (!name) throw new Error("Unsupported MathML attribute");
        element.setAttribute(
          name,
          Array.isArray(value2) ? value2.map((item) => `${item}${key.endsWith("Em") ? "em" : ""}`).join(" ") : `${value2}${key.endsWith("Em") ? "em" : ""}`
        );
      }
      for (const child of node3.children ?? []) element.append(math(child));
      return element;
    }
    function build(node3) {
      if (node3.type === "svg-image") {
        if (!image2) throw new Error("SVG image presentation requires a lifetime owner");
        return image2(node3);
      }
      if (node3.type === "text") return document.createTextNode(node3.text);
      if (node3.type === "math") return math(node3.tree);
      if (node3.type === "reference") {
        const button = document.createElement("button");
        button.type = "button";
        button.className = "milkup-document-reference";
        button.textContent = node3.label;
        referenceTargets.set(button, node3.target);
        button.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
          navigate?.(node3.target);
        });
        return button;
      }
      if (node3.type === "vector") {
        const svg = document.createElementNS(svgNS, "svg");
        svg.setAttribute("viewBox", `0 0 ${node3.width} ${node3.height}`);
        svg.setAttribute("width", String(node3.width));
        svg.setAttribute("height", String(node3.height));
        svg.style.maxWidth = "none";
        svg.style.display = "block";
        for (const primitive2 of node3.children) {
          const element2 = document.createElementNS(svgNS, primitive2.type);
          for (const [key, value2] of Object.entries(primitive2)) {
            if (key === "type") continue;
            if (key === "text") element2.textContent = String(value2);
            else if (key === "points")
              element2.setAttribute(
                "points",
                value2.map((point) => `${point.x},${point.y}`).join(" ")
              );
            else if (key === "stroke" || key === "fill" || key === "tone")
              element2.setAttribute(key === "tone" ? "fill" : key, tones2[value2]);
            else
              element2.setAttribute(
                {
                  radius: "rx",
                  strokeWidth: "stroke-width",
                  fontSize: "font-size",
                  anchor: "text-anchor"
                }[key] ?? key,
                String(value2)
              );
          }
          if (primitive2.type !== "text" && !("fill" in primitive2))
            element2.setAttribute("fill", "none");
          svg.append(element2);
        }
        return svg;
      }
      const element = document.createElement(
        { group: "span", emphasis: "em", code: "code", sup: "sup", sub: "sub" }[node3.type]
      );
      for (const child of node3.children) element.append(build(child));
      return element;
    }
    return build(root);
  }
  function fragmentKey(fragment) {

  var DocumentPresentation = class {
    constructor(root, content, config, snapshot, layout, reveal) {

      this.content = content;

      this.layout = layout;
      this.reveal = reveal;

      this.observer = new MutationObserver(() => this.schedule());

      this.resize = typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined" ? void 0 : new ResizeObserver(() => {
        const view = this.root.ownerDocument.defaultView;
        if (this.stopped || this.resizeFrame || !view) return;

          if (this.stopped) return;
          this.layout();
          if (!this.stopped) this.schedule();

      };
    };
    referencePointerUp = (event) => {
      if (event.pointerId !== this.referencePointer?.id) return;
      clearTimeout(this.referencePointerTimer);
      this.referencePointerTimer = setTimeout(this.releaseReferencePointer, 0);
    };
    referencePointerCancel = (event) => {
      if (event.pointerId === this.referencePointer?.id) this.releaseReferencePointer();
    };
    referencePointerMove = (event) => {
      if (event.pointerId === this.referencePointer?.id && event.buttons === 0)
        this.releaseReferencePointer();
    };
    referenceVisibility = () => {
      if (this.root.ownerDocument.visibilityState === "hidden") this.releaseReferencePointer();
    };
    /** Internal background-layout gate; never changes focus or synthesizes activation. */
    hasReferencePointer() {
      const pointer = this.referencePointer;
      if (!pointer) return false;
      const current = this.snapshot(), before = pointer.snapshot;
      if (this.stopped || this.config !== pointer.config || !this.content.contains(pointer.element) || current.mode !== "live" || current.sourceIdentity !== before.sourceIdentity || current.sourceVersion !== before.sourceVersion || current.documentVersion !== before.documentVersion || !sameSelection(current.selection, before.selection)) {
        this.releaseReferencePointer();
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    }
    waitForReferencePointer(signal) {
      if (!this.hasReferencePointer() || signal?.aborted) return void 0;
      const pointer = this.referencePointer;

        const resume = () => {
          pointer.waiters.delete(resume);
          signal?.removeEventListener("abort", resume);
          resolve();
        };
        pointer.waiters.add(resume);
        signal?.addEventListener("abort", resume, { once: true });
      });
    }
    clearProxyNavigation = () => {
      this.tabOrigin = void 0;
      this.proxyNavigation = void 0;
      clearTimeout(this.tabOriginTimer);
      this.tabOriginTimer = void 0;
    };
    navigationKeyDown = (event) => {
      const tab = event.key === "Tab" || event.key === "Unidentified" && event.code === "Tab";
      if (event.key === "Shift" && !event.altKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey) return;
      if (tab && event.shiftKey && !event.altKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey && event.target === this.root.querySelector(".milkup-input-proxy"))

      this.clearProxyNavigation();
      if (tab && !event.shiftKey && !event.altKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey && event.target instanceof HTMLElement && event.target.closest("[data-document-presentation]") && this.content.contains(event.target)) {

      }
    };
    navigationFocusIn = (event) => {

        this.proxyNavigation = { element: origin.element, snapshot: this.snapshot() };
    };
    navigationFocusOut = (event) => {
      if (event.target === this.root.querySelector(".milkup-input-proxy")) this.clearProxyNavigation();
    };
    /** Let the browser reverse the immediately preceding preview-to-proxy Tab. */
    returnToPreviewWithTab(event) {
      if (event.key !== "Tab" && !(event.key === "Unidentified" && event.code === "Tab")) return false;
      const navigation = this.proxyNavigation;
      if (!navigation) return false;
      this.clearProxyNavigation();
      const current = this.snapshot(), before = navigation.snapshot;
      return (event.key === "Tab" || event.key === "Unidentified" && event.code === "Tab") && event.shiftKey && !event.altKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey && !event.isComposing && event.target === this.root.querySelector(".milkup-input-proxy") && this.content.contains(navigation.element) && current.mode === "live" && current.sourceIdentity === before.sourceIdentity && current.sourceVersion === before.sourceVersion && sameSelection(current.selection, before.selection);
    }
    observe() {
      this.observer.observe(this.content, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    setConfig(config) {
      this.config = config;
      this.invalidate();
    }
    expandLineWindow(fromLine, toLine) {
      const snapshot = this.snapshot();
      if (snapshot.sourceIdentity !== this.blocksIdentity || snapshot.sourceVersion !== this.blocksVersion)
        return { fromLine, toLine };
      for (const block of this.blocks)
        if (fromLine <= block.toLine && toLine >= block.fromLine) {
          fromLine = Math.min(fromLine, block.fromLine);
          toLine = Math.max(toLine, block.toLine);
        }
      return { fromLine, toLine };
    }
    /** Refresh provider data without withdrawing an unchanged, validated presentation. */
    refresh() {
      this.cancelPreparationRetry();
      const current = this.snapshot();
      if (!this.config || current.mode !== "live" || !this.cache || this.cache.identity !== current.sourceIdentity || this.cache.documentVersion !== current.documentVersion || !this.cache.key.startsWith(`${current.sourceVersion}:`) || !sameSelection(this.cache.response.request.selection, current.selection)) {
        this.invalidate();
        return;
      }
      this.epoch++;
      this.job?.controller.abort();
      this.job = void 0;
      this.failedKey = void 0;
      this.schedule();
    }
    invalidate() {
      this.releaseReferencePointer();
      this.cancelPreparationRetry();
      this.epoch++;
      this.job?.controller.abort();
      this.job = void 0;
      this.cache = void 0;
      this.failedKey = void 0;
      this.blocks = [];
      this.restore();
      this.layout();
      this.schedule();
    }
    destroy() {
      this.releaseReferencePointer();
      this.cancelPreparationRetry();
      this.clearProxyNavigation();
      this.stopped = true;
      this.job?.controller.abort();
      this.observer.disconnect();
      this.resize?.disconnect();
      if (this.resizeFrame) {
        this.resizeFrame.view.cancelAnimationFrame(this.resizeFrame.id);
        this.resizeFrame = void 0;
      }
      this.root.removeEventListener("pointerdown", this.referencePointerDown, true);
      this.root.removeEventListener("keydown", this.releaseReferencePointer, true);
      this.root.removeEventListener("beforeinput", this.releaseReferencePointer, true);

    schedule() {
      if (this.stopped || this.scheduled) return;
      this.scheduled = true;
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        this.scheduled = false;
        if (!this.stopped) this.sync();
      });

    /** Called before cursor/selection geometry, including optimistic edits. */
    selectionChanged() {
      this.hasReferencePointer();
      if (this.painting || this.stopped) return;
      if (!this.config && !this.saved.size && !this.job) return;
      const current = this.snapshot();
      if (this.preparationRetry && !this.preparationRetry.matches()) this.cancelPreparationRetry();

        this.job = void 0;
      }
      if (current.mode !== "live" || !this.config || this.cache && (this.cache.identity !== current.sourceIdentity || this.cache.documentVersion !== current.documentVersion || !this.cache.key.startsWith(`${current.sourceVersion}:`) || !sameSelection(this.cache.response.request.selection, current.selection))) {
        this.restore();
        this.schedule();
        return;
      }
      if (this.cache) this.paint(this.cache.response);
      this.schedule();

    window(current) {
      const children = this.content.children;
      if (!children.length) return void 0;
      const rect = this.root.getBoundingClientRect();
      const measured = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      const owners = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      const lineRect = (line) => {
        const ownerId = line.dataset.presentationOwner;
        if (ownerId && !owners.has(ownerId))

            ownerId,
            this.content.querySelector(`.milkup-line[data-line="${ownerId}"]`)
          );
        const owner = (ownerId ? owners.get(ownerId) : void 0) ?? line;

        if (!bounds) {

            break;
          }

        }

          continue;
        const expanded = [];

      });

        from,
        to,

        completeStart: from === 0,
        completeEnd: Number(last.dataset.line) === current.lineCount,
        selection: { anchor: current.selection.anchor, head: current.selection.head },

    sync() {

    }
    cancelPreparationRetry() {
      clearTimeout(this.preparationRetry?.timer);
      this.preparationRetry = void 0;
    }
    valid(job) {
      const current = this.snapshot();

    }
    fallback(message) {

        }
      }

    /** The view invokes this around a synchronous source-DOM replacement. */
    updateContent(update) {
      const before = this.snapshot();
      const cache = this.cache;
      const config = this.config;
      const epoch = this.epoch;
      const eligible = config !== void 0 && before.mode === "live" && !this.stopped;
      const reusable = cache !== void 0 && config !== void 0 && before.mode === "live" && cache.identity === before.sourceIdentity && cache.documentVersion === before.documentVersion && cache.key.startsWith(`${before.sourceVersion}:`) && sameSelection(cache.response.request.selection, before.selection);

      };

            measured = true;
          } finally {

          }

          );

        }
        if (current() && reusable && this.cache === cache) {
          this.restoreFocus(focus);
          this.restorePreviewScroll(previewScroll, cache.response);
        }
      }
      return measured;
    }
    captureFocus() {
      const focused = this.content.ownerDocument.activeElement;
      const focusedPreview = focused instanceof HTMLElement && this.content.contains(focused) ? focused.closest("[data-document-presentation]") : null;
      const focusedReference = focused instanceof HTMLElement ? referenceTargets.get(focused) : void 0;
      return focused instanceof HTMLElement && focusedPreview ? { focused, focusedPreview, focusedReference } : void 0;
    }
    restoreFocus(focus) {
      if (!focus) return;
      const { focused, focusedPreview, focusedReference } = focus;
      if (focusedPreview && (this.content.ownerDocument.activeElement === this.content.ownerDocument.body || this.content.ownerDocument.activeElement === focused)) {
        const replacement = Array.from(
          this.content.querySelectorAll("[data-document-presentation]")
        ).find(
          (element) => element.dataset.presentationFrom === focusedPreview.dataset.presentationFrom && element.dataset.presentationTo === focusedPreview.dataset.presentationTo
        );
        if (focused === focusedPreview && replacement && replacement.tabIndex >= 0)
          replacement.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        else if (focusedReference && replacement) {
          const reference = Array.from(
            replacement.querySelectorAll(".milkup-document-reference")
          ).find((element) => {
            const target = referenceTargets.get(element);
            return target && target.documentId === focusedReference.documentId && target.version === focusedReference.version && target.from === focusedReference.from && target.to === focusedReference.to && element.textContent === focused?.textContent;
          });
          reference?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        }
      }
    }
    captureScrollAnchor(selection = this.snapshot().selection) {
      const viewport = this.root.getBoundingClientRect();
      const sourceLines = Array.from(this.content.children).filter(
        (child) => child instanceof HTMLElement && child.classList.contains("milkup-line")
      );
      const selectedAnchor = [selection.head, selection.anchor].map(
        (position) => sourceLines.find((line) => {
          if (Number(line.dataset.from) > position || Number(line.dataset.to) < position)
            return false;
          const rect = line.getBoundingClientRect();
          return rect.height > 0 && rect.bottom > viewport.top && rect.top < viewport.bottom;
        })
      ).find((line) => line !== void 0);
      const anchor = selectedAnchor ?? sourceLines.find((child) => child.getBoundingClientRect().bottom >= viewport.top);
      return anchor ? { line: anchor, top: anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top } : void 0;
    }
    restoreScrollAnchor(anchor) {
      if (anchor && this.content.contains(anchor.line) && anchor.line.dataset.presentationCollapsed !== "true")
        this.root.scrollTop += anchor.line.getBoundingClientRect().top - anchor.top;
    }
    restore(preserveImages = false) {
      if (!preserveImages) {
        this.imageGeneration++;
        this.disposeFragmentImages();
      }
      const anchor = !this.painting && !this.stopped && (this.saved.size || this.notice) ? this.captureScrollAnchor() : void 0;
      this.notice?.remove();
      this.notice = void 0;
      if (!this.saved.size && !this.painted) {
        if (this.content.dataset.documentPresentationStatus) {
          delete this.content.dataset.documentPresentationStatus;
          this.content.removeAttribute("aria-description");
        }
        this.restoreScrollAnchor(anchor);
        return;
      }
      this.observer.disconnect();
      this.painted = void 0;
      for (const element of Array.from(this.content.querySelectorAll("[data-document-presentation]")))
        this.resize?.unobserve(element);
      for (const [line, saved] of this.saved) {
        if (this.content.contains(line)) {
          line.replaceChildren(...saved.nodes);
          line.style.display = saved.display;
          line.style.minHeight = saved.minHeight;
          delete line.dataset.presentationCollapsed;
          delete line.dataset.presentationOwner;
        }
      }
      this.saved.clear();
      delete this.content.dataset.documentPresentationStatus;
      this.content.removeAttribute("aria-description");
      this.restoreScrollAnchor(anchor);
      if (!this.stopped) this.observe();
    }
    paint(response) {
      if (this.painting) return;
      const context = this.paintContext();
      const currentSelection = context.selection;
      const selectionKey = `${currentSelection.anchor}:${currentSelection.head}`;
      const childLines = Array.from(this.content.children);
      if (this.painted?.response === response && this.samePaintContext(this.painted.context, context) && this.painted.selection === selectionKey && this.painted.imageRevision === this.imageRevision && childLines.length === this.painted.lines.length && childLines.every((line, index) => line === this.painted.lines[index]))

      if (this.painted?.response === response && this.samePaintContext(this.painted.context, context) && this.painted.imageRevision !== this.imageRevision && this.successfulImageRevision === this.imageRevision && !Array.from(this.fragmentImages.values()).some((owner) => owner.failed) && this.hasReferencePointer()) {

          const waiting = this.waitForReferencePointer();
          if (waiting) {
            this.referenceImagePaintPending = true;
            void waiting.then(() => {
              this.referenceImagePaintPending = false;
              this.schedule();
            });
          }
        }
        return;
      }

      this.observer.disconnect();

        response.result.fragments.map((fragment) => [fragmentKey(fragment), fragment])
      );
      for (const [key, previous] of this.previousFragmentImages) {
        const replacement = replacements.get(key);
        if (!replacement || replacement.status === "error" || !this.sameImageContext(previous.owner, response, context))
          this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(key);
      }
      for (const [fragment, owner] of Array.from(this.fragmentImages)) {
        if (owner.response === response && this.sameImageContext(owner, response, context)) continue;
        const replacement = replacements.get(fragmentKey(fragment));
        const decoded = !owner.failed && owner.images.every((image2) => image2.state === "decoded");
        if (replacement && decoded && this.sameImageContext(owner, response, context)) {
          if (owner.signature === JSON.stringify(replacement)) {
            this.fragmentImages.delete(fragment);
            owner.fragment = replacement;
            owner.response = response;
            this.fragmentImages.set(replacement, owner);
            this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(fragmentKey(fragment));
            continue;
          }
          if (replacement.status !== "error" && this.hasFragmentImages(replacement)) {
            this.fragmentImages.delete(fragment);
            this.retainPreviousFragmentImages(owner, response);
            continue;
          }
        }
        this.disposeFragmentImages(fragment);
      }
      this.restore(true);
      this.observer.disconnect();
      try {
        const { anchor: selectionAnchor, head } = this.snapshot().selection;
        const start = Math.min(selectionAnchor, head), end = Math.max(selectionAnchor, head);
        const candidates = response.result.fragments.filter(
          (fragment) => !(start <= fragment.to && end >= fragment.from)
        );
        const fragments = [];
        const blocks = [];
        const allLines = this.content.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line");
        for (const fragment of candidates) {
          if (fragment.status === "error") {
            this.fallback(fragment.message ?? "\u9884\u89C8\u4E0D\u53EF\u7528\uFF0C\u4FDD\u7559 Markdown \u6E90\u7801\u3002");
            continue;
          }

            (line) => Number(line.dataset.from) < fragment.to && Number(line.dataset.to) >= fragment.from
          );
          if (!lines.length || Number(lines[0].dataset.from) > fragment.from || Number(lines.at(-1).dataset.to) < fragment.to) {
            this.fallback("\u9884\u89C8\u8303\u56F4\u5C1A\u672A\u5B8C\u6574\u52A0\u8F7D\uFF0C\u4FDD\u7559 Markdown \u6E90\u7801\u3002");
            continue;
          }
          if (fragment.layout === "inline" && lines.length !== 1) continue;
          const images = this.prepareFragmentImages(fragment, response, context);
          if (images) {
            retainedImages.add(fragment);
            if (images.failed) {
              this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(fragmentKey(fragment));
              this.fallback("\u9884\u89C8\u4E0D\u53EF\u7528\uFF0C\u4FDD\u7559 Markdown \u6E90\u7801\u3002");
              continue;

              if (!previous || previous.images.some((image2) => image2.state !== "decoded")) continue;
              fragments.push(previous.fragment);
            } else {
              this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(fragmentKey(fragment));
              fragments.push(fragment);
            }
          } else {
            this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(fragmentKey(fragment));
            fragments.push(fragment);
          }

              toLine: Number(lines.at(-1).dataset.line)

          );

          for (const fragment of relevant) {

            cursor2 = Math.max(cursor2, Math.min(fragment.to, to));

          } else line.style.minHeight = "1em";

          ...previousBlocks.filter(
            (previous) => !blocks.some((block) => block.fromLine === previous.fromLine)
          ),
          ...blocks
        ].slice(-128);

        this.blocksVersion = snapshot.sourceVersion;

        this.restoreFocus(focus);
        this.restorePreviewScroll(previewScroll, response);
        this.painted = {
          response,
          context,
          selection: selectionKey,
          imageRevision: this.imageRevision,
          lines: childLines
        };

          if (!retainedImages.has(fragment)) this.disposeFragmentImages(fragment);

        for (const key of this.previousFragmentImages.keys())
          if (!retainedKeys.has(key)) this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(key);

        this.observe();
      }
    }
    sameImageContext(owner, response, context) {
      const previous = owner.response.request, next = response.request;
      return owner.generation === this.imageGeneration && this.samePaintContext(owner.context, context) && previous.documentId === next.documentId && previous.version === next.version && previous.theme === next.theme && previous.width === next.width && previous.target === next.target && JSON.stringify(owner.response.declarations) === JSON.stringify(response.declarations);
    }

    }
    retainPreviousFragmentImages(owner, response) {

      this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(key);

          owner.fragment

          response.declarations

        this.releaseReferencePointer();

        for (const current of this.fragmentImages.values()) {
          if (fragmentKey(current.fragment) !== key) continue;
          current.failed = true;

        }
        this.imageRevision++;
        this.schedule();
      }, imageDecodeDeadlineMs);
      this.previousFragmentImages.set(key, { owner, timer });
    }
    disposePreviousFragmentImages(key) {

      if (!previous) return;
      this.previousFragmentImages.delete(key);
      clearTimeout(previous.timer);
      for (const image2 of previous.owner.images) image2.dispose();
    }
    disposeFragmentImages(fragment) {
      if (fragment) {
        const owner = this.fragmentImages.get(fragment);
        this.fragmentImages.delete(fragment);
        for (const image2 of owner?.images ?? []) image2.dispose();
      } else {
        for (const owner of this.fragmentImages.values())
          for (const image2 of owner.images) image2.dispose();
        this.fragmentImages.clear();
        for (const key of this.previousFragmentImages.keys()) this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(key);
      }
    }
    prepareFragmentImages(fragment, response, context) {
      const existing = this.fragmentImages.get(fragment);
      if (existing) return existing;
      const nodes = [];
      const collect = (node3) => {
        if (node3.type === "svg-image") nodes.push(node3);
        else if (node3.type === "group" || node3.type === "emphasis" || node3.type === "code" || node3.type === "sup" || node3.type === "sub")
          for (const child of node3.children) collect(child);
      };

        fragment,
        response,
        signature: JSON.stringify(fragment),
        context,

        images: [],

      };

          if (owner.images.some((image2) => image2.state !== "decoded")) {
            this.releaseReferencePointer();
            this.disposePreviousFragmentImages(key);
            this.imageRevision++;
            this.schedule();
          }
          return;
        }
        if (this.fragmentImages.get(currentFragment) !== owner) return;
        if (this.stopped || this.cache?.response !== owner.response || owner.generation !== this.imageGeneration || !this.root.isConnected || !this.content.isConnected || !this.samePaintContext(owner.context, this.paintContext())) {
          this.disposeFragmentImages(currentFragment);
          return;
        }

          this.releaseReferencePointer();
          owner.failed = true;
          for (const image2 of owner.images) image2.dispose();
        }

          if (!owner.failed) this.successfulImageRevision = this.imageRevision;
          this.schedule();
        }
      };

      } catch {

        for (const image2 of owner.images) image2.dispose();
      }
      return owner;
    }
    paintContext() {
      const snapshot = this.snapshot();
      return {
        mode: snapshot.mode,
        sourceIdentity: snapshot.sourceIdentity,
        sourceVersion: snapshot.sourceVersion,
        documentVersion: snapshot.documentVersion,
        config: this.config,
        selection: { ...snapshot.selection }
      };
    }
    samePaintContext(a, b) {
      return a.mode === b.mode && a.sourceIdentity === b.sourceIdentity && a.sourceVersion === b.sourceVersion && a.documentVersion === b.documentVersion && a.config === b.config && sameSelection(a.selection, b.selection);
    }
    displayedFragment(fragment) {
      return this.previousFragmentImages.get(fragmentKey(fragment))?.owner.fragment ?? fragment;
    }
    /** Keep offsets only within this synchronous replacement, never across documents or edits. */
    capturePreviewScroll() {
      const painted = this.painted;
      if (!painted || !this.samePaintContext(painted.context, this.paintContext())) return;
      const fragments = new Map(
        painted.response.result.fragments.map((fragment) => [
          fragmentKey(fragment),
          this.displayedFragment(fragment)
        ])
      );
      const positions = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      for (const node3 of Array.from(
        this.content.querySelectorAll("[data-document-presentation]")
      )) {
        if (positions.size >= DOCUMENT_PRESENTATION_LIMITS.fragments) break;
        if (node3.scrollTop === 0 && node3.scrollLeft === 0) continue;
        const key = `${node3.dataset.documentPresentation}:${node3.dataset.presentationFrom}:${node3.dataset.presentationTo}`;
        const fragment = fragments.get(key);
        if (fragment && fragment.status !== "error")
          positions.set(key, { fragment, top: node3.scrollTop, left: node3.scrollLeft });
      }
      return positions.size ? { context: painted.context, positions } : void 0;
    }
    restorePreviewScroll(saved, response) {
      if (!saved || !this.samePaintContext(saved.context, this.paintContext())) return;
      const fragments = new Map(
        response.result.fragments.map((fragment) => [
          fragmentKey(fragment),
          this.displayedFragment(fragment)
        ])
      );
      for (const node3 of Array.from(
        this.content.querySelectorAll("[data-document-presentation]")
      )) {
        const key = `${node3.dataset.documentPresentation}:${node3.dataset.presentationFrom}:${node3.dataset.presentationTo}`;
        const position = saved.positions.get(key);
        const fragment = fragments.get(key);
        if (!position || !fragment || fragment.status === "error" || fragment !== position.fragment && JSON.stringify(fragment) !== JSON.stringify(position.fragment))
          continue;
        node3.scrollTop = position.top;
        node3.scrollLeft = position.left;
      }
    }
    preview(fragment, request) {
      const element = this.content.ownerDocument.createElement("span");
      element.dataset.documentPresentation = fragment.layout;
      element.dataset.presentationFrom = String(fragment.from);
      element.dataset.presentationTo = String(fragment.to);
      element.className = "milkup-document-presentation";
      element.setAttribute("aria-label", fragment.accessibleText);
      element.style.display = fragment.layout === "block" ? "block" : "inline-block";
      element.style.maxWidth = "100%";
      element.style.overflow = "auto";
      element.style.verticalAlign = "middle";
      element.style.maxHeight = "min(480px, 65vh)";
      element.setAttribute("role", "group");
      if (fragment.layout === "block") element.tabIndex = 0;
      let imageIndex = 0;
      element.append(
        createOwnedPresentationNode(
          this.content.ownerDocument,
          fragment.content,
          (target) => {
            if (this.cache?.response.request !== request || !this.content.contains(element)) return;
            const failed = () => {
              if (!this.stopped && this.cache?.response.request === request)
                this.fallback("\u5BFC\u822A\u76EE\u6807\u5DF2\u5931\u6548\u3002");
            };
            try {
              void Promise.resolve(this.config?.onNavigate?.(target)).catch(failed);
            } catch {
              failed();
            }
          },
          () => {
            const owner = this.fragmentImages.get(fragment) ?? this.previousFragmentImages.get(fragmentKey(fragment))?.owner;
            const image2 = owner?.images[imageIndex++];
            if (!image2 || image2.state !== "decoded") throw new Error("SVG image is not decoded");
            return image2.element;
          }
        )
      );
      element.addEventListener("mousedown", (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (!(event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest("button")))
          event.preventDefault();
      });
      element.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => event.stopPropagation());
      element.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (event.target === element && event.key === "Enter") {
          event.preventDefault();
          this.reveal(fragment.from, fragment.to);
        }
      });
      element.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (!(event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest("button")))
          this.reveal(fragment.from, fragment.to);
      });
      return element;
    }
  };
  function sameSelection(left, right) {
    return left.anchor === right.anchor && left.head === right.head;
  }
  function cloneMappedSlice(line, from, to) {
    const wrapper = line.ownerDocument.createElement("span");
    wrapper.dataset.from = String(from);
    wrapper.dataset.to = String(to);
    function copy(node3, position) {
      if (node3 instanceof Text) {
        const end2 = position + node3.length;
        const text2 = node3.data.slice(
          Math.max(0, from - position),
          Math.max(0, Math.min(node3.length, to - position))
        );
        return {
          ...end2 > from && position < to && text2 ? { node: node3.ownerDocument.createTextNode(text2) } : {},
          end: end2
        };
      }
      if (!(node3 instanceof HTMLElement)) return { end: position };
      if (node3.dataset.documentPresentation !== void 0) return { end: position };
      const start = node3.dataset.from !== void 0 ? Number(node3.dataset.from) : position;
      let cursor3 = start;
      const clone = node3.cloneNode(false);
      for (const child of Array.from(node3.childNodes)) {
        const result = copy(child, cursor3);
        cursor3 = result.end;
        if (result.node) clone.append(result.node);
      }
      const end = node3.dataset.to !== void 0 ? Number(node3.dataset.to) : cursor3;
      if (node3.dataset.from !== void 0) {
        clone.dataset.from = String(Math.max(from, start));
        clone.dataset.to = String(Math.min(to, end));
      }
      return {
        ...(clone.childNodes.length || node3.matches("button, input, br, [data-from][data-to]")) && end > from && start < to ? { node: clone } : {},
        end
      };
    }
    let cursor2 = Number(line.dataset.from);
    for (const child of Array.from(line.childNodes)) {
      const result = copy(child, cursor2);
      cursor2 = result.end;
      if (result.node) wrapper.append(result.node);
    }
    return wrapper;
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/image-presentation.ts
  function managedImageURL(value) {
    try {
      const path = decodeURIComponent(value);
      return /^(?:assets|[^/\\:?#\u0000-\u001f]+\.assets)\/[^/\\:?#\u0000-\u001f]+\.(?:png|jpe?g|webp)$/i.test(
        path
      ) && !path.split("/").some((part) => part === "." || part === "..");
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
  var ImagePasteSession = class {
    constructor(handler, onError, emptyHandler) {
      this.handler = handler;
      this.onError = onError;
      this.emptyHandler = emptyHandler;
    }
    pending = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    handle(event, selection) {
      const data = event.clipboardData;
      if (!data) return false;
      const files = Array.from(event.clipboardData?.files ?? []).filter(
        (file) => /^image\/(?:png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(file.type)
      );
      if (!files.length) {
        if (!this.emptyHandler || data.files.length || data.items?.length || data.types?.length || data.getData("text/plain") || data.getData("text/html"))
          return false;
        this.abort();
        const controller2 = new AbortController();
        this.pending.add(controller2);
        try {
          const claimed = this.emptyHandler({
            selection: Object.freeze({ anchor: selection.anchor, head: selection.head }),
            signal: controller2.signal,
            event
          });
          if (claimed) {
            event.preventDefault();
            return true;
          }
        } catch (error) {
          this.onError(error);
        }
        controller2.abort();
        this.pending.delete(controller2);
        return false;
      }
      if (!this.handler) return false;
      event.preventDefault();
      this.abort();
      const controller = new AbortController();
      this.pending.add(controller);
      try {
        const result = this.handler(Object.freeze(files), {
          selection: Object.freeze({ anchor: selection.anchor, head: selection.head }),
          signal: controller.signal,
          event
        });
        void Promise.resolve(result).catch((error) => {
          if (!controller.signal.aborted) this.onError(error);
        });
      } catch (error) {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) this.onError(error);
      }
      return true;
    }
    abort() {
      for (const controller of this.pending) controller.abort();
      this.pending.clear();
    }
  };
  function imageAltText(source) {
    return source.replace(/\\(.)/g, (match, character) => {
      const code = character.charCodeAt(0);
      return code >= 33 && code <= 47 || code >= 58 && code <= 64 || code >= 91 && code <= 96 || code >= 123 && code <= 126 ? character : match;
    });
  }
  var ImagePresentation = class {
    constructor(content, resolver, onLayout) {
      this.content = content;
      this.resolver = resolver;
      this.onLayout = onLayout;
      if (!resolver) return;
      this.observer = new MutationObserver(() => this.sync());
      this.observer.observe(content, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      this.sync();
    }
    entries = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    observer;
    stopped = false;
    sync() {
      if (this.stopped || !this.resolver) return;
      for (const [element, entry] of this.entries)
        if (!this.content.contains(element)) {
          entry.controller.abort();
          entry.resource?.dispose();
          this.entries.delete(element);
        }
      const elements = this.content.querySelectorAll("[data-image-url]");
      for (const element of Array.from(elements)) {
        if (this.entries.has(element) || this.entries.size >= 32) continue;
        const url = element.dataset.imageUrl;
        if (!managedImageURL(url)) continue;
        const entry = { controller: new AbortController() };
        this.entries.set(element, entry);
        void this.resolve(element, url, entry);
      }
    }
    async resolve(element, url, entry) {
      try {
        const resource = await this.resolver(url, { signal: entry.controller.signal });
        if (!resource) return;
        if (entry.controller.signal.aborted || this.stopped || !this.content.contains(element)) {
          resource.dispose();
          return;
        }
        if (!/^blob:/.test(resource.url) || !/^image\/(?:png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(resource.mimeType) || !Number.isFinite(resource.width) || !Number.isFinite(resource.height) || resource.width <= 0 || resource.height <= 0 || resource.width > 16384 || resource.height > 16384 || resource.width * resource.height > 4e7) {
          resource.dispose();
          return;
        }
        entry.resource = resource;
        const image2 = element.ownerDocument.createElement("img");
        image2.className = "milkup-image-preview";
        image2.alt = imageAltText(element.dataset.imageAlt ?? "");
        image2.draggable = false;
        image2.decoding = "async";
        image2.style.display = "block";
        image2.style.maxWidth = "100%";
        image2.style.maxHeight = "480px";
        image2.style.width = "auto";
        image2.style.height = "auto";
        image2.style.objectFit = "contain";
        image2.width = resource.width;
        image2.height = resource.height;
        image2.addEventListener("load", () => {
          if (!entry.controller.signal.aborted) this.onLayout();
        });
        image2.addEventListener(
          "error",
          () => {
            image2.remove();
            if (!entry.controller.signal.aborted) this.onLayout();
          },
          { once: true }
        );
        entry.image = image2;
        image2.src = resource.url;
        element.prepend(image2);
        this.onLayout();
      } catch {
      }
    }
    clear() {
      for (const entry of this.entries.values()) {
        entry.controller.abort();
        entry.image?.remove();
        entry.resource?.dispose();
      }
      this.entries.clear();
    }
    refresh() {
      if (this.stopped) return;
      this.clear();
      this.sync();
      this.onLayout();
    }
    destroy() {
      this.stopped = true;
      this.observer?.disconnect();
      this.clear();
    }
  };

  // packages/markdown/src/cst/node.ts
  function createNode(node3) {
    assertNodeRange(node3);
    return Object.freeze({
      type: node3.type,
      from: node3.from,
      to: node3.to,
      status: node3.status,
      ...node3.markerRanges ? { markerRanges: freezeRanges(node3.markerRanges) } : {},
      ...node3.contentRanges ? { contentRanges: freezeRanges(node3.contentRanges) } : {},
      ...node3.children ? { children: Object.freeze([...node3.children]) } : {},
      ...node3.data ? { data: Object.freeze({ ...node3.data }) } : {}
    });
  }
  function assertNodeRange(node3) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(node3.from) || !Number.isInteger(node3.to)) {
      throw new RangeError(`Node ${node3.type} range offsets must be integers`);
    }
    if (node3.from < 0 || node3.to < node3.from) {
      throw new RangeError(`Invalid ${node3.type} node range: ${node3.from}-${node3.to}`);
    }
  }
  function freezeRanges(ranges) {
    return Object.freeze(
      ranges.map((range2) => {
        if (!Number.isInteger(range2.from) || !Number.isInteger(range2.to)) {
          throw new RangeError(`Range offsets must be integers: ${range2.from}-${range2.to}`);
        }
        if (range2.from < 0 || range2.to < range2.from) {
          throw new RangeError(`Invalid range: ${range2.from}-${range2.to}`);
        }
        return Object.freeze({ ...range2 });
      })
    );
  }

  // packages/markdown/src/extensions/safe.ts
  function compileMarkdownSyntaxPattern(extension) {
    if (extension.pattern.length > 256) {
      throw new Error(`Markdown syntax pattern is too long: ${extension.id}`);
    }
    if (/\(\?[<!=]|\\[1-9]|\([^)]*(?:\*|\+|\{\d+,?\d*\})[^)]*\)(?:\*|\+|\{)/.test(extension.pattern)) {
      throw new Error(`Markdown syntax pattern uses unsafe features: ${extension.id}`);
    }
    const flags = extension.flags ?? "";
    if (!/^[imu]*$/.test(flags)) {
      throw new Error(`Markdown syntax flags are invalid: ${extension.id}`);
    }
    return new RegExp(extension.pattern, flags);
  }
  function runMarkdownExtensionSafely(context, run) {
    try {
      return Object.freeze({
        ok: true,
        value: run()
      });
    } catch (error) {
      return Object.freeze({
        ok: false,
        error,
        context: Object.freeze({ ...context })
      });
    }
  }

  // packages/markdown/src/block/lines.ts
  function scanLines(source) {
    const lines = [];
    let pos = 0;
    let number2 = 1;
    while (pos < source.length) {
      const newline = source.indexOf("\n", pos);
      const hasNewline = newline !== -1;
      const lineBreakStart = hasNewline ? newline : source.length;
      const to = hasNewline ? newline + 1 : source.length;
      const contentTo = lineBreakStart > pos && source.charCodeAt(lineBreakStart - 1) === 13 ? lineBreakStart - 1 : lineBreakStart;
      lines.push(
        Object.freeze({
          number: number2,
          from: pos,
          contentTo,
          to,
          text: source.slice(pos, contentTo)
        })
      );
      pos = to;
      number2 += 1;
    }
    return Object.freeze(lines);
  }

  // packages/markdown/src/block/parser.ts
  function parseMarkdown(source, options = {}) {
    try {
      return {
        source,
        root: createNode({
          type: "document",
          from: 0,
          to: source.length,
          status: "valid",
          children: parseBlocks(source, options.syntaxExtensions ?? [])
        })
      };
    } catch {
      return {
        source,
        root: createNode({
          type: "document",
          from: 0,
          to: source.length,
          status: "fallback",
          children: source.length > 0 ? [
            createNode({
              type: "fallbackText",
              from: 0,
              to: source.length,
              status: "fallback",
              contentRanges: [{ from: 0, to: source.length }]
            })
          ] : []
        })
      };
    }
  }
  function parseBlocks(source, extensions) {
    const lines = scanLines(source);
    const nodes = [];
    let index = 0;
    while (index < lines.length) {
      const line = lines[index];
      if (!line) {
        break;
      }
      if (isBlankLine(line)) {
        nodes.push(parseBlankLine(line));
        index += 1;
        continue;
      }
      const extensionBlock = parseExtensionBlock(line, extensions);
      if (extensionBlock) {
        nodes.push(extensionBlock);
        index += 1;
        continue;
      }
      const htmlBlock = parseHtmlBlock(lines, index);
      if (htmlBlock) {
        nodes.push(htmlBlock.node);
        index = htmlBlock.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const fencedCode = parseFencedCode(lines, index);
      if (fencedCode) {
        nodes.push(fencedCode.node);
        index = fencedCode.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const indentedCode = parseIndentedCode(lines, index);
      if (indentedCode) {
        nodes.push(indentedCode.node);
        index = indentedCode.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const blockquote = parseBlockquote(lines, index);
      if (blockquote) {
        nodes.push(blockquote.node);
        index = blockquote.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const thematicBreak = parseThematicBreak(line);
      if (thematicBreak) {
        nodes.push(thematicBreak);
        index += 1;
        continue;
      }
      const heading = parseAtxHeading(line);
      if (heading) {
        nodes.push(heading);
        index += 1;
        continue;
      }
      const table = parseTable(lines, index);
      if (table) {
        nodes.push(table.node);
        index = table.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const list2 = parseList(lines, index);
      if (list2) {
        nodes.push(list2.node);
        index = list2.nextIndex;
        continue;
      }
      const previous = nodes.at(-1);
      const allowSetext = !previous || !["unorderedList", "orderedList", "blockquote"].includes(previous.type);
      const paragraph = collectParagraph(lines, index, extensions, allowSetext);
      const setext = allowSetext ? parseSetextHeading(paragraph, lines[index + paragraph.length]) : void 0;
      nodes.push(setext ?? parseParagraph(source, paragraph, extensions));
      index += paragraph.length + (setext ? 1 : 0);
    }
    return Object.freeze(nodes);
  }
  function parseExtensionBlock(line, extensions) {
    for (const extension of extensions) {
      if (!extension.block) {
        continue;
      }
      const result = runMarkdownExtensionSafely(
        { extensionName: extension.id, hook: "block" },
        () => compileMarkdownSyntaxPattern(extension).exec(line.text)
      );
      const match = result.ok ? result.value : void 0;
      if (!match) {
        continue;
      }
      const from = line.from + match.index;
      const to = from + match[0].length;
      return createNode({
        type: extension.nodeType,
        from,
        to,
        status: "valid",
        contentRanges: [{ from, to }],
        data: { extensionId: extension.id }
      });
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function isBlankLine(line) {
    return line.text.trim().length === 0;
  }
  function parseBlankLine(line) {
    return createNode({
      type: "blankLine",
      from: line.from,
      to: line.to,
      status: "valid",
      contentRanges: []
    });
  }
  function parseAtxHeading(line) {
    const match = /^( {0,3})(#{1,6})(?:[ \t]+|$)(.*)$/.exec(line.text);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indent = match[1]?.length ?? 0;
    const marker = match[2];
    if (!marker) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const markerFrom = line.from + indent;
    const markerTo = markerFrom + marker.length;
    const contentFrom = findHeadingContentStart(line.text, markerTo - line.from);
    const content = line.text.slice(contentFrom);
    const closing = /(?:^|[ \t]+)(#+)[ \t]*$/.exec(content);
    const contentEnd = closing ? contentFrom + closing.index : line.text.length;
    const trimmedContentEnd = line.text.slice(0, contentEnd).replace(/[ \t]+$/, "").length;
    const contentRange = {
      from: line.from + contentFrom,
      to: line.from + Math.max(contentFrom, trimmedContentEnd)
    };
    const closingRange = closing ? {
      from: line.from + contentFrom + closing.index + closing[0].indexOf("#"),
      to: line.from + contentFrom + closing.index + closing[0].indexOf("#") + closing[1].length
    } : void 0;
    return createNode({
      type: "heading",
      from: line.from,
      to: line.to,
      status: "valid",
      markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom, to: markerTo }, ...closingRange ? [closingRange] : []],
      contentRanges: contentRange.from <= contentRange.to ? [contentRange] : [],
      data: { level: marker.length }
    });
  }
  function parseSetextHeading(lines, underline) {
    const first = lines[0];
    const last = lines.at(-1);
    const match = underline && /^ {0,3}(=+|-+)[ \t]*$/.exec(underline.text);
    if (!first || !last || !underline || !match) return void 0;
    const contentFrom = first.from + (first.text.match(/^[ \t]*/)?.[0].length ?? 0);
    const contentTo = last.from + last.text.replace(/[ \t]+$/, "").length;
    const markerFrom = underline.from + underline.text.indexOf(match[1]);
    return createNode({
      type: "heading",
      from: first.from,
      to: underline.to,
      status: "valid",
      markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom, to: markerFrom + match[1].length }],
      contentRanges: [{ from: contentFrom, to: contentTo }],
      data: { level: match[1][0] === "=" ? 1 : 2, style: "setext" }
    });
  }
  function htmlBlockEnd(text2, inParagraph) {
    const special = /^ {0,3}<(script|pre|style|textarea)(?:[ \t>]|$)/i.exec(text2);
    if (special) return new RegExp(`</${special[1]}>`, "i");
    if (/^ {0,3}<!--/.test(text2)) return /-->/;
    if (/^ {0,3}<\?/.test(text2)) return /\?>/;
    if (/^ {0,3}<!\[CDATA\[/.test(text2)) return /\]\]>/;
    if (/^ {0,3}<![A-Z]/.test(text2)) return />/;
    if (/^ {0,3}<\/?(?:address|article|aside|base|basefont|blockquote|body|caption|center|col|colgroup|dd|details|dialog|dir|div|dl|dt|fieldset|figcaption|figure|footer|form|frame|frameset|h[1-6]|head|header|hr|html|iframe|legend|li|link|main|menu|menuitem|nav|noframes|ol|optgroup|option|p|param|search|section|summary|table|tbody|td|tfoot|th|thead|title|tr|track|ul)(?:[ \t/>]|$)/i.test(
      text2
    ))
      return "blank";
    if (!inParagraph && /^ {0,3}(?:<\/[a-zA-Z][\w-]*[ \t]*>|<[a-zA-Z][\w-]*(?:[ \t]+[a-zA-Z_:][\w.:-]*(?:[ \t]*=[ \t]*(?:[^ \t"'=<>`]+|'[^']*'|"[^"]*"))?)*[ \t]*\/?>)[ \t]*$/.test(
      text2
    ))
      return "blank";
    return void 0;
  }
  function parseHtmlBlock(lines, index) {
    const first = lines[index];
    if (!first) return void 0;
    const end = htmlBlockEnd(first.text, false);
    if (!end) return void 0;
    let nextIndex = index;
    while (nextIndex < lines.length) {
      const line = lines[nextIndex];
      if (end === "blank" && isBlankLine(line)) break;
      nextIndex += 1;
      if (end !== "blank" && end.test(line.text)) break;
    }
    const last = lines[nextIndex - 1] ?? first;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "fallbackText",
        from: first.from,
        to: last.to,
        status: "valid",
        contentRanges: [{ from: first.from, to: last.to }],
        data: { block: "html" }
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function parseFencedCode(lines, startIndex) {
    const opener = lines[startIndex];
    if (!opener) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const opening = /^( {0,3})(`{3,}|~{3,})(.*)$/.exec(opener.text);
    if (!opening) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indent = opening[1]?.length ?? 0;
    const fence = opening[2];
    const info = opening[3]?.trim() ?? "";
    if (!fence) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const fenceChar = fence[0];
    if (!fenceChar || fenceChar === "`" && info.includes("`")) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const fenceLength = fence.length;
    const openingMarkerFrom = opener.from + indent;
    const openingMarkerTo = openingMarkerFrom + fenceLength;
    let closingLine;
    let closingMarkerFrom;
    let closingMarkerTo;
    let nextIndex = lines.length;
    for (let index = startIndex + 1; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
      const line = lines[index];
      if (!line) {
        break;
      }
      const closing = matchClosingFence(line, fenceChar, fenceLength);
      if (closing) {
        closingLine = line;
        closingMarkerFrom = line.from + closing.indent;
        closingMarkerTo = closingMarkerFrom + closing.length;
        nextIndex = index + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    const lastLine = closingLine ?? lines[lines.length - 1] ?? opener;
    const contentFrom = opener.to;
    const contentTo = closingLine ? closingLine.from : lastLine.to;
    const markerRanges = closingMarkerFrom !== void 0 && closingMarkerTo !== void 0 ? [
      { from: openingMarkerFrom, to: openingMarkerTo },
      { from: closingMarkerFrom, to: closingMarkerTo }
    ] : [{ from: openingMarkerFrom, to: openingMarkerTo }];
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "fencedCode",
        from: opener.from,
        to: lastLine.to,
        status: closingLine ? "valid" : "incomplete",
        markerRanges,
        contentRanges: [{ from: contentFrom, to: contentTo }],
        data: { fenceChar, fenceLength, info }
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function matchClosingFence(line, fenceChar, fenceLength) {
    const escaped = fenceChar === "`" ? "`" : "~";
    const pattern = new RegExp(`^( {0,3})(${escaped}{${fenceLength},})[ \\t]*$`);
    const match = pattern.exec(line.text);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return {
      indent: match[1]?.length ?? 0,
      length: match[2]?.length ?? fenceLength
    };
  }
  function parseIndentedCode(lines, startIndex) {
    const first = lines[startIndex];
    if (!first || !isIndentedCodeLine(first)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const codeLines = [];
    let nextIndex = startIndex;
    while (nextIndex < lines.length) {
      const line = lines[nextIndex];
      if (!line || !isIndentedCodeLine(line)) {
        break;
      }
      codeLines.push(line);
      nextIndex += 1;
    }
    const last = codeLines[codeLines.length - 1] ?? first;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "indentedCode",
        from: first.from,
        to: last.to,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: codeLines.map((line) => ({
          from: line.from,
          to: line.from + indentedCodePrefixLength(line.text)
        })),
        contentRanges: codeLines.map((line) => ({
          from: line.from + indentedCodePrefixLength(line.text),
          to: line.contentTo
        }))
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function isIndentedCodeLine(line) {
    return /^(?: {4}| {0,3}\t)/.test(line.text);
  }
  function indentedCodePrefixLength(text2) {
    return /^(?: {4}| {0,3}\t)/.exec(text2)?.[0].length ?? 0;
  }
  function parseBlockquote(lines, startIndex) {
    const first = parseBlockquoteLine(lines[startIndex]);
    if (!first) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const children = [first];
    let nextIndex = startIndex + 1;
    while (nextIndex < lines.length) {
      const child = parseBlockquoteLine(lines[nextIndex]);
      if (!child) {
        break;
      }
      children.push(child);
      nextIndex += 1;
    }
    const last = children[children.length - 1] ?? first;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "blockquote",
        from: first.from,
        to: last.to,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: children.flatMap((child) => child.markerRanges ?? []),
        contentRanges: children.flatMap((child) => child.contentRanges ?? []),
        children
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function parseBlockquoteLine(line) {
    if (!line) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const match = /^( {0,3})>(?:[ \t]?)(.*)$/.exec(line.text);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indent = match[1]?.length ?? 0;
    const markerFrom = line.from + indent;
    const markerTo = markerFrom + 1;
    const contentFrom = findBlockquoteContentStart(line.text, markerTo - line.from);
    return createNode({
      type: "blockquoteLine",
      from: line.from,
      to: line.to,
      status: "valid",
      markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom, to: markerTo }],
      contentRanges: [{ from: line.from + contentFrom, to: line.contentTo }]
    });
  }
  function findBlockquoteContentStart(text2, markerEnd) {
    let pos = markerEnd;
    if (text2[pos] === " " || text2[pos] === "	") {
      pos += 1;
    }
    return pos;
  }
  function parseThematicBreak(line) {
    const match = /^( {0,3})((?:\*[ \t]*){3,}|(?:-[ \t]*){3,}|(?:_[ \t]*){3,})$/.exec(line.text);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indent = match[1]?.length ?? 0;
    return createNode({
      type: "thematicBreak",
      from: line.from,
      to: line.to,
      status: "valid",
      markerRanges: [{ from: line.from + indent, to: line.contentTo }]
    });
  }
  function parseList(lines, startIndex) {
    const first = parseListItem(lines[startIndex]);
    if (!first) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const items = [first.node];
    let nextIndex = startIndex + 1;
    while (nextIndex < lines.length) {
      const item = parseListItem(lines[nextIndex]);
      if (!item || item.kind !== first.kind) {
        break;
      }
      items.push(item.node);
      nextIndex += 1;
    }
    const last = items[items.length - 1] ?? first.node;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: first.kind === "ordered" ? "orderedList" : "unorderedList",
        from: first.node.from,
        to: last.to,
        status: "valid",
        children: items,
        data: first.kind === "ordered" ? { start: first.start ?? 1 } : {}
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function parseListItem(line) {
    if (!line || isBlankLine(line) || parseThematicBreak(line)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const unordered = /^( {0,3})([-+*])(?:[ \t]+|$)(.*)$/.exec(line.text);
    if (unordered) {
      const indent2 = unordered[1]?.length ?? 0;
      const marker = unordered[2];
      if (!marker) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const markerFrom2 = line.from + indent2;
      const markerTo2 = markerFrom2 + marker.length;
      const contentFrom2 = findListContentStart(line.text, markerTo2 - line.from);
      return {
        kind: "unordered",
        node: createNode({
          type: "listItem",
          from: line.from,
          to: line.to,
          status: "valid",
          markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom2, to: markerTo2 }],
          contentRanges: [{ from: line.from + contentFrom2, to: line.contentTo }],
          data: { marker }
        })
      };
    }
    const ordered = /^( {0,3})(\d{1,9})([.)])(?:[ \t]+|$)(.*)$/.exec(line.text);
    if (!ordered) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indent = ordered[1]?.length ?? 0;
    const numberText = ordered[2];
    const delimiter = ordered[3];
    if (!numberText || !delimiter) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const markerFrom = line.from + indent;
    const markerTo = markerFrom + numberText.length + delimiter.length;
    const contentFrom = findListContentStart(line.text, markerTo - line.from);
    return {
      kind: "ordered",
      start: Number.parseInt(numberText, 10),
      node: createNode({
        type: "listItem",
        from: line.from,
        to: line.to,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom, to: markerTo }],
        contentRanges: [{ from: line.from + contentFrom, to: line.contentTo }],
        data: { number: Number.parseInt(numberText, 10), delimiter }
      })
    };
  }
  function findListContentStart(text2, markerEnd) {
    let pos = markerEnd;
    while (pos < text2.length && (text2[pos] === " " || text2[pos] === "	")) {
      pos += 1;
    }
    return pos;
  }
  function findHeadingContentStart(text2, markerEnd) {
    let pos = markerEnd;
    while (pos < text2.length && (text2[pos] === " " || text2[pos] === "	")) {
      pos += 1;
    }
    return pos;
  }
  function parseTable(lines, startIndex) {
    const header = parseTableRow(lines[startIndex]);
    const delimiterLine = lines[startIndex + 1];
    const delimiter = parseTableDelimiterRow(delimiterLine);
    if (!header || !delimiter || header.cells.length < 2 || header.cells.length !== delimiter.alignments.length) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const rows = [header.node];
    let nextIndex = startIndex + 2;
    while (nextIndex < lines.length) {
      const row = parseTableRow(lines[nextIndex]);
      if (!row || row.cells.length === 0) {
        break;
      }
      rows.push(row.node);
      nextIndex += 1;
    }
    const last = rows[rows.length - 1] ?? header.node;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "table",
        from: header.node.from,
        to: Math.max(last.to, delimiterLine.to),
        status: "valid",
        children: rows,
        data: {
          alignments: delimiter.alignments
        }
      }),
      nextIndex
    };
  }
  function parseTableRow(line) {
    if (!line || !line.text.includes("|")) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const cells = splitTableCells(line);
    if (cells.length === 0) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const children = cells.map(
      (cell) => createNode({
        type: "tableCell",
        from: cell.from,
        to: cell.to,
        status: "valid",
        contentRanges: [{ from: cell.from, to: cell.to }]
      })
    );
    return {
      cells: children,
      line,
      node: createNode({
        type: "tableRow",
        from: line.from,
        to: line.to,
        status: "valid",
        children,
        contentRanges: children.flatMap((cell) => cell.contentRanges ?? [])
      })
    };
  }
  function parseTableDelimiterRow(line) {
    const row = parseTableRow(line);
    if (!row) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const parsedAlignments = row.cells.map((cell) => parseTableDelimiterCell(row.line, cell));
    if (parsedAlignments.some((alignment) => alignment === false)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return {
      alignments: parsedAlignments.map(
        (alignment) => alignment === true || alignment === false ? void 0 : alignment
      )
    };
  }
  function parseTableDelimiterCell(line, cell) {
    const text2 = line.text.slice(cell.from - line.from, cell.to - line.from).trim();
    if (!/^:?-{3,}:?$/.test(text2)) {
      return false;
    }
    const left = text2.startsWith(":");
    const right = text2.endsWith(":");
    if (left && right) {
      return "center";
    }
    if (right) {
      return "right";
    }
    if (left) {
      return "left";
    }
    return true;
  }
  function splitTableCells(line) {
    const ranges = [];
    const text2 = line.text;
    const startsWithPipe = text2.trimStart().startsWith("|");
    const lastPipe = text2.trimEnd().length - 1;
    const endsWithPipe = text2[lastPipe] === "|" && !isEscapedPipe(text2, lastPipe);
    const start = startsWithPipe ? text2.indexOf("|") + 1 : 0;
    const end = endsWithPipe ? text2.lastIndexOf("|") : text2.length;
    let cellStart = start;
    let pos = start;
    while (pos <= end) {
      const atCellEnd = pos === end;
      const char = text2[pos];
      const escaped = isEscapedPipe(text2, pos);
      if (atCellEnd || char === "|" && !escaped) {
        ranges.push(trimTableCellRange(line.from + cellStart, line.from + pos, text2, line.from));
        cellStart = pos + 1;
      }
      pos += 1;
    }
    return Object.freeze(ranges);
  }
  function isEscapedPipe(text2, position) {
    let slashes = 0;
    for (let i = position - 1; i >= 0 && text2[i] === "\\"; i--) slashes++;
    return slashes % 2 === 1;
  }
  function trimTableCellRange(from, to, lineText, lineFrom) {
    let start = from;
    let end = to;
    while (start < end && /\s/.test(lineText[start - lineFrom] ?? "")) {
      start += 1;
    }
    while (end > start && /\s/.test(lineText[end - lineFrom - 1] ?? "")) {
      end -= 1;
    }
    return Object.freeze({ from: start, to: end });
  }
  function collectParagraph(lines, startIndex, extensions, allowSetext) {
    const paragraph = [];
    for (let index = startIndex; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
      const line = lines[index];
      if (!line || isBlankLine(line) || allowSetext && paragraph.length > 0 && /^ {0,3}(=+|-+)[ \t]*$/.test(line.text) || htmlBlockEnd(line.text, true) || parseFencedCode(lines, index) || parseBlockquote(lines, index) || parseThematicBreak(line) || parseAtxHeading(line) || parseTable(lines, index) || /^ {0,3}(?:[-+*]|1[.)])[ \t]+\S/.test(line.text) || parseExtensionBlock(line, extensions)) {
        break;
      }
      paragraph.push(line);
    }
    return paragraph;
  }
  function parseParagraph(source, lines, extensions) {
    const first = lines[0];
    const last = lines[lines.length - 1];
    if (!first || !last) {
      return createNode({
        type: "paragraph",
        from: 0,
        to: 0,
        status: "fallback",
        contentRanges: []
      });
    }
    return createNode({
      type: "paragraph",
      from: first.from,
      to: last.to,
      status: "valid",
      contentRanges: [{ from: first.from, to: last.contentTo }],
      children: parseInlineExtensions(source, first.from, last.contentTo, extensions)
    });
  }
  function parseInlineExtensions(source, from, to, extensions) {
    const nodes = [];
    const text2 = source.slice(from, to);
    for (const extension of extensions) {
      if (!extension.inline) {
        continue;
      }
      const result = runMarkdownExtensionSafely(
        { extensionName: extension.id, hook: "inline" },
        () => {
          const base = compileMarkdownSyntaxPattern(extension);
          const pattern = new RegExp(base.source, `${base.flags}g`);
          return [...text2.matchAll(pattern)];
        }
      );
      if (!result.ok) {
        continue;
      }
      for (const match of result.value) {
        const matchFrom = from + (match.index ?? 0);
        const matchTo = matchFrom + match[0].length;
        if (matchTo <= matchFrom) {
          continue;
        }
        nodes.push(
          createNode({
            type: extension.nodeType,
            from: matchFrom,
            to: matchTo,
            status: "valid",
            contentRanges: [{ from: matchFrom, to: matchTo }],
            data: { extensionId: extension.id }
          })
        );
      }
    }
    return Object.freeze(nodes.sort((left, right) => left.from - right.from || left.to - right.to));
  }

  // packages/markdown/src/incremental/window.ts
  async function parseMarkdownWindow(store, options) {
    const window = await store.readLineWindow(options.fromLine, options.toLine);
    const parsed = parseMarkdown(window.text);
    return Object.freeze({
      documentId: store.documentId,
      version: store.version,
      window,
      localSource: parsed.source,
      source: parsed.source,
      root: shiftNode(parsed.root, window.from)
    });
  }
  function shiftNode(node3, offset) {
    return createNode({
      ...node3,
      from: node3.from + offset,
      to: node3.to + offset,
      ...node3.markerRanges ? { markerRanges: shiftRanges(node3.markerRanges, offset) } : {},
      ...node3.contentRanges ? { contentRanges: shiftRanges(node3.contentRanges, offset) } : {},
      ...node3.children ? { children: node3.children.map((child) => shiftNode(child, offset)) } : {}
    });
  }
  function shiftRanges(ranges, offset) {
    return ranges.map(
      (range2) => Object.freeze({
        from: range2.from + offset,
        to: range2.to + offset
      })
    );
  }

  // packages/markdown/src/inline/parser.ts
  function parseInline(source, from = 0, to = source.length) {
    if (to <= from) {
      return [];
    }
    const nodes = [];
    let pos = from;
    while (pos < to) {
      const parsed = parseHardBreak(source, pos, to) ?? parseEscape(source, pos, to) ?? parseCodeSpan(source, pos, to) ?? parseImage(source, pos, to) ?? parseLink(source, pos, to) ?? parseAutolink(source, pos, to) ?? parseStrikethrough(source, pos, to) ?? parseStrong(source, pos, to) ?? parseEmphasis(source, pos, to);
      if (parsed) {
        nodes.push(parsed.node);
        pos = parsed.next;
        continue;
      }
      const textEnd = findNextSpecial(source, pos + 1, to);
      nodes.push(createTextNode(source, pos, textEnd));
      pos = textEnd;
    }
    return Object.freeze(nodes);
  }
  function parseEscape(source, pos, to) {
    if (source[pos] !== "\\" || pos + 1 >= to) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "escape",
        from: pos,
        to: pos + 2,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + 1 }],
        contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 1, to: pos + 2 }],
        data: { value: source.slice(pos + 1, pos + 2) }
      }),
      next: pos + 2
    };
  }
  function parseHardBreak(source, pos, to) {
    const html = source[pos] === "<" ? /^<br[ \t]*\/?>/i.exec(source.slice(pos, to))?.[0] : void 0;
    if (html)
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "hardBreak",
          from: pos,
          to: pos + html.length,
          status: "valid",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + html.length }],
          data: { kind: "html" }
        }),
        next: pos + html.length
      };
    if (pos + 2 < to && source[pos] === " " && source[pos + 1] === " " && source[pos + 2] === "\n") {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "hardBreak",
          from: pos,
          to: pos + 3,
          status: "valid",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + 2 }],
          data: { kind: "spaces" }
        }),
        next: pos + 3
      };
    }
    if (pos + 1 < to && source[pos] === "\\" && source[pos + 1] === "\n") {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "hardBreak",
          from: pos,
          to: pos + 2,
          status: "valid",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + 1 }],
          data: { kind: "backslash" }
        }),
        next: pos + 2
      };
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function parseCodeSpan(source, pos, to) {
    if (source[pos] !== "`") {
      return void 0;
    }
    const markerTo = consumeRun(source, pos, to, "`");
    const markerLength = markerTo - pos;
    const closing = findClosingRun(source, markerTo, to, "`", markerLength);
    if (closing === -1) {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "inlineCode",
          from: pos,
          to,
          status: "incomplete",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: markerTo }],
          contentRanges: [{ from: markerTo, to }]
        }),
        next: to
      };
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "inlineCode",
        from: pos,
        to: closing + markerLength,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [
          { from: pos, to: markerTo },
          { from: closing, to: closing + markerLength }
        ],
        contentRanges: [{ from: markerTo, to: closing }],
        data: { value: source.slice(markerTo, closing) }
      }),
