      next: closing + markerLength
    };
  }
  function parseImage(source, pos, to) {
    if (source.slice(pos, pos + 2) !== "![") {
      return void 0;
    }
    return parseLinkLike(source, pos, to, true);
  }
  function parseLink(source, pos, to) {
    if (source[pos] !== "[") {
      return void 0;
    }
    return parseLinkLike(source, pos, to, false);
  }
  function parseLinkLike(source, pos, to, image2) {
    const labelOpen = image2 ? pos + 1 : pos;
    const labelStart = labelOpen + 1;
    let labelClose = -1;
    for (let index = labelStart; index < to; index++) {
      if (source[index] === "\\") index++;
      else if (source[index] === "]") {
        labelClose = index;
        break;
      }
    }
    const initialMarkerRanges = image2 ? [
      { from: pos, to: pos + 1 },
      { from: labelOpen, to: labelOpen + 1 }
    ] : [{ from: labelOpen, to: labelOpen + 1 }];
    if (labelClose === -1 || labelClose >= to) {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: image2 ? "image" : "link",
          from: pos,
          to,
          status: "incomplete",
          markerRanges: initialMarkerRanges,
          contentRanges: [{ from: labelStart, to }]
        }),
        next: to
      };
    }
    const destinationOpen = labelClose + 1;
    if (source[destinationOpen] !== "(" || destinationOpen >= to) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const destinationStart = destinationOpen + 1;
    const destinationClose = source.indexOf(")", destinationStart);
    const markerRanges = [
      ...initialMarkerRanges,
      { from: labelClose, to: labelClose + 1 },
      { from: destinationOpen, to: destinationOpen + 1 }
    ];
    if (destinationClose === -1 || destinationClose >= to) {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: image2 ? "image" : "link",
          from: pos,
          to,
          status: "incomplete",
          markerRanges,
          contentRanges: [
            { from: labelStart, to: labelClose },
            { from: destinationStart, to }
          ],
          children: image2 ? [] : parseInline(source, labelStart, labelClose)
        }),
        next: to
      };
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: image2 ? "image" : "link",
        from: pos,
        to: destinationClose + 1,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [...markerRanges, { from: destinationClose, to: destinationClose + 1 }],
        contentRanges: [
          { from: labelStart, to: labelClose },
          { from: destinationStart, to: destinationClose }
        ],
        children: image2 ? [] : parseInline(source, labelStart, labelClose),
        data: {
          label: source.slice(labelStart, labelClose),
          destination: source.slice(destinationStart, destinationClose)
        }
      }),
      next: destinationClose + 1
    };
  }
  function parseAutolink(source, pos, to) {
    if (source[pos] !== "<") {
      return void 0;
    }
    const close = source.indexOf(">", pos + 1);
    if (close === -1 || close >= to) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const value = source.slice(pos + 1, close);
    const kind = classifyAutolink(value);
    if (!kind) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "autolink",
        from: pos,
        to: close + 1,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [
          { from: pos, to: pos + 1 },
          { from: close, to: close + 1 }
        ],
        contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 1, to: close }],
        data: { kind, value }
      }),
      next: close + 1
    };
  }
  function classifyAutolink(value) {
    if (/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]{1,31}:[^\s<>]*$/.test(value)) {
      return "url";
    }
    if (/^[^\s@<>]+@[^\s@<>]+\.[^\s@<>]+$/.test(value)) {
      return "email";
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function parseStrikethrough(source, pos, to) {
    if (source.slice(pos, pos + 2) !== "~~" || source[pos - 1] === "~" || source[pos + 2] === "~" || pos + 2 >= to || /\s/u.test(source[pos + 2]))
      return void 0;
    let closing = -1;
    for (let index = pos + 2; index < to; ) {
      if (source[index] === "\\") {
        index += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (source[index] === "`") {
        const end = consumeRun(source, index, to, "`");
        const codeEnd = findClosingRun(source, end, to, "`", end - index);
        if (codeEnd < 0) break;
        index = codeEnd + end - index;
        continue;
      }
      if (source[index] === "~") {
        const end = consumeRun(source, index, to, "~");
        if (end - index === 2 && !/\s/u.test(source[index - 1])) {
          closing = index;
          break;
        }
        index = end;
        continue;
      }
      index++;
    }
    const contentTo = closing < 0 ? to : closing;
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "strikethrough",
        from: pos,
        to: closing < 0 ? to : closing + 2,
        status: closing < 0 ? "incomplete" : "valid",
        markerRanges: closing < 0 ? [{ from: pos, to: pos + 2 }] : [
          { from: pos, to: pos + 2 },
          { from: closing, to: closing + 2 }
        ],
        contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 2, to: contentTo }],
        ...closing >= 0 ? { children: parseInline(source, pos + 2, closing) } : {}
      }),
      next: closing < 0 ? to : closing + 2
    };
  }
  function parseStrong(source, pos, to) {
    if (pos + 2 > to || source.slice(pos, pos + 2) !== "**") {
      return void 0;
    }
    const closing = findEmphasisClosing(source, pos + 2, to, 2);
    if (closing === -1 || closing >= to) {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "strong",
          from: pos,
          to,
          status: "incomplete",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + 2 }],
          contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 2, to }]
        }),
        next: to
      };
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "strong",
        from: pos,
        to: closing + 2,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [
          { from: pos, to: pos + 2 },
          { from: closing, to: closing + 2 }
        ],
        contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 2, to: closing }],
        children: parseInline(source, pos + 2, closing)
      }),
      next: closing + 2
    };
  }
  function parseEmphasis(source, pos, to) {
    if (source[pos] !== "*" || pos + 1 < to && source[pos + 1] === "*") {
      return void 0;
    }
    const closing = findEmphasisClosing(source, pos + 1, to, 1);
    if (closing === -1 || closing >= to) {
      return {
        node: createNode({
          type: "emphasis",
          from: pos,
          to,
          status: "incomplete",
          markerRanges: [{ from: pos, to: pos + 1 }],
          contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 1, to }]
        }),
        next: to
      };
    }
    return {
      node: createNode({
        type: "emphasis",
        from: pos,
        to: closing + 1,
        status: "valid",
        markerRanges: [
          { from: pos, to: pos + 1 },
          { from: closing, to: closing + 1 }
        ],
        contentRanges: [{ from: pos + 1, to: closing }],
        children: parseInline(source, pos + 1, closing)
      }),
      next: closing + 1
    };
  }
  function findEmphasisClosing(source, from, to, markerLength) {
    let nestedEmphasis = false;
    for (let pos = from; pos < to; ) {
      if (source[pos] === "\\") {
        pos += 2;
        continue;
      }
      if (source[pos] === "`") {
        const markerTo = consumeRun(source, pos, to, "`");
        const closing = findClosingRun(source, markerTo, to, "`", markerTo - pos);
        if (closing === -1) {
          for (let candidate = markerTo; candidate + markerLength <= to; candidate += 1) {
            if (source[candidate] === "*" && (markerLength === 1 || source[candidate + 1] === "*")) {
              return candidate;
            }
          }
          return -1;
        }
        pos = closing + (markerTo - pos);
        continue;
      }
      if (source[pos] !== "*") {
        pos += 1;
        continue;
      }
      if (markerLength === 1) return pos;
      if (pos + 1 < to && source[pos + 1] === "*") {
        const closesNested = nestedEmphasis && pos + 2 < to && source[pos + 2] === "*";
        return pos + (closesNested ? 1 : 0);
      }
      nestedEmphasis = !nestedEmphasis;
      pos += 1;
    }
    return -1;
  }
  function createTextNode(source, from, to) {
    return createNode({
      type: "text",
      from,
      to,
      status: "valid",
      contentRanges: [{ from, to }],
      data: { value: source.slice(from, to) }
    });
  }
  function findNextSpecial(source, from, to) {
    for (let pos = from; pos < to; pos += 1) {
      const char = source[pos];
      if (char === "\\" || char === "`" || char === "*" || char === "~" && source[pos + 1] === "~" || char === "[" || char === "!" || char === "<" || char === " " && source[pos + 1] === " " && source[pos + 2] === "\n") {
        return pos;
      }
    }
    return to;
  }
  function consumeRun(source, from, to, char) {
    let pos = from;
    while (pos < to && source[pos] === char) {
      pos += 1;
    }
    return pos;
  }
  function findClosingRun(source, from, to, char, length2) {
    for (let pos = from; pos < to; pos += 1) {
      if (source[pos] !== char) {
        continue;
      }
      const end = consumeRun(source, pos, to, char);
      if (end - pos === length2) {
        return pos;
      }
      pos = end - 1;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/grapheme-navigation.ts
  var segmenter = new Intl.Segmenter(void 0, { granularity: "grapheme" });
  function previousGraphemePosition(doc, position) {
    const line = doc.lineAt(position);
    if (position === line.from) return line.number > 1 ? doc.line(line.number - 1).to : 0;
    const segment = segmenter.segment(line.text).containing(position - line.from - 1);
    return line.from + (segment?.index ?? line.text.length);
  }
  function nextGraphemePosition(doc, position) {
    const line = doc.lineAt(position);
    const segment = segmenter.segment(line.text).containing(position - line.from);
    if (segment) return line.from + segment.index + segment.segment.length;
    return line.number < doc.lineCount ? doc.line(line.number + 1).from : doc.length;
  }
  function snapGraphemePosition(doc, position, direction = "backward") {
    const line = doc.lineAt(position);
    const offset = position - line.from;
    const segment = segmenter.segment(line.text).containing(offset);
    if (!segment || segment.index === offset) return position;
    return line.from + segment.index + (direction === "forward" ? segment.segment.length : 0);
  }
  function previousGraphemeOffset(text2, offset) {
    return segmenter.segment(text2).containing(offset - 1)?.index ?? 0;
  }
  function nextGraphemeOffset(text2, offset) {
    const segment = segmenter.segment(text2).containing(offset);
    return segment ? segment.index + segment.segment.length : text2.length;
  }
  function snapGraphemeOffset(text2, offset) {
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(offset, text2.length));
    return segmenter.segment(text2).containing(clamped)?.index ?? clamped;
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/table-editing.ts
  function isTableCommand(value) {
    return typeof value === "string" && [
      "next",
      "previous",
      "down",
      "exit",
      "add-row",
      "delete-row",
      "add-column",
      "delete-column",
      "select-cell",
      "select-table",
      "clear-cell",
      "set-cell",
      "align-left",
      "align-center",
      "align-right",
      "align-default"
    ].includes(value);
  }
  function tableContext(state, root) {
    const range2 = state.selection.main;
    const table = root.children?.find(
      (node3) => node3.type === "table" && range2.from >= node3.from && range2.to <= node3.to && (range2.from < node3.to || node3.to === state.doc.length && !state.doc.text.endsWith("\n"))
    );
    if (!table) return;
    const rows = table.children ?? [];
    const row = rows.findIndex(
      (node3) => range2.head >= node3.from && (range2.head < node3.to || node3.to === state.doc.length)
    );
    if (row < 0) return;
    const cells = rows[row].children ?? [];
    const column = Math.max(
      0,
      cells.findIndex((cell2, index) => range2.head <= cell2.to || index === cells.length - 1)
    );
    const cell = cells[column];
    if (!cell) return;
    return {
      table,
      rows,
      cells,
      row,
      column,
      cell,
      withinCell: range2.from >= cell.from && range2.to <= cell.to,
      columnCount: Math.max(...rows.map((row2) => row2.children?.length ?? 0))
    };
  }
  function select(cell) {
    return {
      selection: Selection.range(cell.from, cell.to),
      addToHistory: false,
      origin: { type: "command", id: "table.navigate" }
    };
  }
  function cursor(position) {
    return {
      selection: Selection.cursor(position),
      addToHistory: false,
      origin: { type: "command", id: "table.navigate" }
    };
  }
  function edit(changes, position, id2) {
    return {
      changes,
      selection: Selection.cursor(position),
      origin: { type: "command", id: `table.${id2}` },
      historyGroup: "isolate"
    };
  }
  function eol(state, context) {
    return state.doc.slice(context.table.from, context.table.to).includes("\r\n") ? "\r\n" : "\n";
  }
  function changedTable(state, context, changes, row, column, id2) {
    const source = state.doc.apply(changes).text;
    const table = parseMarkdown(
      source.slice(context.table.from, changes.mapPosition(context.table.to))
    ).root.children?.find((node3) => node3.type === "table");
    const cells = table?.children?.[row]?.children;
    const cell = cells?.[Math.min(column, cells.length - 1)];
    return edit(changes, context.table.from + (cell?.from ?? 0), id2);
  }
  function addRow(state, context, after, column) {
    const nl = eol(state, context);
    const delimiter = state.doc.line(state.doc.lineAt(context.table.from).number + 1);
    const delimiterEnd = delimiter.to + (state.doc.text.slice(delimiter.to, delimiter.to + 2) === "\r\n" ? 2 : state.doc.text[delimiter.to] === "\n" ? 1 : 0);
    const at = after === 0 ? delimiterEnd : context.rows[after].to;
    const atEnd = at === state.doc.length && !state.doc.text.endsWith("\n");
    const row = `| ${Array.from({ length: context.columnCount }, () => "").join(" | ")} |`;
    const changes = ChangeSet.insert(at, `${atEnd ? nl : ""}${row}${atEnd ? "" : nl}`);
    return changedTable(state, context, changes, after + 1, column, "add-row");
  }
  function tableCommand(state, context, command, text2) {
    if (!isTableCommand(command)) return;
    const { table, rows, row, column, cell, columnCount } = context;
    if (command === "select-table")
      return {
        selection: Selection.range(table.from, table.to),
        addToHistory: false,
        origin: { type: "command", id: "table.select" }
      };
    if (command === "select-cell") return select(cell);
    if (!context.withinCell) return;
    if (command === "clear-cell")
      return edit(ChangeSet.delete(cell.from, cell.to), cell.from, command);
    if (command === "set-cell") {
      if (typeof text2 !== "string") return;
      const insert = tableCellText(text2);
      return edit(ChangeSet.replace(cell.from, cell.to, insert), cell.from + insert.length, command);
    }
    if (command.startsWith("align-")) {
      const delimiter = state.doc.line(state.doc.lineAt(table.from).number + 1);
      const matches = [...delimiter.text.matchAll(/:?-+:?/g)];
      const target = matches[column];
      if (!target || target.index === void 0) return;
      const alignment = command.slice(6);
      const insert = alignment === "left" ? ":---" : alignment === "right" ? "---:" : alignment === "center" ? ":---:" : "---";
      const changes = ChangeSet.replace(
        delimiter.from + target.index,
        delimiter.from + target.index + target[0].length,
        insert
      );
      return {
        ...edit(changes, changes.mapPosition(state.selection.main.head), command),
        selection: state.selection.map(changes)
      };
    }
    if (command === "exit") {
      const nl2 = eol(state, context);
      if (table.to < state.doc.length && state.doc.lineAt(table.to).text === "")
        return cursor(table.to);
      const insert = table.to === state.doc.length && !state.doc.text.endsWith("\n") ? nl2 + nl2 : nl2;
      return edit(
        ChangeSet.insert(table.to, insert),
        table.to + (table.to === state.doc.length ? insert.length : 0),
        "exit"
      );
    }
    if (command === "next" || command === "previous" || command === "down") {
      let nextRow = row, nextColumn = column;
      if (command === "down") nextRow++;
      else if (command === "next") {
        nextColumn++;
        if (nextColumn >= context.cells.length) {
          nextColumn = 0;
          nextRow++;
        }
      } else {
        nextColumn--;
        if (nextColumn < 0) {
          nextRow--;
          nextColumn = (rows[nextRow]?.children?.length ?? 1) - 1;
        }
      }
      if (nextRow < 0)
        return table.from > 0 ? cursor(state.doc.lineAt(table.from - 1).to) : edit(ChangeSet.insert(0, eol(state, context)), 0, "exit-before");
      if (nextRow >= rows.length)
        return addRow(state, context, rows.length - 1, command === "down" ? column : 0);
      const cells = rows[nextRow].children;
      return select(cells[Math.min(nextColumn, cells.length - 1)]);
    }
    if (command === "add-row") return addRow(state, context, row, column);
    if (command === "delete-row") {
      if (row === 0) return;
      const target = rows[row];
      const changes = ChangeSet.delete(target.from, target.to);
      return changedTable(
        state,
        context,
        changes,
        Math.min(row, rows.length - 2),
        column,
        "delete-row"
      );
    }
    if (command === "delete-column" && columnCount <= 2) return;
    if (command !== "add-column" && command !== "delete-column") return;
    const contents = rows.map(
      (row2) => Array.from({ length: columnCount }, (_, index) => {
        const cell2 = row2.children?.[index];
        return cell2 ? state.doc.slice(cell2.from, cell2.to) : "";
      })
    );
    const aligns = Array.isArray(table.data?.alignments) ? table.data.alignments : [];
    const delimiters = Array.from(
      { length: columnCount },
      (_, i) => aligns[i] === "left" ? ":---" : aligns[i] === "right" ? "---:" : aligns[i] === "center" ? ":---:" : "---"
    );
    for (const values of [...contents, delimiters]) {
      if (command === "add-column") values.splice(column + 1, 0, values === delimiters ? "---" : "");
      else values.splice(column, 1);
    }
    const nl = eol(state, context);
    const lines = [contents[0], delimiters, ...contents.slice(1)].map(
      (cells) => `| ${cells.join(" | ")} |`
    );
    const source = lines.join(nl) + (state.doc.slice(table.from, table.to).endsWith("\n") ? nl : "");
    return changedTable(
      state,
      context,
      ChangeSet.replace(table.from, table.to, source),
      row,
      command === "add-column" ? column + 1 : Math.min(column, columnCount - 2),
      command
    );
  }
  function tableCellText(text2, prefix = "") {
    const precedingSlashes = /\\*$/.exec(prefix)?.[0].length ?? 0;
    return text2.replace(
      /(\\*)\|/g,
      (match, slashes, offset) => (slashes.length + (offset === 0 ? precedingSlashes : 0)) % 2 ? match : `${slashes}\\|`
    ).replace(/\r\n|\r|\n/g, "<br>");
  }
  function tableArrowSelection(state, table, direction, extend) {
    const { doc, selection } = state;
    const range2 = selection.main;
    const forward = direction === "right" || direction === "down";
    let next = range2.head;
    let target = table.cell;
    if (!extend && !range2.empty) next = forward ? range2.to : range2.from;
    else if (extend && (direction === "up" || direction === "down"))
      next = forward ? target.to : target.from;
    else if (direction === "left" || direction === "right") {
      next = forward ? nextGraphemePosition(doc, range2.head) : previousGraphemePosition(doc, range2.head);
      if (next < target.from || next > target.to) {
        if (extend) next = forward ? target.to : target.from;
        else {
          const adjacent = forward ? table.cells[table.column + 1] ?? table.rows[table.row + 1]?.children?.[0] : table.cells[table.column - 1] ?? table.rows[table.row - 1]?.children?.at(-1);
          if (adjacent) {
            target = adjacent;
            next = forward ? target.from : target.to;
          } else next = outsidePosition(state, table, forward);
        }
      }
    } else {
      const row = table.rows[table.row + (forward ? 1 : -1)];
      const adjacent = row?.children?.[Math.min(table.column, (row.children?.length ?? 1) - 1)];
      if (adjacent) {
        target = adjacent;
        next = snapGraphemePosition(
          doc,
          Math.min(target.to, target.from + range2.head - table.cell.from)
        );
      } else next = outsidePosition(state, table, forward);
    }
    for (const atom of doc.slice(target.from, target.to).matchAll(/<br[ \t]*\/?>|\\\|/gi)) {
      const from = target.from + atom.index;
      const to = from + atom[0].length;
      if (next > from && next < to) next = forward ? to : from;
    }
    return extend ? Selection.range(range2.anchor, next) : Selection.cursor(next);
  }
  function outsidePosition(state, table, forward) {
    const doc = state.doc;
    if (forward) return table.table.to < doc.length ? table.table.to : table.cell.to;
    return table.table.from > 0 ? doc.lineAt(table.table.from - 1).to : table.cell.from;
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/editing-context.ts
  var editingContextLimits = Object.freeze({ text: 65536, lines: 256, columns: 16 });
  function tableEditingContext(state, context, offset = 0) {
    const { table } = context;
    if (table.to - table.from > editingContextLimits.text || context.rows.length + 1 > editingContextLimits.lines || context.columnCount > editingContextLimits.columns)
      return;
    const alignment = table.data?.alignments?.[context.column];
    return {
      from: offset + table.from,
      to: offset + table.to,
      text: state.doc.slice(table.from, table.to),
      selection: {
        anchor: offset + state.selection.main.anchor,
        head: offset + state.selection.main.head
      },
      row: context.row,
      column: context.column,
      rowCount: context.rows.length,
      columnCount: context.columnCount,
      alignment: alignment === "left" || alignment === "right" || alignment === "center" ? alignment : "default",
      withinCell: context.withinCell
    };
  }
  function editingContextFromState(state, root, offset = 0) {
    const range2 = state.selection.main;
    const code = (node3) => node3.from <= range2.to && (node3.to > range2.from || range2.empty && node3.to === range2.from && node3.to === state.doc.length && !state.doc.text.endsWith("\n")) && (node3.type === "fencedCode" || node3.type === "indentedCode" || !!node3.children?.some(code));
    if (code(root)) return { kind: "code", complete: true };
    const uncertainContainer = (node3) => {
      if (node3.from > range2.to) return false;
      if (["blockquote", "unorderedList", "orderedList", "listItem"].includes(node3.type)) {
        if (node3.to - node3.from > editingContextLimits.text) return true;
        const text2 = state.doc.slice(node3.from, node3.to);
        if (/^[\t >+*\d.)-]*(?:`{3,}|~{3,})/m.test(text2)) return true;
        if (node3.to >= range2.from && (/^(?: {4}|\t| {0,3}>[ \t]?(?: {4}|\t))/m.test(text2) || /<\/?[A-Za-z!][^>]*>/.test(text2)))
          return true;
      }
      return !!node3.children?.some(uncertainContainer);
    };
    const unsupportedHTML = (node3) => node3.from <= range2.to && node3.to >= range2.from && (node3.type === "fallbackText" && node3.data?.block === "html" || !!node3.children?.some(unsupportedHTML));
    if (unsupportedHTML(root) || uncertainContainer(root)) return { kind: "unknown", complete: false };
    const context = tableContext(state, root);
    if (context) {
      const table = tableEditingContext(state, context, offset);
      return { kind: "table", complete: !!table, ...table ? { table } : {} };
    }
    if (root.children?.some(
      (node3) => node3.type === "table" && node3.from < range2.to && node3.to > range2.from
    ))
      return { kind: "table", complete: false };
    return { kind: "text", complete: true };
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/presentation.ts
  var fonts2 = {
    system: 'system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif',
    serif: 'Georgia, "Times New Roman", "Noto Serif CJK SC", serif',
    monospace: '"Cascadia Code", "SFMono-Regular", Consolas, "Liberation Mono", Menlo, monospace'
  };
  function validatePresentation(value) {
    if (!Object.hasOwn(fonts2, value.fontFamily) || !Number.isFinite(value.fontSize) || value.fontSize < 12 || value.fontSize > 32 || !Number.isFinite(value.lineHeight) || value.lineHeight < 1.2 || value.lineHeight > 2.4 || !Number.isFinite(value.contentWidth) || value.contentWidth !== 0 && (value.contentWidth < 480 || value.contentWidth > 1200) || !["spaces-2", "spaces-4", "tabs"].includes(value.indentation)) {
      throw new RangeError("Invalid editor presentation");
    }
    return Object.freeze({ ...value });
  }
  function applyPresentation(dom, value) {
    const height = value.fontSize * value.lineHeight;
    dom.style.fontFamily = fonts2[value.fontFamily];
    dom.style.fontSize = `${value.fontSize}px`;
    dom.style.lineHeight = `${height}px`;
    dom.style.setProperty("--milkup-line-height", `${height}px`);
    dom.style.setProperty(
      "--milkup-content-width",
      value.contentWidth ? `${value.contentWidth}px` : "none"
    );
  }
  function samePresentationLayout(previous, next) {
    return previous !== void 0 && previous.fontFamily === next.fontFamily && previous.fontSize === next.fontSize && previous.lineHeight === next.lineHeight && previous.contentWidth === next.contentWidth;
  }
  function indentationText(value) {
    return value?.indentation === "tabs" ? "	" : value?.indentation === "spaces-4" ? "    " : "  ";
  }
  function presentationOutdent(text2, value) {
    if (text2.startsWith("	")) return 1;
    return Math.min(/^ */u.exec(text2)[0].length, value?.indentation === "spaces-4" ? 4 : 2);
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/live-controls.ts
  function applyTableAlignments(line, table) {
    const alignments = table?.data?.alignments;
    if (!Array.isArray(alignments)) return;
    line.querySelectorAll(".milkup-table-cell").forEach((cell, column) => {
      const alignment = alignments[column];
      if (alignment === "left" || alignment === "center" || alignment === "right") {
        cell.style.textAlign = alignment;
      }
    });
  }
  function createTaskMarker(document, from, to, text2) {
    const task = document.createElement("button");
    task.type = "button";
    task.className = "milkup-task-marker";
    task.dataset.from = String(from);
    task.dataset.to = String(to);
    task.dataset.checked = String(/[xX]/u.test(text2));
    task.setAttribute("role", "checkbox");
    task.setAttribute("aria-checked", task.dataset.checked);
    task.setAttribute("aria-label", "Toggle task");
    return task;
  }
  function taskMarkerFromEvent(event, content) {
    const target = event.target;
    const task = target instanceof Element ? target.closest(".milkup-task-marker") : null;
    return task && content.contains(task) ? task : void 0;
  }
  function syncTaskAvailability(content, editable) {
    content.querySelectorAll(".milkup-task-marker").forEach((task) => {
      if (task.disabled === editable) task.disabled = !editable;
    });
  }
  function taskMarkerEdit(from, text2) {
    const marker = /^(\s*\[)([ xX])\]\s*$/u.exec(text2);
    if (!marker) return;
    const position = from + marker[1].length;
    return { from: position, to: position + 1, insert: marker[2] === " " ? "x" : " " };
  }

  // packages/view-dom/src/source-position-mapping.ts
  function isHorizontalScrollbarDragStart(event) {
    const target = event.target;
    if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) {
      return false;
    }
    const scrollable = target.closest(".milkup-block-code");
    if (!scrollable || scrollable.scrollWidth <= scrollable.clientWidth) {
      return false;
    }
    const scrollbarHeight = scrollable.offsetHeight - scrollable.clientHeight;
    if (scrollbarHeight <= 0) {
      return false;
    }
    const rect = scrollable.getBoundingClientRect();
    return event.clientY >= rect.bottom - scrollbarHeight && event.clientY <= rect.bottom;
  }
  function isEditorScrollbarDragStart(event, editor) {
    const rect = editor.getBoundingClientRect();
    const vertical = Math.max(editor.offsetWidth - editor.clientWidth, 10);
    const horizontal = Math.max(editor.offsetHeight - editor.clientHeight, 10);
    return editor.scrollHeight > editor.clientHeight && event.clientX >= rect.right - vertical && event.clientX <= rect.right && event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom || editor.scrollWidth > editor.clientWidth && event.clientY >= rect.bottom - horizontal && event.clientY <= rect.bottom && event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right;
  }
  function lineElementFromEvent(event, contentDOM, overlayDOM) {
    if (event.target instanceof HTMLElement) {
      const direct = event.target.closest(".milkup-line");
      if (direct && contentDOM.contains(direct)) {
        return direct;
      }
    }
    const previousPointerEvents = overlayDOM?.style.pointerEvents;
    if (overlayDOM) {
      overlayDOM.style.pointerEvents = "none";
    }
    const elements = typeof contentDOM.ownerDocument.elementsFromPoint === "function" ? contentDOM.ownerDocument.elementsFromPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY) : [];
    if (overlayDOM) {
      overlayDOM.style.pointerEvents = previousPointerEvents ?? "";
    }
    for (const element of elements) {
      if (!(element instanceof HTMLElement) || !contentDOM.contains(element)) {
        continue;
      }
      const line = element.closest(".milkup-line");
      if (line && contentDOM.contains(line)) {
        return line;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function sourcePositionFromPoint(lineDOM, event, overlayDOM, options = {}) {
    const tableCell = tableCellFromPointer(lineDOM, event);
    if (tableCell) {
      const mapped2 = mappedElementInTableCellFromPointer(tableCell, event);
      const from2 = Number(mapped2.dataset.from);
      const to2 = Number(mapped2.dataset.to);
      if (Number.isInteger(from2) && Number.isInteger(to2)) {
        return clamp2(from2 + estimateElementOffsetFromPointer(mapped2, event), from2, to2);
      }
    }
    const point = caretPointFromDocument(
      lineDOM.ownerDocument,
      event.clientX,
      event.clientY,
      overlayDOM
    );
    if (point && lineDOM.contains(point.node)) {
      if (point.node instanceof Text) {
        const fromText = sourcePositionFromTextNode(
          point.node,
          nearestTextOffsetFromPointer(point.node, event.clientX, event.clientY, point.offset),
          lineDOM
        );
        if (fromText !== void 0) {
          return fromText;
        }
      }
      if (point.node instanceof HTMLElement) {
        const child = point.node.childNodes.item(point.offset);
        const textNode2 = findNearestTextNode(child, point.node);
        if (textNode2 instanceof Text && !isHiddenTextNode(textNode2, lineDOM)) {
          const fromText = sourcePositionFromTextNode(
            textNode2,
            nearestTextOffsetFromPointer(textNode2, event.clientX, event.clientY),
            lineDOM
          );
          if (fromText !== void 0) {
            return fromText;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (!options.allowGeometryFallback) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const target = event.target;
    const element = target instanceof HTMLElement ? target : target instanceof Node ? target.parentElement : null;
    const mapped = element?.closest("[data-from][data-to]");
    if (mapped && lineDOM.contains(mapped) && !mapped.classList.contains("milkup-marker-hidden")) {
      const from2 = Number(mapped.dataset.from);
      const to2 = Number(mapped.dataset.to);
      if (Number.isInteger(from2) && Number.isInteger(to2)) {
        return clamp2(from2 + estimateElementOffsetFromPointer(mapped, event), from2, to2);
      }
    }
    const visibleMappedPosition = estimateVisibleMappedPositionFromPointer(lineDOM, event);
    if (visibleMappedPosition !== void 0) {
      return visibleMappedPosition;
    }
    const from = Number(lineDOM.dataset.from);
    const to = Number(lineDOM.dataset.to);
    if (!Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return clamp2(from + estimateElementOffsetFromPointer(lineDOM, event), from, to);
  }
  function tableCellFromPointer(lineDOM, event) {
    const target = event.target;
    const targetElement = target instanceof HTMLElement ? target : target instanceof Node ? target.parentElement : null;
    const direct = targetElement?.closest(".milkup-table-cell");
    if (direct && lineDOM.contains(direct)) {
      return direct;
    }
    return Array.from(lineDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-table-cell")).find((cell) => {
      const rect = cell.getBoundingClientRect();
      return event.clientX >= rect.left && event.clientX <= rect.right && event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom;
    });
  }
  function mappedElementInTableCellFromPointer(tableCell, event) {
    const target = event.target;
    const targetElement = target instanceof HTMLElement ? target : target instanceof Node ? target.parentElement : null;
    const direct = targetElement?.closest("[data-from][data-to]");
    if (direct && (direct === tableCell || tableCell.contains(direct)) && !direct.classList.contains("milkup-marker-hidden")) {
      return direct;
    }
    let nearest = tableCell;
    for (const candidate of Array.from(
      tableCell.querySelectorAll("[data-from][data-to]")
    )) {
      if (candidate.classList.contains("milkup-marker-hidden")) {
        continue;
      }
      const rect = candidate.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (event.clientX < rect.left || event.clientX > rect.right || event.clientY < rect.top || event.clientY > rect.bottom) {
        continue;
      }
      if (nearest === tableCell || nearest.contains(candidate)) {
        nearest = candidate;
      }
    }
    return nearest;
  }
  function domRectForLineSourcePosition(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, position, options = {}) {
    const emptyCell = Array.from(lineDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-table-cell")).find(
      (cell) => Number(cell.dataset.from) === position && Number(cell.dataset.to) === position
    );
    if (emptyCell) {
      const rect2 = emptyCell.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect2.width > 0 && rect2.height > 0) {
        const reference = referenceDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
        const style = document.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(emptyCell);
        const left = rect2.left - reference.left + (Number.parseFloat(style?.paddingLeft ?? "") || 0);
        const top = rect2.top - reference.top + (Number.parseFloat(style?.paddingTop ?? "") || 0) + (Number.parseFloat(style?.borderTopWidth ?? "") || 0);
        const height = resolveLineCursorHeight(emptyCell, options.fallbackLineHeight ?? 20);
        return { left, top, right: left, bottom: top + height, width: 0, height };
      }
    }
    const sourceRect = domRectForMappedSourcePosition(
      document,
      lineDOM,
      referenceDOM,
      position,
      options
    );
    if (sourceRect) {
      return sourceRect;
    }
    const rect = domRectForLineVisualOffset(
      document,
      lineDOM,
      referenceDOM,
      sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(lineDOM, position),
      options
    );
    if (rect || !options.allowGeometryFallback) {
      return rect;
    }
    return estimateLineSourcePositionRect(lineDOM, referenceDOM, position, options.fallbackLineHeight);
  }
  function domRectsForLineSourceRange(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, from, to) {
    const sourceRects = domRectsForMappedSourceRange(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, from, to);
    if (sourceRects.length > 0) {
      return sourceRects;
    }
    return domRectsForLineVisualRange(
      document,
      lineDOM,
      referenceDOM,
      sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(lineDOM, from),
      sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(lineDOM, to)
    );
  }
  function domRectForLineVisualOffset(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, visualOffset, options = {}) {
    if (typeof document.createRange !== "function") {
      return void 0;
    }
    if (!hasMeasurableLayout(lineDOM)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const lineRect = lineDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const range2 = createRangeAtVisualOffset(document, lineDOM, visualOffset);
    if (!range2) {
      return rectFromLineStart(lineDOM, referenceDOM, options.fallbackLineHeight);
    }
    if (typeof range2.getBoundingClientRect !== "function") {
      range2.detach();
      return void 0;
    }
    const rect = range2.getBoundingClientRect();
    range2.detach();
    if (!isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const referenceRect = referenceDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const height = rect.height > 0 ? rect.height : lineRect.height;
    const top = rect.top - referenceRect.top;
    const bottom = top + height;
    return Object.freeze({
      left: rect.left - referenceRect.left,
      top,
      right: rect.right - referenceRect.left,
      bottom,
      width: rect.width,
      height
    });
  }
  function domRectsForLineVisualRange(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, fromOffset, toOffset) {
    if (typeof document.createRange !== "function") {
      return Object.freeze([]);
    }
    const range2 = createRangeBetweenVisualOffsets(document, lineDOM, fromOffset, toOffset);
    if (!range2) {
      return Object.freeze([]);
    }
    const lineRect = lineDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const referenceRect = referenceDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientRects = Array.from(range2.getClientRects?.() ?? []);
    const usableRects = clientRects.filter((rect) => isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect));
    if (usableRects.length === 0 && typeof range2.getBoundingClientRect === "function") {
      const rect = range2.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect)) {
        usableRects.push(rect);
      }
    }
    range2.detach();
    return Object.freeze(
      usableRects.map((rect) => {
        const top = rect.top - referenceRect.top;
        const height = rect.height > 0 ? rect.height : lineRect.height;
        const clipCode = lineDOM.classList.contains("milkup-block-code") && lineDOM.clientWidth > 0;
        const clipLeft = lineRect.left + lineDOM.clientLeft;
        const clipRight = clipLeft + lineDOM.clientWidth;
        const left = clipCode ? clamp2(rect.left, clipLeft, clipRight) : rect.left;
        const right = clipCode ? clamp2(rect.right, clipLeft, clipRight) : rect.right;
        return Object.freeze({
          left: left - referenceRect.left,
          top,
          right: right - referenceRect.left,
          bottom: top + height,
          width: Math.max(0, right - left),
          height
        });
      })
    );
  }
  function sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(lineDOM, position) {
    const lineFrom = Number(lineDOM.dataset.from);
    const lineTo = Number(lineDOM.dataset.to);
    if (!Number.isInteger(lineFrom) || !Number.isInteger(lineTo)) {
      return 0;
    }
    const segments = visibleTextSourceSegments(lineDOM);
    if (segments.length === 0) {
      return clamp2(position, lineFrom, lineTo) - lineFrom;
    }
    for (const segment of segments) {
      if (position <= segment.from) {
        return segment.visualFrom;
      }
      if (position <= segment.to) {
        const sourceLength = segment.to - segment.from;
        if (sourceLength <= 0) {
          return segment.visualFrom;
        }
        const ratio = clamp2((position - segment.from) / sourceLength, 0, 1);
        return Math.round(segment.visualFrom + ratio * (segment.visualTo - segment.visualFrom));
      }
    }
    return segments.at(-1)?.visualTo ?? 0;
  }
  function domRectForMappedSourcePosition(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, position, options) {
    if (typeof document.createRange !== "function" || !hasMeasurableLayout(lineDOM)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const textPosition = resolveTextPositionAtSourcePosition(lineDOM, position, 1);
    if (!textPosition) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const range2 = document.createRange();
    range2.setStart(textPosition.node, textPosition.offset);
    range2.collapse(true);
    if (typeof range2.getBoundingClientRect !== "function") {
      range2.detach();
      return void 0;
    }
    const lineRect = lineDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const rect = range2.getBoundingClientRect();
    range2.detach();
    if (!isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return viewRectFromClientRect(rect, referenceDOM, lineRect, options.fallbackLineHeight);
  }
  function domRectsForMappedSourceRange(document, lineDOM, referenceDOM, from, to) {
    if (typeof document.createRange !== "function" || to <= from) {
      return Object.freeze([]);
    }
    const start = resolveTextPositionAtSourcePosition(lineDOM, from, 1);
    const end = resolveTextPositionAtSourcePosition(lineDOM, to, -1);
    if (!start || !end) {
      return Object.freeze([]);
    }
    const range2 = document.createRange();
    range2.setStart(start.node, start.offset);
    range2.setEnd(end.node, end.offset);
    const lineRect = lineDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const referenceRect = referenceDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientRects = Array.from(range2.getClientRects?.() ?? []);
    const usableRects = clientRects.filter((rect) => isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect));
    if (usableRects.length === 0 && typeof range2.getBoundingClientRect === "function") {
      const rect = range2.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (isUsableMeasuredRect(rect, lineRect)) {
        usableRects.push(rect);
      }
    }
    range2.detach();
    return Object.freeze(
      usableRects.map((rect) => {
        const top = rect.top - referenceRect.top;
        const height = rect.height > 0 ? rect.height : lineRect.height;
        const clipCode = lineDOM.classList.contains("milkup-block-code") && lineDOM.clientWidth > 0;
        const clipLeft = lineRect.left + lineDOM.clientLeft;
        const clipRight = clipLeft + lineDOM.clientWidth;
        const left = clipCode ? clamp2(rect.left, clipLeft, clipRight) : rect.left;
        const right = clipCode ? clamp2(rect.right, clipLeft, clipRight) : rect.right;
        return Object.freeze({
          left: left - referenceRect.left,
          top,
          right: right - referenceRect.left,
          bottom: top + height,
          width: Math.max(0, right - left),
          height
        });
      })
    );
  }
  function resolveTextPositionAtSourcePosition(lineDOM, position, affinity) {
    const element = sourceMappedElementAtPosition(lineDOM, position, affinity);
    if (!element) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const from = Number(element.dataset.from);
    const to = Number(element.dataset.to);
    if (!Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const sourceLength = Math.max(0, to - from);
    const textNodes = visibleTextNodes(element);
    const textLength = textNodes.reduce((length2, node3) => length2 + node3.length, 0);
    if (textLength <= 0) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const sourceOffset = clamp2(position, from, to) - from;
    const textOffset = sourceLength > 0 ? Math.round(sourceOffset / sourceLength * textLength) : 0;
    return resolveTextPositionInNodes(textNodes, textOffset);
  }
  function sourceMappedElementAtPosition(lineDOM, position, affinity) {
    const lineFrom = Number(lineDOM.dataset.from);
    const lineTo = Number(lineDOM.dataset.to);
    if (!Number.isInteger(lineFrom) || !Number.isInteger(lineTo)) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const sourcePosition = clamp2(position, lineFrom, lineTo);
    const elements = visibleSourceMappedElements(lineDOM);
    if (elements.length === 0) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const exactBoundary = affinity > 0 ? elements.filter((element) => Number(element.dataset.from) === sourcePosition) : elements.filter((element) => Number(element.dataset.to) === sourcePosition);
    if (exactBoundary.length > 0) {

    }

      const from = Number(element.dataset.from);
      const to = Number(element.dataset.to);
      return Number.isInteger(from) && Number.isInteger(to) && sourcePosition > from && sourcePosition < to;
    });
    if (containing.length > 0) {
      return mostSpecificMappedElement(containing);
    }
    const adjacentBoundary = affinity > 0 ? elements.filter((element) => Number(element.dataset.to) === sourcePosition) : elements.filter((element) => Number(element.dataset.from) === sourcePosition);
    if (adjacentBoundary.length > 0) {
      return mostSpecificMappedElement(adjacentBoundary);
    }
    const next = elements.find((element) => sourcePosition < Number(element.dataset.from));
    if (next) {
      return next;
    }
    return elements.at(-1);
  }
  function visibleSourceMappedElements(lineDOM) {

      })
    );

      const rightLength = Number(right.dataset.to) - Number(right.dataset.from);

    })[0];

  function resolveTextPositionInNodes(textNodes, textOffset) {

  function visibleTextSourceSegments(lineDOM) {

  function sourcePositionFromTextNode(node3, offset, lineDOM) {

      return void 0;

  function caretRectForTextOffset(node3, offset) {

  }

  function textOffsetWithinElement(root, node3, offset) {

      }

        textOffset += current.data.length;
      }
      current = walker.nextNode();
    }

  }
  function createRangeAtVisualOffset(document, lineDOM, visualOffset) {

    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function createRangeBetweenVisualOffsets(document, lineDOM, fromOffset, toOffset) {

  function visibleTextNodes(root) {

  function isHiddenTextNode(node3, root) {

  function estimateElementOffsetFromPointer(element, event) {
    const from = Number(element.dataset.from);
    const to = Number(element.dataset.to);

      }
    }

      return 0;
    }

  }
  function nearestTextOffsetFromPointer(node3, clientX, clientY, fallbackOffset = 0) {
    let nearestOffset = clamp2(fallbackOffset, 0, node3.length);
    let nearestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const candidates = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    if (node3.length <= 256) {
      for (let offset = 0; offset <= node3.length; offset += 1) {
        candidates.add(offset);
      }
    } else {
      let low = 0;
      let high = node3.length;
      while (low <= high) {
        const offset = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
        const rect = caretRectForTextOffset(node3, offset);
        candidates.add(offset);
        if (!rect) {
          break;
        }
        if (clientY > rect.bottom || clientY >= rect.top && clientX > rect.left) {
          low = offset + 1;
        } else {
          high = offset - 1;
        }
      }
      for (const offset of [0, node3.length, fallbackOffset, low, high]) {
        for (let nearby = offset - 2; nearby <= offset + 2; nearby += 1) {
          candidates.add(clamp2(nearby, 0, node3.length));
        }
      }
    }
    for (const offset of candidates) {
      const rect = caretRectForTextOffset(node3, offset);
      if (!rect) {
        continue;
      }
      const distance = pointToRectDistance(clientX, clientY, rect);
      if (distance < nearestDistance) {
        nearestDistance = distance;
        nearestOffset = offset;
      }
    }
    return nearestOffset;
  }
  function nearestElementTextOffsetFromPointer(element, clientX, clientY) {
    let elementOffset = 0;
    let nearestOffset;
    let nearestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (const node3 of visibleTextNodes(element)) {
      const offset = nearestTextOffsetFromPointer(node3, clientX, clientY);
      const rect = caretRectForTextOffset(node3, offset);
      if (rect) {
        const distance = pointToRectDistance(clientX, clientY, rect);
        if (distance < nearestDistance) {
          nearestDistance = distance;
          nearestOffset = elementOffset + offset;
        }
      }
      elementOffset += node3.length;
    }
    return nearestOffset;
  }
  function pointToRectDistance(clientX, clientY, rect) {
    const horizontal = clientX < rect.left ? rect.left - clientX : clientX > rect.right ? clientX - rect.right : 0;
    const vertical = clientY < rect.top ? rect.top - clientY : clientY > rect.bottom ? clientY - rect.bottom : 0;
    return horizontal * horizontal + vertical * vertical;
  }
  function estimateVisibleMappedPositionFromPointer(lineDOM, event) {
    const mappedElements = Array.from(lineDOM.querySelectorAll("[data-from][data-to]")).filter((element) => !element.classList.contains("milkup-marker-hidden")).sort((left, right) => Number(left.dataset.from) - Number(right.dataset.from));
    if (mappedElements.length === 0) {
      return void 0;
    }

        continue;
      }

      }

      if (!nearest || distance < nearest.distance) {
        nearest = { element, distance };
      }
    }

      return void 0;
    }

  }
  function estimateLineSourcePositionRect(lineDOM, referenceDOM, position, fallbackLineHeight = 20) {

  function estimateLineSourcePositionClientLeft(lineDOM, position, lineRect) {

  function resolveLineCursorHeight(lineDOM, fallbackLineHeight) {

    if (Number.isFinite(fontSize) && fontSize > 0) {

  }

  function scrollSourcePositionIntoView(scrollable, line, position) {
    if (scrollable.clientWidth <= 0 || scrollable.scrollWidth <= scrollable.clientWidth) return false;
    const rect = domRectForLineSourcePosition(line.ownerDocument, line, scrollable, position);
    if (!rect) return false;
    const padding = 8;
    const left = scrollable.clientLeft + padding;
    const right = scrollable.clientLeft + scrollable.clientWidth - padding;
    const delta = rect.left < left ? rect.left - left : rect.right > right ? rect.right - right : 0;
    const previous = scrollable.scrollLeft;
    scrollable.scrollLeft = clamp2(
      previous + delta,
      0,
      scrollable.scrollWidth - scrollable.clientWidth
    );
    return scrollable.scrollLeft !== previous;
  }
  function isCodePositionClipped(line, reference, rect) {

  function getVisibleLineWindow(config) {
    const lineCount = Math.max(1, Math.floor(config.lineCount));
    const lineHeight = Math.max(1, config.lineHeight ?? defaultViewMetrics.lineHeight);
    const viewportHeight = Math.max(lineHeight, config.viewportHeight ?? lineHeight * 24);
    const overscanLines = Math.max(0, Math.floor(config.overscanLines ?? 4));
    const firstVisibleLine = clamp3(
      Math.floor(Math.max(0, config.scrollTop) / lineHeight) + 1,
      1,
      lineCount
    );
    const visibleLineCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(viewportHeight / lineHeight));
    const fromLine = clamp3(firstVisibleLine - overscanLines, 1, lineCount);
    const toLine = clamp3(firstVisibleLine + visibleLineCount + overscanLines - 1, fromLine, lineCount);
    return Object.freeze({
      fromLine,
      toLine,
      topSpacerHeight: (fromLine - 1) * lineHeight,
      bottomSpacerHeight: (lineCount - toLine) * lineHeight
    });
  }

        const table = parsed.root.children?.find((n) => n.type === "table");
        if (!table || (table.children?.length ?? 0) + 1 > windowedTableLimits.lines || table.children?.some((row) => (row.children?.length ?? 0) > windowedTableLimits.columns))
          window = void 0;
        return window;
      } catch (error) {
        if (!String(error).includes("text budget") && !String(error).includes("Table source version changed"))
          throw error;
        return void 0;
      } finally {
        if (this.source === source && this.version === version && source.version === version) {
          this.entries.push({ from, to, ...window ? { window } : {} });
          while (this.entries.length > 4) this.entries.shift();
        }
      }
    }
  };

  // packages/view-dom/src/bounded-scroll.ts
  var maxVirtualContentHeight = 8e6;
  function boundedScrollGeometry(lineCount, lineHeight, viewportHeight, padding = 0) {
    const logicalHeight = lineCount * lineHeight;
    const physicalHeight = Math.min(logicalHeight, maxVirtualContentHeight);
    const logicalRange = Math.max(0, logicalHeight + padding - viewportHeight);
    const physicalRange = Math.max(0, physicalHeight + padding - viewportHeight);
    const edge = Math.min(2048, physicalRange / 4);
    const scale = physicalRange > 2 * edge ? (logicalRange - 2 * edge) / (physicalRange - 2 * edge) : 1;
    const toLogical = (offset) => {
      const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(offset, physicalRange));
      if (value <= edge) return value;
      if (value >= physicalRange - edge) return logicalRange - (physicalRange - value);
      return edge + (value - edge) * scale;
    };
    const toPhysical = (offset) => {
      const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(offset, logicalRange));
      if (value <= edge) return value;
      if (value >= logicalRange - edge) return physicalRange - (logicalRange - value);
      return edge + (value - edge) / scale;
    };
    return {
      compressed: logicalHeight > physicalHeight,
      physicalHeight,
      toLogical,
      toPhysical,
      spacers(fromLine, toLine, scrollTop) {
        const physical = Math.max(0, Math.min(scrollTop, physicalRange));
        const top = Math.max(0, physical - toLogical(physical) + (fromLine - 1) * lineHeight);
        return {
          top,
          bottom: Math.max(0, physicalHeight - top - (toLine - fromLine + 1) * lineHeight)
        };
      }
    };
  }

    async revealSelection(lineNumber, selection, options = {}) {
      options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
      options.assertCurrent?.();
      const revision = ++this.navigationRevision;
      const source = this.currentSource;
      const documentId = source.documentId;
      const version = source.version;
      const assertCurrent = () => {
        options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
        options.assertCurrent?.();
        if (options.assertCurrent || options.signal) {
          if (this.destroyed || revision !== this.navigationRevision || source !== this.currentSource || version !== source.version)
            throw new DOMException("Navigation invalidated", "AbortError");
        }
      };
      await this.scrollToLine(lineNumber, { assertCurrent });
      assertCurrent();
      if (this.destroyed || source !== this.currentSource || revision !== this.navigationRevision || documentId !== this.currentSource.documentId || version !== this.currentSource.version)
        return;
      const line = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
        `.milkup-line[data-line="${lineNumber}"]`
      );
      if (!line) {
        if (options.signal || options.assertCurrent)
          throw new DOMException("Navigation target window superseded", "AbortError");
        return;
      }
      const from = Number(line.dataset.from);
      const target = selection ?? Selection.cursor(from);
      const previousPosition = this.cursorPosition;
      this.cursorPosition = clamp4(target.main.head, 0, this.currentSource.length ?? target.main.head);
      this.selectionAnchor = clamp4(
        target.main.anchor,
        0,
        this.currentSource.length ?? target.main.anchor
      );
      if (this.mode === "live")
        await this.renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previousPosition, this.cursorPosition);
      assertCurrent();
      if (this.destroyed || source !== this.currentSource || version !== source.version || revision !== this.navigationRevision)
        return;
      this.revealHorizontalCursor();
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
    }
    async scrollToLine(lineNumber, options = {}) {
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version;
      const check = () => {
        options.signal?.throwIfAborted();
        options.assertCurrent?.();
        return !this.destroyed && source === this.currentSource && version === source.version;
      };
      if (!check()) return;
      if (!Number.isInteger(lineNumber) || lineNumber < 1 || lineNumber > this.currentSource.lineCount) {
        throw new RangeError(`Invalid line number: ${lineNumber}`);
      }
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry();
      this.dom.scrollTop = geometry.compressed ? geometry.toPhysical(this.lineTop(lineNumber) + this.contentPadding().top) : this.lineTop(lineNumber);
      this.scrollAnchor = Object.freeze({ scrollTop: this.dom.scrollTop });
      await this.renderVisibleWindow(this.createRenderRequestForLine(lineNumber));
      if (!check()) return;
      const target = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
        `.milkup-line[data-line="${lineNumber}"]`
      );
      if (target && target.offsetTop > 0 && Math.abs(this.dom.scrollTop - target.offsetTop) > 1) {
        this.dom.scrollTop = target.offsetTop;
        this.scrollAnchor = Object.freeze({ scrollTop: this.dom.scrollTop });
      }
    }
    async renderVisibleWindow(request = this.createRenderRequest()) {
      this.documentPresentation.hasReferencePointer();
      const editRevision = this.editRevision;
      const source = this.currentSource;
      const version = source.version;
      await (this.pendingVisibleEdit ? new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        this.pendingVisibleEditResolutions.push({ resolve, reject });
      }) : this.editCommitQueue).catch(() => void 0);
      if (this.destroyed || source !== this.currentSource || editRevision !== this.editRevision)
        return;
      if (version !== source.version) request = this.createRenderRequest();
      const renderVersion = source.version;
      const renderMode = this.mode;
      const presentationReadRevision = this.presentationReadRevision;
      const rendered = this.mode === "live" ? await this.renderLiveLineWindow(request) : await this.renderSourceLineWindow(request);
      if (this.destroyed || source !== this.currentSource || renderVersion !== source.version || renderMode !== this.mode || presentationReadRevision !== this.presentationReadRevision || request.id < this.latestAppliedRequestId || editRevision !== this.editRevision) {
        return;
      }
      if (this.documentPresentation.hasReferencePointer()) {
        const pending = this.deferredPresentationRender;
        if (pending) {
          if (request.id > pending.request.id) pending.request = request;
          return pending.promise;
        }
        const deferred = { request, promise: Promise.resolve() };
        this.deferredPresentationRender = deferred;
        deferred.promise = this.documentPresentation.waitForReferencePointer().then(async () => {
          if (this.deferredPresentationRender !== deferred) return;
          this.deferredPresentationRender = void 0;
          if (this.destroyed || source !== this.currentSource || renderVersion !== source.version || editRevision !== this.editRevision || deferred.request.id < this.latestAppliedRequestId)
            return;
          await this.renderVisibleWindow(deferred.request);
        });
        return deferred.promise;
      }
      if (rendered.presentationRange) {
        const { fromLine, toLine } = rendered.presentationRange;
        request = {
          ...request,
          fromLine,
          toLine,
          ...request.spacers ? { spacers: this.lineSpacers(fromLine, toLine, this.dom.scrollTop) } : {}
        };
      }
      this.latestAppliedRequestId = request.id;
      this.renderedSourceWindow = rendered.sourceWindow;
      this.renderedDocumentVersion = renderVersion;
      this.tableStarts = rendered.tableStarts ?? [];
      const lineHeight = this.getLineHeight();
      const focusedTask = this.ownerDocument.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement && this.contentDOM.contains(this.ownerDocument.activeElement) && this.ownerDocument.activeElement.classList.contains("milkup-task-marker") ? this.ownerDocument.activeElement.dataset.from : void 0;
      const codeScroll = new Map(
        Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-block-code"), (line) => [
          line.dataset.line,
          line.scrollLeft
        ])
      );
      const presentationMeasured = this.documentPresentation.updateContent(() => {
        this.contentDOM.replaceChildren(
          renderSpacer(
            this.ownerDocument,
            "top",
            request.spacers?.top ?? this.lineTop(request.fromLine)
          ),
          ...rendered.lines,
          renderSpacer(
            this.ownerDocument,
            "bottom",
            request.spacers?.bottom ?? this.lineTop(this.currentSource.lineCount + 1) - this.lineTop(request.toLine + 1)
          )
        );
        this.contentDOM.dataset.virtualized = "true";
        this.contentDOM.dataset.fromLine = String(request.fromLine);
        this.contentDOM.dataset.toLine = String(request.toLine);
        this.contentDOM.dataset.renderMode = this.mode;
      });
      this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-block-code").forEach((line) => {
        line.scrollLeft = codeScroll.get(line.dataset.line) ?? 0;
      });
      if (!presentationMeasured) this.measureTableRows();
      this.renderSearchHighlights();
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
      if (focusedTask !== void 0)
        this.contentDOM.querySelector(`.milkup-task-marker[data-from="${focusedTask}"]`)?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      this.scheduleMarkdownWarmup(request);
    }
    // History must share the key queue: optimistic text can be visible before its
    // transaction exists, and following keys must wait for the history operation.
    runAfterPendingEdits(action) {
      const queued = this.enqueueVisibleEdit(async () => {
        await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
        await action();
      });
      this.activeVisibleEditTasks.add(queued);
      void queued.then(
        () => this.activeVisibleEditTasks.delete(queued),
        () => this.activeVisibleEditTasks.delete(queued)
      );
      return queued;
    }
    async flushPendingEdits() {
      await this.compositionCommitQueue;
      if (this.compositionCommitError !== void 0) {
        const error = this.compositionCommitError;
        this.compositionCommitError = void 0;
        throw error;
      }
      while (this.activeVisibleEditTasks.size > 0) {
        await Promise.all([...this.activeVisibleEditTasks]);
      }
      if (this.visibleEditPreparationError !== void 0) {
        const error = this.visibleEditPreparationError;
        this.visibleEditPreparationError = void 0;
        throw error;
      }
      await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
    }
    destroy() {
      this.presentationReadRevision += 1;
      this.presentationFenceReads.clear();
      this.imagePaste.abort();
      this.images.destroy();
      this.documentPresentation.destroy();
      this.destroyed = true;
      this.navigationRevision += 1;
      this.cancelMarkdownWarmup();
      if (this.editCommitTimer !== void 0) {
        clearTimeout(this.editCommitTimer);
        this.editCommitTimer = void 0;
      }
      this.contentDOM.removeEventListener("scroll", this.handleInnerScrollEvent, true);
      this.contentDOM.removeEventListener("click", this.handleTaskClickEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("scroll", this.handleScrollEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("keydown", this.handleKeyDownEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("copy", this.handleCopyEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("cut", this.handleCutEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("paste", this.handlePasteEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("pointerdown", this.handlePointerDownEvent);
      this.dom.removeEventListener("pointermove", this.handlePointerMoveEvent);
      this.ownerDocument.removeEventListener("pointerup", this.handlePointerUpEvent);
      this.ownerDocument.removeEventListener("pointercancel", this.handlePointerCancelEvent);
      this.inputDOM.removeEventListener("input", this.handleInputEvent);
      this.inputDOM.removeEventListener("keydown", this.handleKeyDownEvent);
      this.inputDOM.removeEventListener("compositionstart", this.handleCompositionStartEvent);
      this.inputDOM.removeEventListener("compositionupdate", this.handleCompositionUpdateEvent);
      this.inputDOM.removeEventListener("compositionend", this.handleCompositionEndEvent);
      this.dom.remove();
    }
    contentPadding() {
      const style = this.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(this.contentDOM);
      return {
        top: Number.parseFloat(style?.paddingTop ?? "") || 0,
        bottom: Number.parseFloat(style?.paddingBottom ?? "") || 0
      };
    }
    lineTop(line) {
      return this.measuredHeights.top(line, this.getLineHeight());
    }
    lineAtTop(top) {
      return this.measuredHeights.lineAt(top, this.currentSource.lineCount, this.getLineHeight());
    }
    lineSpacers(fromLine, toLine, scrollTop = this.dom.scrollTop) {
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry();
      const top = Math.max(
        0,
        (geometry.compressed ? scrollTop - geometry.toLogical(scrollTop) : 0) + this.lineTop(fromLine)
      );
      return {
        top,
        bottom: Math.max(
          0,
          geometry.physicalHeight - top - (this.lineTop(toLine + 1) - this.lineTop(fromLine))
        )
      };
    }
    measureTableRows() {
      if (this.mode !== "live") return;
      for (const row of Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line"))) {
        const height = row.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        if (height > 0 || row.classList.contains("milkup-table-delimiter") || row.dataset.presentationCollapsed === "true")
          this.measuredHeights.set(Number(row.dataset.line), height);
      }
      const from = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.fromLine), to = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.toLine);
      if (!from || !to) return;
      const spacers = this.lineSpacers(from, to);
      for (const side of ["top", "bottom"]) {
        const spacer = this.contentDOM.querySelector(`[data-spacer="${side}"]`);
        if (spacer) spacer.style.height = `${spacers[side]}px`;
      }
    }
    scrollGeometry(lineCount = this.currentSource.lineCount) {
      const padding = lineCount * this.getLineHeight() > maxVirtualContentHeight ? this.contentPadding() : { top: 0, bottom: 0 };
      return boundedScrollGeometry(
        lineCount,
        this.lineTop(lineCount + 1) / Math.max(1, lineCount),
        this.getViewportHeight() ?? this.getLineHeight() * 24,
        padding.top + padding.bottom
      );
    }
    createRenderRequest(scrollTop = this.dom.scrollTop) {
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry();
      const logicalTop = geometry.compressed ? geometry.toLogical(scrollTop) : scrollTop;
      const viewportHeight = this.getViewportHeight();
      const lineWindow = getVisibleLineWindow({
        lineCount: this.currentSource.lineCount,
        scrollTop: (this.lineAtTop(
          geometry.compressed ? Math.max(0, logicalTop - this.contentPadding().top) : scrollTop
        ) - 1) * this.getLineHeight(),
        lineHeight: this.getLineHeight(),
        overscanLines: this.getOverscanLines(),
        ...viewportHeight === void 0 ? {} : { viewportHeight }
      });
      const fromLine = geometry.compressed ? Math.max(
        lineWindow.fromLine,
        this.lineAtTop(logicalTop - scrollTop) + (this.lineTop(this.lineAtTop(logicalTop - scrollTop)) < logicalTop - scrollTop ? 1 : 0)
      ) : lineWindow.fromLine;
      const expanded = this.documentPresentation?.expandLineWindow(fromLine, lineWindow.toLine) ?? {
        fromLine,
        toLine: lineWindow.toLine
      };
      const id2 = this.nextRequestId++;
      return Object.freeze({
        id: id2,
        fromLine: expanded.fromLine,
        toLine: expanded.toLine,
        ...geometry.compressed ? { spacers: this.lineSpacers(expanded.fromLine, expanded.toLine, scrollTop) } : {}
      });
    }
    createRenderRequestForLine(lineNumber) {
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry();
      if (geometry.compressed)
        return this.createRenderRequest(
          geometry.toPhysical(this.lineTop(lineNumber) + this.contentPadding().top)
        );
      const lineHeight = this.getLineHeight();
      const visibleLines = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.ceil((this.getViewportHeight() ?? lineHeight) / lineHeight)
      );
      const overscan = this.getOverscanLines();
      const fromLine = Math.max(1, lineNumber - overscan);
      const toLine = Math.min(
        this.currentSource.lineCount,
        Math.max(lineNumber, fromLine + visibleLines + overscan * 2 - 1)
      );
      const id2 = this.nextRequestId;
      this.nextRequestId += 1;
      return Object.freeze({
        id: id2,
        ...this.documentPresentation?.expandLineWindow(fromLine, toLine) ?? { fromLine, toLine }
      });
    }
    getOverscanLines() {
      const configured = this.virtualViewport.overscanLines ?? defaultOverscanLines;
      const window = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      const averageLength = window ? window.text.length / Math.max(1, window.lines.length) : (this.currentSource.length ?? 0) / Math.max(1, this.currentSource.lineCount);
      return Math.min(configured, Math.max(2, Math.floor(16384 / Math.max(1, averageLength))));
    }
    getLineHeight() {
      return this.presentation ? this.presentation.fontSize * this.presentation.lineHeight : Math.max(1, this.virtualViewport.lineHeight ?? defaultLineHeight);
    }
    getViewportHeight() {
      return this.virtualViewport.viewportHeight ?? (this.dom.clientHeight > 0 ? this.dom.clientHeight : void 0);
    }
    async renderSourceLineWindow(request) {
      const source = this.currentSource;
      const version = source.version;
      const versionKey = `${source.documentId}:${version}`;
      if (this.sourceWindowCacheKey !== versionKey) {
        this.sourceWindowCache.clear();
        this.sourceWindowCacheLength = 0;
        this.sourceWindowCacheKey = versionKey;
      }
      const key = `${request.fromLine}:${request.toLine}`;
      let window = this.sourceWindowCache.get(key);
      if (window) {
        this.sourceWindowCache.delete(key);
        this.sourceWindowCache.set(key, window);
      } else {
        window = await source.readLineWindow(request.fromLine, request.toLine);
        if (source === this.currentSource && version === source.version && versionKey === this.sourceWindowCacheKey && window.text.length <= 524288) {
          const previous = this.sourceWindowCache.get(key);
          this.sourceWindowCacheLength += window.text.length - (previous?.text.length ?? 0);
          this.sourceWindowCache.set(key, window);
          while (this.sourceWindowCache.size > 16 || this.sourceWindowCacheLength > 524288) {
            const oldest = this.sourceWindowCache.keys().next().value;
            this.sourceWindowCacheLength -= this.sourceWindowCache.get(oldest).text.length;
            this.sourceWindowCache.delete(oldest);
          }
        }
      }
      return Object.freeze({
        lines: Object.freeze(window.lines.map((line) => renderSourceLine(this.ownerDocument, line))),
        sourceWindow: window
      });
    }
    async renderLiveLineWindow(request) {
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version;
      const revision = this.presentationReadRevision;
      const fromLine = Math.max(1, request.fromLine - this.markdownContextLines);
      const toLine = Math.min(
        this.currentSource.lineCount,
        request.toLine + this.markdownContextLines
      );
      let parsed = await this.parseLiveWindow(fromLine, toLine);
      const current = () => !this.destroyed && this.mode === "live" && source === this.currentSource && version === source.version && revision === this.presentationReadRevision;
      if (current() && this.documentPresentationEnabled)
        parsed = await this.readPresentationFenceWindow(parsed, request, source, version, revision);
      const blocks = parsed.root.children ?? [];
      let renderedFrom = request.fromLine, renderedTo = request.toLine;
      if (current() && this.documentPresentationEnabled) {
        for (const block of blocks) {
          if (block.type !== "fencedCode" || block.status !== "valid") continue;
          const closing = block.markerRanges?.at(-1);
          if (!closing || (block.markerRanges?.length ?? 0) < 2) continue;
          const start = parsed.window.lines.find((line) => line.from === block.from);
          const end = parsed.window.lines.find(
            (line) => line.from <= closing.to && line.to >= closing.to
          );
          if (!start || !end || start.number > request.toLine || end.number < request.fromLine)
            continue;
          const first = parsed.window.lines.find(
            (line) => line.number === Math.min(renderedFrom, start.number)
          );
          const last = parsed.window.lines.find(
            (line) => line.number === Math.max(renderedTo, end.number)
          );
          if (!first || !last || last.number - first.number + 1 > 256 || last.to - first.from > 65536)
            continue;
          renderedFrom = first.number;
          renderedTo = last.number;
        }
      }
      return Object.freeze({
        lines: Object.freeze(
          parsed.window.lines.filter((line) => line.number >= renderedFrom && line.number <= renderedTo).map(
            (line) => renderLiveLine(this.ownerDocument, line, blocks, {
              cursorPosition: this.cursorPosition,
              selection: this.currentSelection()
            })
          )
        ),
        sourceWindow: parsed.window,
        tableStarts: blocks.filter((node3) => node3.type === "table").map((node3) => node3.from),
        ...renderedFrom !== request.fromLine || renderedTo !== request.toLine ? { presentationRange: { fromLine: renderedFrom, toLine: renderedTo } } : {}
      });
    }
    async readPresentationFenceWindow(base, request, source, version, revision) {
      const candidate = base.root.children?.find(
        (block) => block.type === "fencedCode" && block.status === "incomplete"
      );
      const opening = candidate && base.window.lines.find((line) => line.from === candidate.from);
      if (!candidate || !opening || opening.number > request.toLine) return base;
      const first = Math.min(request.fromLine, opening.number);
      const last = Math.min(source.lineCount, first + 255);
      if (last <= base.window.toLine || base.window.text.length > 65536) return base;
      const key = `${source.documentId}:${version}:${revision}:${first}:${last}:${candidate.from}`;
      let pending = this.presentationFenceReads.get(key);
      if (!pending) {
        pending = (async () => {
          try {
            const parsed2 = await parseMarkdownWindow(
              {
                documentId: source.documentId,
                version,
                readLineWindow: (from, to) => source.readLineWindow(from, to, 65536)
              },
              { fromLine: first, toLine: last }
            );
            if (parsed2.window.text.length > 65536 || parsed2.window.lines.length > 256)
              return void 0;
            const closed = parsed2.root.children?.some(
              (block) => block.type === "fencedCode" && block.status === "valid" && block.from === candidate.from
            );
            return closed ? parsed2 : void 0;
          } catch {
            return void 0;
          }
        })();
        this.presentationFenceReads.set(key, pending);
        while (this.presentationFenceReads.size > this.markdownCacheSize) {
          const oldest = this.presentationFenceReads.keys().next().value;
          if (oldest === void 0) break;
          this.presentationFenceReads.delete(oldest);
        }
      }
      const parsed = await pending;
      return parsed && !this.destroyed && this.mode === "live" && source === this.currentSource && version === source.version && revision === this.presentationReadRevision && this.documentPresentationEnabled && parsed.window.fromLine <= request.fromLine && parsed.window.toLine >= request.toLine ? parsed : base;
    }
    async parseLiveWindow(fromLine, toLine) {
      const key = [this.currentSource.documentId, this.currentSource.version, fromLine, toLine].join(
        ":"
      );
      const cached = this.markdownCache.get(key);
      if (cached) {
        return cached;
      }
      const parsed = this.parseCompleteTableWindow(fromLine, toLine);
      this.markdownCache.set(key, parsed);
      this.trimMarkdownCache();
      try {
        return await parsed;
      } catch (error) {
        this.markdownCache.delete(key);
        throw error;
      }
    }
    async parseCompleteTableWindow(fromLine, toLine) {
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version;
      const base = await parseMarkdownWindow(source, { fromLine, toLine });
      const starts = [];
      let first = fromLine, last = toLine, previousPipe = false;
      for (const line of base.window.lines) {
        const pipe = pipeLine(line.text);
        if (pipe && !previousPipe) {
          const window = await this.tableReader.read(source, line.number);
          if (source !== this.currentSource || source.version !== version) return base;
          if (window) {
            const context = await this.blockContext(line.from, 262144, false).catch((error) => {
              if (String(error).includes("text budget")) return void 0;
              throw error;
            });
            if (context && !context.code) {
              const parsed2 = await parseMarkdownWindow(
                { documentId: source.documentId, version, readLineWindow: async () => window },
                { fromLine: window.fromLine, toLine: window.toLine }
              );
              starts.push(
                ...(parsed2.root.children ?? []).filter((n) => n.type === "table").map((n) => n.from)
              );
              first = Math.min(first, window.fromLine);
              last = Math.max(last, window.toLine);
            }
          }
        }
        previousPipe = pipe;
      }
      const parsed = first === fromLine && last === toLine ? base : await parseMarkdownWindow(
        {
          documentId: source.documentId,
          version,
          readLineWindow: (from, to) => source.readLineWindow(from, to, base.window.text.length + 1048576)
        },
        { fromLine: first, toLine: last }
      );
      return {
        ...parsed,
        root: {
          ...parsed.root,
          children: (parsed.root.children ?? []).filter(
            (node3) => node3.type !== "table" || starts.includes(node3.from)
          )
        }
      };
    }
    localTable() {
      const window = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      const range2 = this.currentSelection().main;
      if (this.mode !== "live" || !window || range2.from < window.from || range2.to > window.to || !this.tableStarts.length)
        return;
      const table = boundedTableState(window, this.currentSelection());
      return table && this.tableStarts.includes(table.context.table.from + table.offset) ? table : void 0;
    }
    get activeTable() {
      return this.localTable()?.context;
    }
    runTableCommand(command) {
      return this.trackVisibleEditTask(async () => {
        if (this.mode === "live") return this.applyTableCommand(command);
        if (!this.editable || this.composing) return;
        const revision = this.selectionRevision;
        const context = await this.readEditingContext();
