        if (!context.table || !this.editable || this.composing || this.selectionRevision !== revision)
          return;
        const table = context.table;
        const text2 = table.text + (this.editingContextCache?.window.text.slice(table.to) ?? "");
        const state = new EditorState({
          doc: new MemoryTextDocument(text2),
          selection: Selection.range(
            table.selection.anchor - table.from,
            table.selection.head - table.from
          )
        });
        const local = boundedTableState(
          {
            from: table.from,
            to: table.from + text2.length,
            fromLine: 1,
            toLine: state.doc.lineCount,
            text: text2,
            lines: []
          },
          this.currentSelection()
        );
        if (!local) return;
        const transaction = tableCommand(local.state, local.context, command);
        if (transaction) await this.applyLocalTableTransaction(transaction, table.from);
      });
    }
    /** No edit queue is entered here: a host may call this inside runAfterPendingEdits. */
    async readEditingContext(selection = this.currentSelection().main, signal) {
      signal?.throwIfAborted();
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version;
      const unknown = { kind: "unknown", complete: false };
      const current = () => !this.destroyed && this.currentSource === source && source.version === version;
      try {
        let cached = this.editingContextCache;
        if (!cached || cached.source !== source || cached.version !== version) {
          const window2 = await source.readLineWindow(
            1,
            Math.min(source.lineCount, editingContextLimits.lines),
            editingContextLimits.text
          );
          signal?.throwIfAborted();
          if (!current()) return unknown;
          const state2 = new EditorState({ doc: new MemoryTextDocument(window2.text) });
          cached = { source, version, window: window2, state: state2, root: parseMarkdown(window2.text).root };
          this.editingContextCache = cached;
        }
        const { window } = cached;
        signal?.throwIfAborted();
        if (!current() || Math.max(selection.anchor, selection.head) > window.to) return unknown;
        const state = cached.state.applyTransaction({
          selection: Selection.range(selection.anchor, selection.head),
          addToHistory: false
        });
        const context = editingContextFromState(state, cached.root);
        if (context.table && window.toLine < source.lineCount && context.table.to >= window.to)
          return { kind: "table", complete: false };
        return context;
      } catch (error) {
        signal?.throwIfAborted();
        if (error instanceof RangeError && String(error).includes("text budget")) return unknown;
        throw error;
      }
    }
    async applyTableCommand(command) {
      const table = this.localTable();
      if (!table || !this.editable || this.composing) return;
      const transaction = tableCommand(table.state, table.context, command);
      if (transaction) await this.applyLocalTableTransaction(transaction, table.offset);
    }
    async applyLocalTableTransaction(transaction, offset) {
      const selected = transaction.selection;
      const selection = selected ? Selection.range(offset + selected.main.anchor, offset + selected.main.head) : this.currentSelection();
      const changes = transaction.changes?.changes ?? [];
      if (changes.length) {
        if (changes.length !== 1) throw new Error("Table command requires one atomic edit");
        const change = changes[0];
        await this.applyVisibleEdit(
          offset + change.from,
          offset + change.to,
          change.insert,
          "isolate",
          selection
        );
      } else {
        this.cursorPosition = selection.main.head;
        this.selectionAnchor = selection.main.anchor;
        await this.ensureCursorRendered();
        this.renderSelection();
        this.renderCursor();
      }
    }
    async applyTextInput(from, to, text2, historyGroup) {
      const table = this.localTable();
      await this.applyVisibleEdit(
        from,
        to,
        table?.context.withinCell ? tableCellText(
          text2,
          table.state.doc.slice(table.context.cell.from, table.state.selection.main.from)
        ) : text2,
        historyGroup
      );
    }
    async deleteTableText(direction) {
      const table = this.localTable();
      if (!table?.context.withinCell || !table.state.selection.main.empty) return false;
      const position = table.state.selection.main.head, cell = table.context.cell;
      if (position === (direction < 0 ? cell.from : cell.to)) return true;
      const text2 = table.state.doc.slice(
        direction < 0 ? cell.from : position,
        direction < 0 ? position : cell.to
      );
      const atom = direction < 0 ? /(?:<br[ \t]*\/?>|\\\|)$/i.exec(text2)?.[0] : /^(?:<br[ \t]*\/?>|\\\|)/i.exec(text2)?.[0];
      if (!atom) return false;
      const from = table.offset + position - (direction < 0 ? atom.length : 0);
      await this.applyVisibleEdit(from, from + atom.length, "", "isolate");
      return true;
    }
    trimMarkdownCache() {
      while (this.markdownCache.size > this.markdownCacheSize) {
        const oldest = this.markdownCache.keys().next().value;
        if (typeof oldest !== "string") {
          return;
        }
        this.markdownCache.delete(oldest);
      }
    }
    async warmMarkdownCacheAroundVisibleWindow() {
      const request = this.createRenderRequest();
      const ranges = this.createMarkdownWarmupRanges(request);
      const warmed = [];
      for (const range2 of ranges) {
        await this.parseLiveWindow(range2.fromLine, range2.toLine);
        warmed.push(`${range2.fromLine}-${range2.toLine}`);
      }
      return Object.freeze(warmed);
    }
    scheduleMarkdownWarmup(request) {
      this.cancelMarkdownWarmup();
      if (!this.backgroundMarkdownWarmup || this.mode !== "live" || (this.renderedSourceWindow?.text.length ?? 0) > 65536) {
        return;
      }
      this.markdownWarmupTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.markdownWarmupTimer = void 0;
        void this.warmMarkdownCacheForRequest(request).catch(() => void 0);
      }, this.markdownWarmupDelayMs);
    }
    async warmMarkdownCacheForRequest(request) {
      for (const range2 of this.createMarkdownWarmupRanges(request)) {
        await this.parseLiveWindow(range2.fromLine, range2.toLine);
      }
    }
    createMarkdownWarmupRanges(request) {
      const visibleWindowSize = Math.max(1, request.toLine - request.fromLine + 1);
      const ranges = [];
      for (let index = -this.markdownWarmupWindows; index <= this.markdownWarmupWindows; index += 1) {
        const rawFromLine = request.fromLine + index * visibleWindowSize;
        const rawToLine = request.toLine + index * visibleWindowSize;
        const fromLine = Math.max(1, rawFromLine - this.markdownContextLines);
        const toLine = Math.min(this.currentSource.lineCount, rawToLine + this.markdownContextLines);
        if (toLine < 1 || fromLine > this.currentSource.lineCount || toLine < fromLine) {
          continue;
        }
        const key = `${fromLine}-${toLine}`;
        if (!ranges.some((range2) => `${range2.fromLine}-${range2.toLine}` === key)) {
          ranges.push(Object.freeze({ fromLine, toLine }));
        }
      }
      return Object.freeze(ranges);
    }
    cancelMarkdownWarmup() {
      if (this.markdownWarmupTimer === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      clearTimeout(this.markdownWarmupTimer);
      this.markdownWarmupTimer = void 0;
    }
    async readInputProxy() {
      if (!this.editable) {
        this.inputDOM.value = "";
        return;
      }
      if (this.composing) {
        return;
      }
      const text2 = this.inputDOM.value;
      if (text2.length === 0) {
        return;
      }
      this.inputDOM.value = "";
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      await this.applyTextInput(range2.from, range2.to, text2);
    }
    async commitComposition(data) {
      if (!this.editable || data.length === 0) {
        return;
      }
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      await this.applyTextInput(range2.from, range2.to, data, "isolate");
    }
    handleEditingKey(event) {
      if (isCompositionKeyEvent(event)) {
        return false;
      }
      if (!this.editable) {
        return false;
      }
      if (!event.altKey && (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && (event.key === "Home" || event.key === "End")) {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(async () => {
          await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
          const number2 = event.key === "Home" ? 1 : this.currentSource.lineCount;
          const line = await this.readSourceLine(number2);
          const head = event.key === "Home" ? line.from : line.to;
          await this.revealSelection(
            number2,
            Selection.range(event.shiftKey ? this.selectionAnchor : head, head)
          );
        });
        return true;
      }
      if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
        return false;
      }
      if (event.key === "PageUp" || event.key === "PageDown") {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(
          () => this.movePage(event.key === "PageUp" ? -1 : 1, event.shiftKey)
        );
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key.length === 1) {
        if (this.composing) {
          return false;
        }
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => {
          const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
          return this.applyTextInput(range2.from, range2.to, event.key);
        });
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key === "Escape" && this.activeTable) {
        void this.runTableCommand("exit");
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key === "Enter") {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(async () => {
          const table = this.localTable();
          if (table) {
            if (table.context.withinCell && event.shiftKey) {
              const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
              await this.applyVisibleEdit(range2.from, range2.to, "<br>", "isolate");
            } else await this.applyTableCommand("down");
          } else await this.enterBlock();
        });
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key === "Tab" || event.key === "Unidentified" && event.code === "Tab") {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(
          () => this.localTable() ? this.applyTableCommand(event.shiftKey ? "previous" : "next") : this.indentBlock(event.shiftKey)
        );
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key === "Backspace") {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.backspaceBlock());
        return true;
      }
      if (event.key === "Delete") {
        void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.deleteAroundCursor(1));
        return true;
      }
      switch (event.key) {
        case "Home":
        case "End":
          void this.trackVisibleEditTask(async () => {
            await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
            const documentId = this.currentSource.documentId;
            const line = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition);
            if (this.destroyed || documentId !== this.currentSource.documentId) return;
            const table = this.localTable();
            this.cursorPosition = table?.context.withinCell ? table.offset + (event.key === "End" ? table.context.cell.to : table.context.cell.from) : event.key === "End" ? line.to : line.from;
            if (!event.shiftKey) this.selectionAnchor = this.cursorPosition;
            await this.ensureCursorRendered(line.number);
            this.renderSelection();
            this.renderCursor();
          });
          return true;
        case "ArrowLeft":
          void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.moveCursor("left", event.shiftKey));
          return true;
        case "ArrowRight":
          void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.moveCursor("right", event.shiftKey));
          return true;
        case "ArrowUp":
          void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.moveCursor("up", event.shiftKey));
          return true;
        case "ArrowDown":
          void this.trackVisibleEditTask(() => this.moveCursor("down", event.shiftKey));
          return true;
        default:
          return false;
      }
    }
    async movePage(direction, extend) {
      await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
      const current = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition);
      const height = this.getLineHeight();
      const delta = direction * Math.max(1, Math.floor((this.getViewportHeight() ?? height * 24) / height) - 1);
      const target = await this.readSourceLine(
        clamp4(current.number + delta, 1, this.currentSource.lineCount)
      );
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry();
      const logicalTop = geometry.compressed ? geometry.toLogical(this.dom.scrollTop) - this.contentPadding().top : this.dom.scrollTop;
      const first = this.lineAtTop(logicalTop);
      this.cursorPosition = target.from + snapGraphemeOffset(target.text, this.cursorPosition - current.from);
      if (!extend) this.selectionAnchor = this.cursorPosition;
      await this.scrollToLine(clamp4(first + delta, 1, this.currentSource.lineCount));
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
    }
    async moveCursor(direction, extend) {
      const table = this.localTable();
      if (table?.context.withinCell) {
        await this.applyLocalTableTransaction(
          {
            selection: tableArrowSelection(table.state, table.context, direction, extend),
            addToHistory: false
          },
          table.offset
        );
        return;
      }
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      const current = this.cursorPosition;
      const previous = current;
      let next = current;
      let targetLineNumber;
      if (!extend && range2.from !== range2.to) {
        next = direction === "right" || direction === "down" ? range2.to : range2.from;
      } else if (direction === "left" || direction === "right") {
        const move = await this.horizontalCursorTarget(current, direction === "left" ? -1 : 1);
        next = move.position;
        targetLineNumber = move.lineNumber;
      } else {
        const verticalMove = await this.moveCursorVertically(current, direction);
        next = verticalMove.position;
        targetLineNumber = verticalMove.lineNumber;
      }
      if (next === current && (extend || range2.from === range2.to)) {
        return;
      }
      this.cursorPosition = next;
      if (!extend) {
        this.selectionAnchor = next;
      }
      await this.ensureCursorRendered(targetLineNumber);
      await this.renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previous, next);
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
      this.inputDOM.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    async moveCursorVertically(pos, direction) {
      const line = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(pos) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(pos).catch(() => void 0);
      if (!line) {
        return { position: pos };
      }
      const targetLineNumber = direction === "up" ? line.number - 1 : line.number + 1;
      if (targetLineNumber < 1 || targetLineNumber > this.currentSource.lineCount) {
        return { position: pos, lineNumber: line.number };
      }
      const target = await this.readSourceLine(targetLineNumber);
      return {
        position: target.from + snapGraphemeOffset(target.text, pos - line.from),
        lineNumber: targetLineNumber
      };
    }
    async readSourceLine(number2) {
      const cached = this.renderedSourceWindow?.lines.find((line2) => line2.number === number2);
      if (cached) return cached;
      await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
      const window = await this.currentSource.readLineWindow(number2, number2);
      const line = window.lines[0];
      if (!line) throw new RangeError(`Missing source line: ${number2}`);
      return line;
    }
    async horizontalCursorTarget(position, direction) {
      const line = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(position) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(position).catch(() => void 0);
      if (!line) return { position };
      const offset = position - line.from;
      if (direction < 0 && offset > 0) {
        return {
          position: line.from + previousGraphemeOffset(line.text, offset),
          lineNumber: line.number
        };
      }
      if (direction > 0 && position < line.to) {
        return {
          position: line.from + nextGraphemeOffset(line.text, offset),
          lineNumber: line.number
        };
      }
      const adjacentNumber = line.number + direction;
      if (adjacentNumber < 1 || adjacentNumber > this.currentSource.lineCount) {
        return { position, lineNumber: line.number };
      }
      const adjacent = await this.readSourceLine(adjacentNumber);
      return { position: direction < 0 ? adjacent.to : adjacent.from, lineNumber: adjacent.number };
    }
    revealHorizontalCursor() {
      const line = Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line")).find(
        (line2) => this.cursorPosition >= Number(line2.dataset.from) && this.cursorPosition <= Number(line2.dataset.to)
      );
      if (line && (this.mode === "source" ? scrollSourcePositionIntoView(this.dom, line, this.cursorPosition) : scrollCodePositionIntoView(line, this.cursorPosition))) {
        this.renderSelection();
        this.renderSearchHighlights();
        this.renderCursor();
      }
    }
    async ensureCursorRendered(targetLineNumber) {
      this.revealHorizontalCursor();
      const line = this.findRenderedLineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(this.cursorPosition).catch(() => void 0);
      const lineNumber = line?.number ?? targetLineNumber;
      if (lineNumber === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      const fromLine = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.fromLine);
      const toLine = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.toLine);
      if (Number.isInteger(fromLine) && Number.isInteger(toLine) && lineNumber >= fromLine && lineNumber <= toLine) {
        const element = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
          `.milkup-line[data-line="${lineNumber}"]`
        );
        const bounds = element?.getBoundingClientRect();
        const viewport = this.dom.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (!bounds || viewport.height === 0 || bounds.top >= viewport.top && bounds.bottom <= viewport.bottom)
          return;
      }
      const lineHeight = this.getLineHeight();
      const viewportHeight = this.getViewportHeight() ?? lineHeight;
      const visibleLines = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewportHeight / lineHeight));
      const nextFirstLine = clamp4(
        lineNumber - Math.floor(visibleLines / 2),
        1,
        this.currentSource.lineCount
      );
      await this.scrollToLine(nextFirstLine);
      const target = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
        `.milkup-line[data-line="${lineNumber}"]`
      );
      if (target && this.dom.clientHeight > 0) {
        const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect(), viewport = this.dom.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (bounds.top < viewport.top || bounds.bottom > viewport.bottom)
          await this.scrollToLine(lineNumber);
      }
      this.revealHorizontalCursor();
    }
    findRenderedLineAtPosition(position) {
      for (const lineDOM of Array.from(
        this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line")
      )) {
        const number2 = Number(lineDOM.dataset.line);
        const from = Number(lineDOM.dataset.from);
        const to = Number(lineDOM.dataset.to);
        if (!Number.isInteger(number2) || !Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to) || position < from || position > to) {
          continue;
        }
        return Object.freeze({
          number: number2,
          from,
          to,
          text: lineDOM.textContent?.replace(/\u200b/g, "") ?? ""
        });
      }
      return void 0;
    }
    async deleteAroundCursor(direction) {
      if (await this.deleteTableText(direction)) return;
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      if (range2.from !== range2.to) {
        await this.applyVisibleEdit(range2.from, range2.to, "");
        return;
      }
      const position = this.cursorPosition;
      const target = await this.horizontalCursorTarget(position, direction);
      const from = Math.min(position, target.position);
      const to = Math.max(position, target.position);
      if (from === to) {
        return;
      }
      await this.applyVisibleEdit(from, to, "");
    }
    toggleTask(task) {
      if (!this.editable || !this.onEdit || this.composing || this.destroyed || this.mode !== "live" || this.taskTogglePending || this.pendingVisibleEdit || this.activeVisibleEditTasks.size > 0)
        return;
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version, revision = this.editRevision;
      const from = Number(task.dataset.from), to = Number(task.dataset.to);
      const window = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      if (!window || !Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to) || from < window.from || to > window.to)
        return;
      const edit2 = taskMarkerEdit(from, readLineWindowRangeText(window, from, to));
      if (!edit2) return;
      const focused = this.ownerDocument.activeElement === task;
      this.taskTogglePending = true;
      syncTaskAvailability(this.contentDOM, false);
      void this.trackVisibleEditTask(async () => {
        try {
          await this.editCommitQueue;
          if (!this.editable || this.destroyed || this.mode !== "live" || source !== this.currentSource || version !== source.version || revision !== this.editRevision)
            return;
          await this.applyVisibleEdit(
            edit2.from,
            edit2.to,
            edit2.insert,
            "isolate",
            this.currentSelection()
          );
          await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
          await this.renderVisibleWindow();
        } finally {
          this.taskTogglePending = false;
          syncTaskAvailability(this.contentDOM, this.editable && !!this.onEdit);
          if (focused && !this.destroyed && this.currentSource.documentId === source.documentId)
            this.contentDOM.querySelector(`.milkup-task-marker[data-from="${from}"]`)?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        }
      });
    }
    async applyVisibleEdit(from, to, insert, historyGroup, selectionAfter = Selection.cursor(from + insert.length)) {
      if (!this.onEdit) {
        return;
      }
      const editedLine = this.findRenderedLineAtPosition(from);
      const liveTableEdit = !!this.localTable();
      const structuralEdit = insert.includes("\n") || editedLine === void 0 || to > editedLine.to;
      if (structuralEdit) this.measuredHeights.clear();
      const delta = insert.length - (to - from);
      this.tableStarts = this.tableStarts.map(
        (start) => start < from ? start : start >= to ? start + delta : from
      );
      this.editRevision += 1;
      const editRevision = this.editRevision;
      const selectionBefore = this.currentSelection();
      this.cursorPosition = selectionAfter.main.head;
      this.selectionAnchor = selectionAfter.main.anchor;
      const { text: deletedText, lineNumber } = await this.readVisibleEditRange(from, to);
      const edit2 = {
        from,
        to,
        insert,
        deletedText,
        selectionBefore,
        selectionAfter,
        ...historyGroup ? { historyGroup } : {}
      };
      this.cancelMarkdownWarmup();
      if (structuralEdit) {
        const optimistic = this.updateOptimisticSourceWindow(from, to, insert);
        if (optimistic) {
          await this.renderOptimisticSourceWindow(optimistic.window, optimistic.lineCount);
          const commit = this.queueVisibleEditCommit(edit2);
          void commit.then(
            () => this.refreshAfterOptimisticCommit(editRevision),
            (error) => this.rollbackOptimisticEdit(editRevision, error)
          );
        } else {
          await this.queueVisibleEditCommit(edit2, { immediate: true });
          await this.refreshAfterOptimisticCommit(editRevision);
        }
        await this.ensureCursorRendered(lineNumber);
        this.renderCursor();
      } else {
        const optimistic = this.updateOptimisticSourceWindow(from, to, insert);
        if (liveTableEdit && optimistic) {
          const parsed = await parseMarkdownWindow(
            {
              documentId: this.currentSource.documentId,
              version: this.currentSource.version,
              readLineWindow: async () => optimistic.window
            },
            { fromLine: optimistic.window.fromLine, toLine: optimistic.window.toLine }
          );
          const line = optimistic.window.lines.find((line2) => line2.number === editedLine.number);
          this.contentDOM.querySelector(`.milkup-line[data-line="${editedLine.number}"]`)?.replaceWith(
            renderLiveLine(this.ownerDocument, line, parsed.root.children ?? [], {
              selection: this.currentSelection()
            })
          );
        } else this.applyOptimisticRenderedLineEdit(editedLine, from, to, insert);
        this.shiftRenderedLinePositionsAfter(editedLine.number, delta);
        if (liveTableEdit) this.measureTableRows();
        this.revealHorizontalCursor();
        this.renderSelection();
        this.renderCursor();
        const commit = this.queueVisibleEditCommit(edit2);
        if (this.mode === "live") {
          void commit.then(async () => {
            if (editRevision !== this.editRevision) {
              return;
            }
            this.markdownCache.clear();
            await this.renderRenderedLine(editedLine.number);
            this.measureTableRows();
            this.renderSelection();
            this.renderCursor();
          }).catch((error) => this.rollbackOptimisticEdit(editRevision, error));
        }
      }
      this.inputDOM.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
    updateOptimisticSourceWindow(from, to, insert) {
      const current = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      if (!current || from < current.from || to > current.to) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const text2 = current.text.slice(0, from - current.from) + insert + current.text.slice(to - current.from);
      const document = new MemoryTextDocument(text2);
      const lines = Array.from({ length: document.lineCount }, (_, index) => {
        const line = document.line(index + 1);
        return Object.freeze({
          number: current.fromLine + index,
          from: current.from + line.from,
          to: current.from + line.to,
          text: line.text
        });
      });
      const lineDelta = lines.length - current.lines.length;
      const last = lines.at(-1);
      const window = Object.freeze({
        fromLine: current.fromLine,
        toLine: current.fromLine + lines.length - 1,
        from: current.from,
        to: last?.to ?? current.from,
        text: text2,
        lines: Object.freeze(lines)
      });
      this.renderedSourceWindow = window;
      this.renderedDocumentVersion = void 0;
      return Object.freeze({
        window,
        lineCount: this.currentSource.lineCount + lineDelta
      });
    }
    async renderOptimisticSourceWindow(window, lineCount) {
      let lines;
      if (this.mode === "live") {
        const parsed = await parseMarkdownWindow(
          {
            documentId: `${this.currentSource.documentId}:optimistic`,
            version: this.currentSource.version + this.editRevision,
            readLineWindow: async () => window
          },
          { fromLine: window.fromLine, toLine: window.toLine }
        );
        const blocks = (parsed.root.children ?? []).filter(
          (node3) => node3.type !== "table" || this.tableStarts.includes(node3.from)
        );
        lines = parsed.window.lines.map(
          (line) => renderLiveLine(this.ownerDocument, line, blocks, {
            cursorPosition: this.cursorPosition,
            selection: this.currentSelection()
          })
        );
      } else {
        lines = window.lines.map((line) => renderSourceLine(this.ownerDocument, line));
      }
      const lineHeight = this.getLineHeight();
      const geometry = this.scrollGeometry(lineCount);
      const spacers = geometry.compressed ? geometry.spacers(window.fromLine, window.toLine, this.dom.scrollTop) : void 0;
      this.latestAppliedRequestId = this.nextRequestId;
      this.nextRequestId += 1;
      this.contentDOM.replaceChildren(
        renderSpacer(this.ownerDocument, "top", spacers?.top ?? (window.fromLine - 1) * lineHeight),
        ...lines,
        renderSpacer(
          this.ownerDocument,
          "bottom",
          spacers?.bottom ?? (lineCount - window.toLine) * lineHeight
        )
      );
      this.contentDOM.dataset.virtualized = "true";
      this.contentDOM.dataset.fromLine = String(window.fromLine);
      this.contentDOM.dataset.toLine = String(window.toLine);
      this.contentDOM.dataset.renderMode = this.mode;
      this.measureTableRows();
      this.renderSearchHighlights();
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
    }
    async refreshAfterOptimisticCommit(editRevision) {
      if (editRevision !== this.editRevision) {
        return;
      }
      this.markdownCache.clear();
      this.sourceWindowCache.clear();
      this.sourceWindowCacheLength = 0;
      try {
        await this.renderVisibleWindow();
      } catch {
      }
    }
    async rollbackOptimisticEdit(editRevision, error) {
      this.visibleEditPreparationError = error;
      if (editRevision !== this.editRevision) {
        return;
      }
      this.markdownCache.clear();
      this.renderedSourceWindow = void 0;
      await this.renderVisibleWindow().catch(() => void 0);
    }
    enqueueVisibleEdit(task) {
      const queued = this.visibleEditQueue.then(task);
      this.visibleEditQueue = queued.catch(() => void 0);
      return queued;
    }
    trackVisibleEditTask(task) {
      const documentId = this.currentSource.documentId;
      const tracked = this.enqueueVisibleEdit(async () => {
        if (!this.destroyed && documentId === this.currentSource.documentId) await task();
      }).catch((error) => {
        this.visibleEditPreparationError = error;
      });
      this.activeVisibleEditTasks.add(tracked);
      void tracked.then(() => this.activeVisibleEditTasks.delete(tracked));
      return tracked;
    }
    queueVisibleEditCommit(edit2, options = {}) {
      if (options.immediate) {
        return this.flushPendingVisibleEdit().then(() => this.commitVisibleEdit(edit2));
      }
      const mergedEdit = this.pendingVisibleEdit ? mergeSourceDocumentEdits(this.pendingVisibleEdit, edit2) : edit2;
      if (this.pendingVisibleEdit && !mergedEdit) {
        void this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
        return this.queueVisibleEditCommit(edit2);
      }
      this.pendingVisibleEdit = mergedEdit;
      const queued = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        this.pendingVisibleEditResolutions.push({ resolve, reject });
      });
      this.schedulePendingVisibleEditCommit();
      return queued;
    }
    schedulePendingVisibleEditCommit() {
      if (this.editCommitTimer !== void 0) {
        clearTimeout(this.editCommitTimer);
      }
      this.editCommitTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.editCommitTimer = void 0;
        void this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
      }, defaultEditCommitDelayMs);
    }
    flushPendingVisibleEdit() {
      if (this.editCommitTimer !== void 0) {
        clearTimeout(this.editCommitTimer);
        this.editCommitTimer = void 0;
      }
      const edit2 = this.pendingVisibleEdit;
      const resolutions = this.pendingVisibleEditResolutions.splice(
        0,
        this.pendingVisibleEditResolutions.length
      );
      this.pendingVisibleEdit = void 0;
      if (!edit2) {
        return this.editCommitQueue;
      }
      const commit = this.commitVisibleEdit(edit2);
      commit.then(
        () => resolutions.forEach((resolution) => resolution.resolve()),
        (error) => resolutions.forEach((resolution) => resolution.reject(error))
      );
      return commit;
    }
    commitVisibleEdit(edit2) {
      this.editCommitQueue = this.editCommitQueue.catch(() => void 0).then(async () => {
        if (!this.onEdit) {
          return;
        }
        await this.onEdit(edit2);
      });
      return this.editCommitQueue;
    }
    async readVisibleEditRange(from, to) {
      if (from === to) {
        return { text: "", lineNumber: this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(from)?.number };
      }
      const readWindow = (window2) => ({
        text: readLineWindowRangeText(window2, from, to),
        lineNumber: window2.lines.find((line) => from >= line.from && from <= line.to)?.number
      });
      const rendered = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      if (rendered && from >= rendered.from && to <= rendered.to) {
        return readWindow(rendered);
      }
      const fromLine = await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(from).catch(() => void 0);
      const toLine = await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(to).catch(() => void 0);
      if (fromLine && toLine) {
        const window2 = await this.currentSource.readLineWindow(fromLine.number, toLine.number);
        return readWindow(window2);
      }
      const renderedFromLine = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.fromLine);
      const renderedToLine = Number(this.contentDOM.dataset.toLine);
      if (!Number.isInteger(renderedFromLine) || !Number.isInteger(renderedToLine)) {
        throw new RangeError("Visible edit range is outside the rendered document window");
      }
      await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
      const window = await this.currentSource.readLineWindow(
        Math.max(1, renderedFromLine - (rendered && from < rendered.from ? 1 : 0)),
        Math.min(
          this.currentSource.lineCount,
          renderedToLine + (rendered && to > rendered.to ? 1 : 0)
        )
      );
      if (from < window.from || to > window.to) {
        throw new RangeError("Visible edit range is outside the rendered document window");
      }
      return readWindow(window);
    }
    startSelectionDrag(event) {
      if (event.button !== 0) {
        return;
      }
      if (isHorizontalScrollbarDragStart(event) || isEditorScrollbarDragStart(event, this.dom)) {
        return;
      }
      const position = this.positionFromLineEvent(event);
      if (position === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      const previousPosition = this.cursorPosition;
      this.dragAnchor = position;
      this.isDraggingSelection = true;
      this.hasDraggedSelection = false;
      this.cursorPosition = position;
      this.selectionAnchor = event.shiftKey ? this.selectionAnchor : position;
      if (this.mode === "live") {
        void this.renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previousPosition, this.cursorPosition);
      }
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
      this.inputDOM.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      event.preventDefault();
    }
    async updateSelectionDrag(event) {
      if (!this.isDraggingSelection || this.dragAnchor === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      if (event.buttons !== void 0 && (event.buttons & 1) === 0) {
        this.clearSelectionDrag();
        return;
      }
      const position = this.positionFromLineEvent(event);
      if (position === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      if (position === this.dragAnchor && !this.hasDraggedSelection) {
        event.preventDefault();
        return;
      }
      const previousPosition = this.cursorPosition;
      this.hasDraggedSelection = true;
      this.selectionAnchor = this.dragAnchor;
      this.cursorPosition = position;
      if (this.mode === "live") {
        await this.renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previousPosition, this.cursorPosition);
      }
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
      event.preventDefault();
    }
    async finishSelectionDrag(event) {
      if (!this.isDraggingSelection) {
        return;
      }
      this.clearSelectionDrag();
      event.preventDefault();
    }
    clearSelectionDrag() {
      this.isDraggingSelection = false;
      this.hasDraggedSelection = false;
      this.dragAnchor = void 0;
    }
    positionFromLineEvent(event) {
      if (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest("input, textarea, select, button")) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const target = lineElementFromEvent(event, this.contentDOM, this.inputDOM);
      if (!target) {
        return this.positionFromRenderedWindowEvent(event);
      }
      const from = Number(target.dataset.from);
      const to = Number(target.dataset.to);
      if (!Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to)) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const position = sourcePositionFromPoint(target, event, this.inputDOM, { allowGeometryFallback: true }) ?? from;
      return this.normalizeRenderedClickPosition(clamp4(position, from, to));
    }
    async moveCursorFromPointer(event) {
      const position = this.positionFromLineEvent(event);
      if (position === void 0) {
        return;
      }
      const previousPosition = this.cursorPosition;
      this.cursorPosition = position;
      if (!event.shiftKey) {
        this.selectionAnchor = this.cursorPosition;
      }
      if (this.mode === "live") {
        await this.renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previousPosition, this.cursorPosition);
      }
      this.renderSelection();
      this.renderCursor();
      this.inputDOM.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      event.preventDefault();
    }
    positionFromRenderedWindowEvent(event) {
      const target = event.target;
      if (!(target instanceof Node) || !this.dom.contains(target)) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const lines = Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line"));
      if (lines.length === 0) {
        return void 0;
      }
      let nearest;
      for (const line of lines) {
        const rect = line.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (event.clientY >= rect.top && event.clientY <= rect.bottom) {
          return this.normalizePositionFromLine(line, event);
        }
        const distance = event.clientY < rect.top ? rect.top - event.clientY : event.clientY - rect.bottom;
        if (!nearest || distance < nearest.distance) {
          nearest = { line, distance };
        }
      }
      return nearest ? this.normalizePositionFromLine(nearest.line, event) : void 0;
    }
    normalizePositionFromLine(line, event) {
      const from = Number(line.dataset.from);
      const to = Number(line.dataset.to);
      if (!Number.isInteger(from) || !Number.isInteger(to)) {
        return void 0;
      }
      const rect = line.getBoundingClientRect();
      const position = rect.width > 0 && event.clientX <= rect.left ? from : rect.width > 0 && event.clientX >= rect.right ? to : sourcePositionFromPoint(line, event, this.inputDOM, { allowGeometryFallback: true }) ?? to;
      return this.normalizeRenderedClickPosition(clamp4(position, from, to));
    }
    normalizeRenderedClickPosition(position) {
      if (this.mode === "source") {
        return position;
      }
      const line = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(position);
      const contentStart = line ? listSyntaxContentStart(line.text, line.from) : void 0;
      if (contentStart === void 0 || position >= contentStart) {
        return position;
      }
      return clamp4(contentStart, line?.from ?? position, line?.to ?? position);
    }
    async blockContext(position, maxTextLength, flush = true) {
      if (flush) await this.flushPendingVisibleEdit();
      const source = this.currentSource;
      const version = source.version;
      const current = () => !this.destroyed && this.currentSource.documentId === source.documentId && this.currentSource.version === version;
      const line = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(position) ?? await source.lineAtPosition(position);
      if (!current()) return void 0;
      const key = `${source.documentId}:${version}`;
      if (key !== this.fenceContextKey) {
        this.fenceContextKey = key;
        this.fenceCheckpoints.clear();
        this.fenceCheckpoints.set(1, void 0);
      }
      let from = 1;
      for (const checkpoint of this.fenceCheckpoints.keys())
        if (checkpoint <= line.number && checkpoint > from) from = checkpoint;
      let fence = this.fenceCheckpoints.get(from);
      let code = false, closing = false;
      for (; from <= line.number; from += 512) {
        const window = await this.readBlockWindow(
          from,
          Math.min(line.number, from + 511),
          maxTextLength
        );
        if (!current()) return void 0;
        for (const item of window.lines) {
          const marker = /^( {0,3})(`{3,}|~{3,})(.*)$/.exec(item.text);
          const ends = !!fence && !!marker && marker[2][0] === fence[0] && marker[2].length >= fence.length && /^[ \t]*$/.test(marker[3]);
          if (item.number === line.number) {
            code = !!fence || !!marker || /^(?: {4}|\t)/.test(item.text);
            closing = ends;
          }
          if (ends) fence = void 0;
          else if (!fence && marker) fence = marker[2];
        }
        if (window.toLine < line.number) this.fenceCheckpoints.set(window.toLine + 1, fence);
      }
      const adjacent = await this.readBlockWindow(
        Math.max(1, line.number - 1),
        Math.min(source.lineCount, line.number + 1),
        maxTextLength
      );
      const newline = adjacent.text.includes("\r\n") ? "\r\n" : "\n";
      return current() ? { line, code, closing, newline, current } : void 0;
    }
    async readBlockWindow(fromLine, toLine, maxTextLength) {
      const cached = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      if (cached && fromLine >= cached.fromLine && toLine <= cached.toLine) {
        const lines = cached.lines.filter((line) => line.number >= fromLine && line.number <= toLine);
        const from = lines[0].from, to = lines.at(-1).to;
        if (maxTextLength !== void 0 && to - from > maxTextLength)
          throw new RangeError("Line window exceeds text budget");
        return { fromLine, toLine, from, to, lines, text: readLineWindowRangeText(cached, from, to) };
      }
      return this.currentSource.readLineWindow(fromLine, toLine, maxTextLength);
    }
    async enterBlock() {
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      const context = await this.blockContext(range2.from);
      if (!context) return;
      const { line, code, closing, newline } = context;
      const prefix = listEditingPrefix(line.text);
      if (!code && prefix && range2.from >= line.from + prefix[0].length && range2.to <= line.to) {
        if (range2.from === range2.to && line.text.slice(prefix[0].length).trim() === "") {
          const remove = prefix[1] ? indentRemovalLength(prefix[1]) : line.text.length;
          const changes = ChangeSet.delete(line.from, line.from + remove);
          await this.applyVisibleEdit(
            line.from,
            line.from + remove,
            "",
            "isolate",
            this.currentSelection().map(changes)
          );
          return;
        }
        await this.applyVisibleEdit(
          range2.from,
          range2.to,
          newline + (listContinuationPrefix(line.text) ?? ""),
          "isolate"
        );
        return;
      }
      const indent = code && !closing ? /^[ \t]*/.exec(line.text.slice(0, range2.from - line.from))[0] : "";
      await this.applyVisibleEdit(range2.from, range2.to, newline + indent, "isolate");
    }
    async indentBlock(outdent) {
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      const context = await this.blockContext(range2.from);
      if (!context) return;
      const first = context.line;
      const lastPosition = range2.to > range2.from ? range2.to - 1 : range2.to;
      const last = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(lastPosition) ?? await this.currentSource.lineAtPosition(lastPosition);
      if (!context.current()) return;
      if (!outdent && context.code && range2.from === range2.to && /[^ \t]/.test(first.text.slice(0, range2.from - first.from))) {
        await this.applyVisibleEdit(
          range2.from,
          range2.to,
          indentationText(this.presentation),
          "isolate"
        );
        return;
      }
      const window = await this.readBlockWindow(first.number, last.number);
      if (!context.current()) return;
      const edits = window.lines.flatMap((line) => {
        const remove = outdent ? presentationOutdent(line.text, this.presentation) : 0;
        return outdent && !remove ? [] : [
          {
            from: line.from,
            to: line.from + remove,
            insert: outdent ? "" : indentationText(this.presentation)
          }
        ];
      });
      if (!edits.length) return;
      const changes = ChangeSet.of(edits);
      const local = ChangeSet.of(
        edits.map((edit2) => ({ ...edit2, from: edit2.from - window.from, to: edit2.to - window.from }))
      );
      const insert = new MemoryTextDocument(window.text).apply(local).text;
      await this.applyVisibleEdit(
        window.from,
        window.to,
        insert,
        "isolate",
        this.currentSelection().map(changes)
      );
    }
    async backspaceBlock() {
      if (await this.deleteTableText(-1)) return;
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      const visible = this.findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(range2.from);
      const prefix = visible ? listEditingPrefix(visible.text) : null;
      const before = visible?.text.slice(0, range2.from - visible.from) ?? "";
      if (range2.from !== range2.to || !/^[ \t]+$/.test(before) && (!prefix || before.length !== prefix[0].length)) {
        await this.deleteAroundCursor(-1);
        return;
      }
      if (range2.from === range2.to) {
        const context = await this.blockContext(range2.from);
        if (!context) return;
        const { line, code } = context;
        const offset = range2.from - line.from;
        const prefix2 = listEditingPrefix(line.text);
        if (!code && prefix2 && offset === prefix2[0].length) {
          const remove = prefix2[1] ? indentRemovalLength(prefix2[1]) : prefix2[0].length;
          const changes = ChangeSet.delete(line.from, line.from + remove);
          await this.applyVisibleEdit(
            line.from,
            line.from + remove,
            "",
            "isolate",
            this.currentSelection().map(changes)
          );
          return;
        }
        const before2 = line.text.slice(0, offset);
        if (/^[ \t]+$/.test(before2)) {
          const remove = before2.endsWith("	") ? 1 : Math.min(offset % 2 || 2, / *$/.exec(before2)[0].length);
          await this.applyVisibleEdit(range2.from - remove, range2.from, "", "isolate");
          return;
        }
      }
      await this.deleteAroundCursor(-1);
    }
    findRenderedSourceLineAtPosition(position) {
      return this.renderedSourceWindow?.lines.find(
        (line) => position >= line.from && position <= line.to
      );
    }
    getSelectedRange() {
      return Object.freeze({
        from: Math.min(this.selectionAnchor, this.cursorPosition),
        to: Math.max(this.selectionAnchor, this.cursorPosition)
      });
    }
    readRenderedSelectionText() {
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      const window = this.renderedSourceWindow;
      if (!window || range2.from === range2.to || range2.from < window.from || range2.to > window.to) {
        return void 0;
      }
      return readLineWindowRangeText(window, range2.from, range2.to);
    }
    currentSelection() {
      return this.selectionAnchor === this.cursorPosition ? Selection.cursor(this.cursorPosition) : Selection.range(this.selectionAnchor, this.cursorPosition);
    }
    renderSelection() {
      this.documentPresentation?.selectionChanged();
      this.selectionLayerDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-selection").forEach((node3) => {
        node3.remove();
      });
      const range2 = this.getSelectedRange();
      if (range2.from === range2.to) {
        return;
      }
      for (const line of Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line"))) {
        const lineFrom = Number(line.dataset.from);
        const lineTo = Number(line.dataset.to);
        const lineNumber = Number(line.dataset.line);
        if (!Number.isInteger(lineFrom) || !Number.isInteger(lineTo) || !Number.isInteger(lineNumber)) {
          continue;
        }
        const from = Math.max(range2.from, lineFrom);
        const to = Math.min(range2.to, lineTo);
        if (to <= from) {
          continue;
        }
        const selection = this.ownerDocument.createElement("div");
        selection.className = "milkup-selection";
        selection.dataset.from = String(from);
        selection.dataset.to = String(to);
        selection.dataset.line = String(lineNumber);
        const rects = domRectsForLineSourceRange(
          this.ownerDocument,
          line,
          this.selectionLayerDOM,
          from,
          to
        );
        if (rects.length === 0) {
          const fromOffset = sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(line, from);
          const toOffset = sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(line, to);
          selection.style.left = `${fromOffset * 8}px`;
          selection.style.top = `${lineLayerTop(
            line,
            this.selectionLayerDOM,
            lineNumber,
            this.getLineHeight()
          )}px`;
          selection.style.width = `${Math.max(1, toOffset - fromOffset) * 8}px`;
          selection.style.height = `${lineVisualHeight(line, this.getLineHeight())}px`;
          this.selectionLayerDOM.append(selection);
          continue;
        }
        for (const [rectIndex, rect] of rects.entries()) {
          const measuredSelection = selection.cloneNode(false);
          measuredSelection.dataset.rectIndex = String(rectIndex);
          measuredSelection.style.left = `${rect.left}px`;
          measuredSelection.style.top = `${rect.top}px`;
          measuredSelection.style.width = `${Math.max(1, rect.width)}px`;
          measuredSelection.style.visibility = rect.width <= 0 ? "hidden" : "";
          measuredSelection.style.height = `${rect.height}px`;
          this.selectionLayerDOM.append(measuredSelection);
        }
      }
    }
    renderSearchHighlights() {
      this.searchLayerDOM.replaceChildren();
      for (const [index, highlight] of this.searchHighlights.entries()) {
        const line = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
          `.milkup-line[data-line="${highlight.line}"]`
        );
        if (!line) {
          continue;
        }
        const lineFrom = Number(line.dataset.from);
        const lineTo = Number(line.dataset.to);
        const from = Math.max(highlight.from, lineFrom);
        const to = Math.min(highlight.to, lineTo);
        if (!Number.isInteger(lineFrom) || !Number.isInteger(lineTo) || to <= from) {
          continue;
        }
        const element = this.ownerDocument.createElement("div");
        element.className = index === this.activeSearchHighlightIndex ? "milkup-search-highlight is-active" : "milkup-search-highlight";
        element.dataset.index = String(index);
        element.dataset.from = String(from);
        element.dataset.to = String(to);
        element.dataset.line = String(highlight.line);
        const rects = domRectsForLineSourceRange(
          this.ownerDocument,
          line,
          this.searchLayerDOM,
          from,
          to
        );
        if (rects.length === 0) {
          const fromOffset = sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(line, from);
          const toOffset = sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(line, to);
          element.style.left = `${fromOffset * 8}px`;
          element.style.top = `${lineLayerTop(
            line,
            this.searchLayerDOM,
            highlight.line,
            this.getLineHeight()
          )}px`;
          element.style.width = `${Math.max(1, toOffset - fromOffset) * 8}px`;
          element.style.height = `${lineVisualHeight(line, this.getLineHeight())}px`;
          this.searchLayerDOM.append(element);
          continue;
        }
        for (const [rectIndex, rect] of rects.entries()) {
          const measured = element.cloneNode(false);
          measured.dataset.rectIndex = String(rectIndex);
          measured.style.left = `${rect.left}px`;
          measured.style.top = `${rect.top}px`;
          measured.style.width = `${Math.max(1, rect.width)}px`;
          measured.style.visibility = rect.width <= 0 ? "hidden" : "";
          measured.style.height = `${rect.height}px`;
          this.searchLayerDOM.append(measured);
        }
      }
    }
    renderCursor() {
      syncTaskAvailability(this.contentDOM, this.editable && !!this.onEdit && !this.taskTogglePending);
      this.cursorLayerDOM.querySelector(".milkup-cursor")?.remove();
      if (!this.editable) {
        return;
      }
      const line = Array.from(this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line")).find(
        (item) => {
          const from = Number(item.dataset.from);
          const to = Number(item.dataset.to);
          return this.cursorPosition >= from && this.cursorPosition <= to;
        }
      );
      if (!line) {
        return;
      }
      const lineFrom = Number(line.dataset.from);
      const lineNumber = Number(line.dataset.line);
      if (!Number.isInteger(lineFrom) || !Number.isInteger(lineNumber)) {
        return;
      }
      const cursor2 = this.ownerDocument.createElement("div");
      cursor2.className = "milkup-cursor";
      cursor2.dataset.position = String(this.cursorPosition);
      cursor2.dataset.line = String(lineNumber);
      cursor2.dataset.offset = String(Math.max(0, this.cursorPosition - lineFrom));
      const rect = domRectForLineSourcePosition(
        this.ownerDocument,
        line,
        this.cursorLayerDOM,
        this.cursorPosition,
        { allowGeometryFallback: true, fallbackLineHeight: this.getLineHeight() }
      );
      const height = lineCursorHeight(line, this.getLineHeight());
      if (rect) {
        cursor2.style.visibility = isCodePositionClipped(line, this.cursorLayerDOM, rect) ? "hidden" : "";
        cursor2.style.left = `${rect.left}px`;
        cursor2.style.top = `${rect.top}px`;
        cursor2.style.height = `${height}px`;
      } else {
        cursor2.style.left = `${sourcePositionToVisualOffsetInLine(line, this.cursorPosition) * 8}px`;
        cursor2.style.top = `${lineLayerTop(
          line,
          this.cursorLayerDOM,
          lineNumber,
          this.getLineHeight()
        )}px`;
        cursor2.style.height = `${height}px`;
      }
      this.cursorLayerDOM.append(cursor2);
      this.syncInputProxyToCursor();
      this.renderCompositionText();
      this.dom.dispatchEvent(new Event("milkup-selection-change"));
    }
    renderCompositionText() {
      this.cursorLayerDOM.querySelector(".milkup-composition")?.remove();
      if (!this.composing || this.compositionText.length === 0) {
        return;
      }
      const cursor2 = this.cursorLayerDOM.querySelector(".milkup-cursor");
      if (!cursor2) {
        return;
      }
      const composition = this.ownerDocument.createElement("span");
      composition.className = "milkup-composition";
      composition.textContent = this.compositionText;
      composition.style.left = cursor2.style.left;
      composition.style.top = cursor2.style.top;
      composition.style.minHeight = cursor2.style.height;
      this.cursorLayerDOM.append(composition);
    }
    syncInputProxyToCursor() {
      const cursor2 = this.cursorLayerDOM.querySelector(".milkup-cursor");
      if (!cursor2) {
        return;
      }
      const left = Number.parseFloat(cursor2.style.left);
      const top = Number.parseFloat(cursor2.style.top);
      const height = Number.parseFloat(cursor2.style.height);
      if (!Number.isFinite(left) || !Number.isFinite(top)) {
        return;
      }
      this.inputDOM.style.left = `${Math.max(0, this.cursorLayerDOM.offsetLeft + left)}px`;
      this.inputDOM.style.top = `${Math.max(0, this.cursorLayerDOM.offsetTop + top)}px`;
      if (Number.isFinite(height) && height > 0) {
        this.inputDOM.style.height = `${height}px`;
      }
    }
    async renderLiveSyntaxAroundPositions(previousPosition, nextPosition) {
      if (this.mode !== "live") {
        return;
      }
      const lineNumbers = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      for (const position of [previousPosition, nextPosition]) {
        const line = this.findRenderedLineAtPosition(position);
        if (line) {
          lineNumbers.add(line.number);
        }
      }
      for (const lineNumber of lineNumbers) {
        await this.renderRenderedLine(lineNumber);
      }
    }
    async renderRenderedLine(lineNumber) {
      const current = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
        `.milkup-line[data-line="${lineNumber}"]`
      );
      if (!current) {
        return;
      }
      const source = this.currentSource, version = source.version, revision = this.editRevision;
      const replacement = this.mode === "live" ? await this.renderSingleLiveLine(lineNumber) : await this.renderSingleSourceLine(lineNumber);
      if (replacement && !this.destroyed && source === this.currentSource && version === source.version && revision === this.editRevision && this.contentDOM.contains(current)) {
        const scrollLeft = current.scrollLeft;
        const active = this.ownerDocument.activeElement;
        const focusedTask = active instanceof HTMLElement && current.contains(active) && active.classList.contains("milkup-task-marker") ? active.dataset.from : void 0;
        current.replaceWith(replacement);
        replacement.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
        syncTaskAvailability(replacement, this.editable && !!this.onEdit && !this.taskTogglePending);
        if (focusedTask !== void 0)
          replacement.querySelector(`.milkup-task-marker[data-from="${focusedTask}"]`)?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    }
    applyOptimisticRenderedLineEdit(line, from, to, insert) {
      const lineDOM = this.contentDOM.querySelector(
        `.milkup-line[data-line="${line.number}"]`
      );
      if (!lineDOM) {
        return;
      }
      const delta = insert.length - (to - from);
      const mapped = Array.from(lineDOM.querySelectorAll("[data-from][data-to]")).filter((element) => !element.classList.contains("milkup-marker-hidden")).find((element) => {
        const elementFrom = Number(element.dataset.from);
        const elementTo = Number(element.dataset.to);
        return Number.isInteger(elementFrom) && Number.isInteger(elementTo) && from >= elementFrom && to <= elementTo;
      });
      if (mapped) {
        const elementFrom = Number(mapped.dataset.from);
        const elementTo = Number(mapped.dataset.to);
        const text2 = mapped.textContent ?? "";
        if (mapped.classList.contains("milkup-task-marker")) {
          mapped.dataset.checked = String(insert !== " ");
          mapped.setAttribute("aria-checked", mapped.dataset.checked);
        } else
          mapped.textContent = text2.slice(0, from - elementFrom) + insert + text2.slice(to - elementFrom);
        mapped.dataset.to = String(elementTo + delta);
        for (const element of Array.from(
          lineDOM.querySelectorAll("[data-from][data-to]")
        )) {
          if (element === mapped) {
            continue;
          }
          const nextFrom = Number(element.dataset.from);
          const nextTo = Number(element.dataset.to);
          if (Number.isInteger(nextFrom) && nextFrom >= to) {
            element.dataset.from = String(nextFrom + delta);
          }
          if (Number.isInteger(nextTo) && nextTo >= to) {
            element.dataset.to = String(nextTo + delta);
          }
        }
      } else {
        const text2 = lineDOM.textContent?.replace(/\u200b/g, "") ?? line.text;
        lineDOM.textContent = text2.slice(0, from - line.from) + insert + text2.slice(to - line.from);
      }
      lineDOM.dataset.to = String(line.to + delta);
    }
    async renderSingleSourceLine(lineNumber) {
      const window = await this.currentSource.readLineWindow(lineNumber, lineNumber);
      const line = window.lines[0];
      return line ? renderSourceLine(this.ownerDocument, line) : void 0;
    }
    async renderSingleLiveLine(lineNumber) {
      const fromLine = Math.max(1, lineNumber - this.markdownContextLines);
      const toLine = Math.min(this.currentSource.lineCount, lineNumber + this.markdownContextLines);
      const parsed = await this.parseLiveWindow(fromLine, toLine);
      const line = parsed.window.lines.find((item) => item.number === lineNumber);
      return line ? renderLiveLine(this.ownerDocument, line, parsed.root.children ?? [], {
        cursorPosition: this.cursorPosition,
        selection: this.currentSelection()
      }) : void 0;
    }
    shiftRenderedLinePositionsAfter(lineNumber, delta) {
      if (delta === 0) {
        return;
      }
      for (const lineDOM of Array.from(
        this.contentDOM.querySelectorAll(".milkup-line")
      )) {
        const renderedLineNumber = Number(lineDOM.dataset.line);
        if (!Number.isInteger(renderedLineNumber) || renderedLineNumber <= lineNumber) {
          continue;
        }
        const from = Number(lineDOM.dataset.from);
        const to = Number(lineDOM.dataset.to);
        if (Number.isInteger(from)) {
          lineDOM.dataset.from = String(from + delta);
        }
        if (Number.isInteger(to)) {
          lineDOM.dataset.to = String(to + delta);
        }
        for (const mapped of Array.from(
          lineDOM.querySelectorAll("[data-from][data-to]")
        )) {
          const mappedFrom = Number(mapped.dataset.from);
          const mappedTo = Number(mapped.dataset.to);
          if (Number.isInteger(mappedFrom)) {
            mapped.dataset.from = String(mappedFrom + delta);
          }
          if (Number.isInteger(mappedTo)) {
            mapped.dataset.to = String(mappedTo + delta);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  };
  function renderSourceLine(document, line) {
    const lineDOM = createLineElement(document, line);
    lineDOM.textContent = line.text.length > 0 ? line.text : "\u200B";
    return lineDOM;
  }
  function renderLiveLine(document, line, blocks, options = {}) {
    const lineDOM = createLineElement(document, line);
    lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-line-live");
    applyLiveBlockClasses(lineDOM, line, blocks);
    const selection = options.selection ?? Selection.cursor(options.cursorPosition ?? line.from);
    const heading = findBlockForLine(blocks, "heading", line.from, line.to);
    const blockquoteLine = findBlockForLine(blocks, "blockquoteLine", line.from, line.to);
    const tableRow = findBlockForLine(blocks, "tableRow", line.from, line.to);
    const table = findBlockForLine(blocks, "table", line.from, line.to);
    const listItem = findListItemForLine(blocks, line.from, line.to) ?? fallbackListItemForLine(line, blocks);
    if (lineDOM.classList.contains("milkup-block-code")) {
      lineDOM.textContent = line.text.length > 0 ? line.text : "\u200B";
    } else if (heading) {
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(
        ...renderMappedLinePieces(document, line, heading, {
          contentClassName: "milkup-heading-content",
          markerClassName: markerClassNameForSelection(
            "milkup-block-marker milkup-heading-marker",
            line,
            heading,
            selection
          ),
          selection
        })
      );
    } else if (blockquoteLine) {
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(
        ...renderMappedLinePieces(document, line, blockquoteLine, {
          contentClassName: "milkup-blockquote-content",
          markerClassName: markerClassNameForSelection(
            "milkup-block-marker milkup-blockquote-marker",
            line,
            blockquoteLine,
            selection
          ),
          selection
        })
      );
    } else if (tableRow) {
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(
        ...renderMappedLinePieces(document, line, tableRow, {
          contentClassName: "milkup-table-cell",
          markerClassName: "milkup-block-marker milkup-table-marker milkup-marker-hidden",
          hideContentGaps: true,
          preserveEmptyContentRanges: true,
          selection
        })
      );
    } else if (table) {
      lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-table-delimiter");
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(
        ...renderHiddenLine(
          document,
          line,
          "milkup-block-marker milkup-table-marker milkup-marker-hidden"
        )
      );
    } else if (listItem) {
      lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-list");
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(...renderListItemLinePieces(document, line, selection, listItem));
    } else {
      lineDOM.replaceChildren(
        ...renderInlineDecorations(document, line, selection, line.from, line.to)
      );
    }
    applyTableAlignments(lineDOM, table);
    return lineDOM;
  }
  function markerClassNameForSelection(baseClassName, line, node3, selection) {
    const shouldShow = selection.ranges.some(
      (range2) => range2.empty ? range2.head >= line.from && range2.head <= line.to && range2.head >= node3.from && range2.head <= node3.to : range2.from < node3.to && range2.to > node3.from
    );
    return shouldShow ? baseClassName : `${baseClassName} milkup-marker-hidden`;
  }
  function createLineElement(document, line) {
    const lineDOM = document.createElement("div");
    lineDOM.className = "milkup-line";
    lineDOM.dataset.line = String(line.number);
    lineDOM.dataset.from = String(line.from);
    lineDOM.dataset.to = String(line.to);
    return lineDOM;
  }
  function readLineWindowRangeText(window, from, to) {
    if (from === to) {
      return "";
    }
    const pieces = [];
    let position = from;
    for (const line of window.lines) {
      if (line.to < from || line.from > to) {
        continue;
      }
      if (position < line.from) {
        pieces.push(readWindowGapText(window, position, Math.min(line.from, to)));
        position = Math.min(line.from, to);
      }
      const lineFrom = Math.max(position, line.from);
      const lineTo = Math.min(to, line.to);
      if (lineTo > lineFrom) {
        pieces.push(line.text.slice(lineFrom - line.from, lineTo - line.from));
        position = lineTo;
      }
      if (position >= to) {
        break;
      }
    }
    if (position < to) {
      pieces.push(readWindowGapText(window, position, to));
    }
    return pieces.join("");
  }
  function readWindowGapText(window, from, to) {
    const text2 = window.text.slice(from - window.from, to - window.from);
    return text2.length === to - from ? text2 : "\n".repeat(to - from);
  }
  function mergeSourceDocumentEdits(previous, next) {
    if (previous.historyGroup === "isolate" || next.historyGroup === "isolate") return void 0;
    const selections = {
      selectionBefore: previous.selectionBefore,
      selectionAfter: next.selectionAfter
    };
    if (previous.from === previous.to && next.from === previous.from + previous.insert.length && next.from === next.to && previous.deletedText.length === 0 && next.deletedText.length === 0) {
      return Object.freeze({
        from: previous.from,
        to: previous.to,
        insert: previous.insert + next.insert,
        deletedText: "",
        ...selections
      });
    }
    if (previous.insert.length === 0 && next.insert.length === 0 && next.from === previous.from && next.to === previous.from + next.deletedText.length) {
      return Object.freeze({
        from: previous.from,
        to: previous.to + next.deletedText.length,
        insert: "",
        deletedText: previous.deletedText + next.deletedText,
        ...selections
      });
    }
    if (previous.insert.length === 0 && next.insert.length === 0 && next.to === previous.from && next.from + next.deletedText.length === previous.from) {
      return Object.freeze({
        from: next.from,
        to: previous.to,
        insert: "",
        deletedText: next.deletedText + previous.deletedText,
        ...selections
      });
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function applyLiveBlockClasses(lineDOM, line, blocks) {
    for (const block of walkSyntaxNodes(blocks)) {
      if (!rangeContainsLine(block, line.from, line.to)) {
        continue;
      }
      if (block.type === "heading") {
        lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-heading");
        const level = block.data?.level;
        if (typeof level === "number") {
          lineDOM.classList.add(`milkup-heading-level-${level}`);
        }
      }
      if (block.type === "blockquote") {
        lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-blockquote");
      }
      if (block.type === "unorderedList" || block.type === "orderedList") {
        lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-list");
      }
      if (block.type === "fencedCode") {
        lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-code");
        const closing = block.markerRanges?.at(-1);
        if (block.status === "valid" && closing && (block.markerRanges?.length ?? 0) >= 2) {
          lineDOM.dataset.presentationBlockFrom = String(block.from);
          lineDOM.dataset.presentationBlockTo = String(closing.to);
        }
      }
      if (block.type === "table") {
        lineDOM.classList.add("milkup-block-table");
      }
    }
  }
  function renderMappedLinePieces(document, line, node3, classNames) {
    const pieces = [
      ...(node3.markerRanges ?? []).map((range2) => ({
        ...range2,
        className: classNames.markerClassName
      })),
      ...(node3.contentRanges ?? []).map((range2) => ({
        ...range2,
        className: classNames.contentClassName
      }))
    ].map((piece) => ({
      ...piece,
      from: Math.max(piece.from, line.from),
      to: Math.min(piece.to, line.to)
    })).filter(
      (piece) => piece.to > piece.from || classNames.preserveEmptyContentRanges === true && piece.to === piece.from
    ).sort((left, right) => left.from - right.from);
    if (pieces.length === 0) {
      return renderHiddenLine(document, line, classNames.contentClassName);
    }
    const rendered = [];
    let position = line.from;
    for (const piece of pieces) {
      if (piece.from > position) {
        rendered.push(
          createMappedSpan(
            document,
            line,
            position,
            piece.from,
            classNames.hideContentGaps ? classNames.markerClassName : "milkup-live-text"
          )
        );
      }
      if (piece.className === classNames.contentClassName) {
        const content = createMappedSpan(document, line, piece.from, piece.to, piece.className);
        if (piece.to > piece.from) {
          content.replaceChildren(
            ...renderInlineDecorations(document, line, classNames.selection, piece.from, piece.to)
          );
        }
        rendered.push(content);
      } else {
        rendered.push(createMappedSpan(document, line, piece.from, piece.to, piece.className));
      }
      position = Math.max(position, piece.to);
    }
    if (position < line.to) {
      rendered.push(
        createMappedSpan(
          document,
          line,
          position,
          line.to,
          classNames.hideContentGaps ? classNames.markerClassName : "milkup-live-text"
        )
      );
    }
    return Object.freeze(rendered.length > 0 ? rendered : [document.createTextNode("\u200B")]);
  }
  function renderHiddenLine(document, line, className) {
    if (line.text.length === 0) {
      return Object.freeze([document.createTextNode("\u200B")]);
    }
    return Object.freeze([createMappedSpan(document, line, line.from, line.to, className)]);
  }
  function renderListItemLinePieces(document, line, selection, listItem) {
    const pieces = collectListItemLinePieces(line, listItem);
    if (pieces.length === 0) {
      return renderInlineDecorations(document, line, selection, line.from, line.to);
    }
    const rendered = [];
    let position = line.from;
    for (const piece of pieces) {
      if (piece.from > position) {
        rendered.push(createMappedSpan(document, line, position, piece.from, "milkup-live-text"));
      }
      if (piece.kind === "marker") {
        const marker = createMappedSpan(document, line, piece.from, piece.to, "milkup-list-marker");
        marker.textContent = formatListMarker(sourceSlice(line, piece.from, piece.to));
        if (pieces.some((piece2) => piece2.kind === "taskMarker"))
          marker.classList.add("milkup-marker-hidden");
        rendered.push(marker);
      } else if (piece.kind === "taskMarker") {
        const task = createTaskMarker(
          document,
          piece.from,
          piece.to,
          sourceSlice(line, piece.from, piece.to)
        );
        rendered.push(task);
      } else {
        const content = createMappedSpan(document, line, piece.from, piece.to, "milkup-list-content");
        content.replaceChildren(
          ...renderInlineDecorations(document, line, selection, piece.from, piece.to)
        );
        rendered.push(content);
      }
      position = piece.to;
    }
    if (position < line.to) {
      rendered.push(createMappedSpan(document, line, position, line.to, "milkup-live-text"));
    }
    return Object.freeze(rendered);
  }
  function collectListItemLinePieces(line, listItem) {
    const pieces = [];
    for (const range2 of listItem.markerRanges ?? []) {
      if (rangesIntersect(range2.from, range2.to, line.from, line.to)) {
        pieces.push({
          from: clamp4(range2.from, line.from, line.to),
          to: clamp4(range2.to, line.from, line.to),
          kind: "marker"
        });
      }
    }
    for (const range2 of listItem.contentRanges ?? []) {
      if (!rangesIntersect(range2.from, range2.to, line.from, line.to)) {
        continue;
      }
      const taskMarker = taskMarkerRange(line, range2.from, range2.to);
      if (taskMarker && rangesIntersect(taskMarker.from, taskMarker.to, line.from, line.to)) {
        pieces.push({
          from: clamp4(taskMarker.from, line.from, line.to),
          to: clamp4(taskMarker.to, line.from, line.to),
          kind: "taskMarker"
        });
      }
      const contentFrom = taskMarker ? taskMarker.to : range2.from;
      const pieceFrom = clamp4(contentFrom, line.from, line.to);
      const pieceTo = clamp4(range2.to, line.from, line.to);
      if (pieceTo > pieceFrom) {
        pieces.push({
          from: pieceFrom,
          to: pieceTo,
          kind: "content"
        });
      }
    }
    return Object.freeze(pieces.sort((left, right) => left.from - right.from || left.to - right.to));
  }
  function renderInlineDecorations(document, line, selection, from, to) {
    if (to <= from) {
      return [document.createTextNode("\u200B")];
    }
    const localFrom = from - line.from;
    const localTo = to - line.from;
    const nodes = parseInline(line.text, localFrom, localTo);
    const fragments = [];
    let position = localFrom;
    for (const node3 of nodes) {
      if (node3.from > position) {
        fragments.push(
          createMappedSpan(
            document,
            line,
            line.from + position,
            line.from + node3.from,
            "milkup-live-text"
          )
        );
      }
      fragments.push(renderInlineNode(document, line, selection, node3));
      position = node3.to;
    }
    if (position < localTo) {
      fragments.push(
        createMappedSpan(
          document,
          line,
          line.from + position,
          line.from + localTo,
          "milkup-live-text"
        )
      );
    }
    return Object.freeze(fragments);
  }
  function renderInlineNode(document, line, selection, node3) {
    const span = document.createElement("span");
    span.className = `milkup-inline milkup-inline-${node3.type}`;
    span.dataset.from = String(line.from + node3.from);
    span.dataset.to = String(line.from + node3.to);
    span.dataset.status = node3.status;
    span.dataset.syntaxVisible = String(shouldShowInlineSyntax(line, node3, selection));
    span.replaceChildren(...renderInlineNodePieces(document, line, selection, node3));
    if (node3.type === "image" && node3.status === "valid" && typeof node3.data?.destination === "string") {
      span.dataset.imageUrl = node3.data.destination;
      span.dataset.imageAlt = typeof node3.data.label === "string" ? node3.data.label : "";
    }
    if (node3.type === "strikethrough") span.style.textDecoration = "line-through";
    if (node3.type === "hardBreak" && node3.data?.kind === "html")
      span.append(document.createElement("br"));
    return span;
  }
  function renderInlineNodePieces(document, line, selection, node3) {
    const pieces = collectInlinePieces(node3);
    if (pieces.length === 0) {
      return [
        createMappedSpan(
          document,
          line,
          line.from + node3.from,
          line.from + node3.to,
          "milkup-live-text"
        )
      ];
    }
    const showSyntax = shouldShowInlineSyntax(line, node3, selection);
    const rendered = [];
    let position = node3.from;
    for (const piece of pieces) {
      if (piece.from > position) {
        rendered.push(
          createMappedSpan(
            document,
            line,
            line.from + position,
            line.from + piece.from,
            "milkup-live-text"
          )
        );
      }
      const span = createMappedSpan(
        document,
        line,
        line.from + piece.from,
        line.from + piece.to,
        `milkup-inline-${piece.kind}`
      );
      if (piece.kind === "content" && node3.children?.some((child) => child.type !== "text")) {
        span.replaceChildren(
          ...renderInlineDecorations(
            document,
            line,
            selection,
            line.from + piece.from,
            line.from + piece.to
          )
        );
      }
      if (piece.kind !== "content" && !showSyntax) {
        span.classList.add("milkup-marker-hidden");
      }
      rendered.push(span);
      position = piece.to;
    }
    if (position < node3.to) {
      rendered.push(
        createMappedSpan(
          document,
          line,
          line.from + position,
          line.from + node3.to,
          "milkup-live-text"
        )
      );
    }
    return Object.freeze(rendered);
  }
  function collectInlinePieces(node3) {
    const pieces = [];
    for (const range2 of node3.markerRanges ?? []) {
      pieces.push({ ...range2, kind: "marker" });
    }
    for (const [index, range2] of (node3.contentRanges ?? []).entries()) {
      pieces.push({
        ...range2,
        kind: node3.type === "link" && index > 0 ? "syntax" : "content"
      });
    }
    return Object.freeze(pieces.sort((left, right) => left.from - right.from || left.to - right.to));
  }
  function shouldShowInlineSyntax(line, node3, selection) {
    if (node3.status !== "valid") {
      return true;
    }
    const from = line.from + node3.from;
    const to = line.from + node3.to;
    return selection.ranges.some(
      (range2) => range2.empty ? range2.head >= from && range2.head <= to : range2.from < to && range2.to > from
    );
  }
  function createMappedSpan(document, line, from, to, className) {
    const span = document.createElement("span");
    span.className = className;
    span.dataset.from = String(from);
    span.dataset.to = String(to);
