    span.textContent = line.text.slice(from - line.from, to - line.from);
    return span;
  }
  function sourceSlice(line, from, to) {
    return line.text.slice(from - line.from, to - line.from);
  }
  function formatListMarker(marker) {
    const trimmed = marker.trim();
    if (trimmed === "-" || trimmed === "*" || trimmed === "+") {
      return "\u2022";
    }
    return trimmed;
  }
  function taskMarkerRange(line, from, to) {
    const text2 = sourceSlice(line, from, to);
    const match = /^(\s*\[[ xX]\]\s*)/u.exec(text2);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    return { from, to: from + (match[1]?.length ?? 0) };
  }
  function listContinuationPrefix(lineText) {
    const unordered = /^(\s*)([-+*])([ \t]+)(?:\[[ xX]\]([ \t]+))?/u.exec(lineText);
    if (unordered) {
      return `${unordered[1] ?? ""}${unordered[2] ?? "-"}${unordered[3] ?? " "}${unordered[4] === void 0 ? "" : `[ ]${unordered[4]}`}`;
    }
    const ordered = /^(\s*)(\d{1,9})([.)])([ \t]+)(?:\[[ xX]\]([ \t]+))?/u.exec(lineText);
    if (!ordered) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const numberText = ordered[2] ?? "1";
    const nextNumber = String(Number.parseInt(numberText, 10) + 1).padStart(numberText.length, "0");
    return `${ordered[1] ?? ""}${nextNumber}${ordered[3] ?? "."}${ordered[4] ?? " "}${ordered[5] === void 0 ? "" : `[ ]${ordered[5]}`}`;
  }
  function listSyntaxContentStart(lineText, lineFrom) {
    const unordered = /^(\s*)([-+*])([ \t]+)(?:\[[ xX]\]([ \t]+))?/u.exec(lineText);
    if (unordered) {
      return lineFrom + (unordered[0]?.length ?? 0);
    }
    const ordered = /^(\s*)(\d{1,9})([.)])([ \t]+)(?:\[[ xX]\]([ \t]+))?/u.exec(lineText);
    return ordered ? lineFrom + (ordered[0]?.length ?? 0) : void 0;
  }
  function isCompositionKeyEvent(event) {
    return event.isComposing || event.key === "Process";
  }
  function findBlockForLine(blocks, type, lineFrom, lineTo) {
    for (const block of walkSyntaxNodes(blocks)) {
      if (block.type === type && rangeContainsLine(block, lineFrom, lineTo)) {
        return block;
      }
    }
    return void 0;
  }
  function findListItemForLine(blocks, lineFrom, lineTo) {
    let containingItem;
    for (const block of walkSyntaxNodes(blocks)) {
      if (block.type !== "listItem" || !rangeContainsLine(block, lineFrom, lineTo)) {
        continue;
      }
      if ((block.markerRanges ?? []).some(
        (range2) => rangesIntersect(range2.from, range2.to, lineFrom, Math.max(lineFrom + 1, lineTo))
      )) {
        return block;
      }
      if (!containingItem || block.to - block.from < containingItem.to - containingItem.from) {
        containingItem = block;
      }
    }
    return containingItem;
  }
  function fallbackListItemForLine(line, blocks) {
    const match = /^(\s*)(?:([-+*])|(\d{1,9})([.)]))(?:[ \t]+|$)/u.exec(line.text);
    if (!match) {
      return void 0;
    }
    if ([...walkSyntaxNodes(blocks)].some(
      (block) => (block.type === "fencedCode" || block.type === "indentedCode") && rangeContainsLine(block, line.from, line.to)
    )) {
      return void 0;
    }
    const indentationLength = match[1]?.length ?? 0;
    const markerLength = (match[2] ?? "").length + (match[3] ?? "").length + (match[4] ?? "").length;
    const markerFrom = line.from + indentationLength;
    const markerTo = markerFrom + markerLength;
    return {
      type: "listItem",
      from: line.from,
      to: line.to,
      status: "valid",
      markerRanges: [{ from: markerFrom, to: markerTo }],
      contentRanges: [{ from: line.from + match[0].length, to: line.to }]
    };
  }
  function* walkSyntaxNodes(nodes) {
    for (const node3 of nodes) {
      yield node3;
      if (node3.children) {
        yield* walkSyntaxNodes(node3.children);
      }
    }
  }
  function rangeContainsLine(node3, lineFrom, lineTo) {
    if (lineFrom === lineTo) {
      return node3.from <= lineFrom && node3.to >= lineFrom;
    }
    return node3.from <= lineFrom && node3.to >= lineTo;
  }
  function rangesIntersect(leftFrom, leftTo, rightFrom, rightTo) {
    return leftFrom < rightTo && leftTo > rightFrom;
  }
  function renderSpacer(document, position, height) {
    const spacer = document.createElement("div");
    spacer.className = "milkup-virtual-spacer";
    spacer.dataset.spacer = position;
    spacer.style.height = `${Math.max(0, height)}px`;
    return spacer;
  }
  function lineLayerTop(lineDOM, layerDOM, lineNumber, lineHeight) {
    const measuredTop = lineDOM.offsetTop - layerDOM.offsetTop;
    if (measuredTop !== 0) {
      return measuredTop;
    }
    return lineNumber <= 1 ? 0 : (lineNumber - 1) * lineHeight;
  }
  function lineVisualHeight(lineDOM, lineHeight) {
    const rect = lineDOM.getBoundingClientRect();
    return rect.height > 0 ? rect.height : lineHeight;
  }
  function lineCursorHeight(lineDOM, fallbackLineHeight) {
    const view = lineDOM.ownerDocument.defaultView;
    const inlineFontSize = Number.parseFloat(lineDOM.style.fontSize);
    const computedFontSize = view ? Number.parseFloat(view.getComputedStyle(lineDOM).fontSize) : 0;
    const fontSize = Number.isFinite(inlineFontSize) && inlineFontSize > 0 ? inlineFontSize : computedFontSize;
    if (Number.isFinite(fontSize) && fontSize > 0) {
      return fontSize;
    }
    return fallbackLineHeight;
  }
  function clamp4(value, min, max) {
    return Math.min(Math.max(value, min), max);
  }
  function listEditingPrefix(line) {
    return /^([ \t]*)(?:[-+*]|\d{1,9}[.)])[ \t]+(?:\[[ xX]\][ \t]+)?/u.exec(line);
  }
  function indentRemovalLength(text2) {
    return /^(?:\t| {1,2})/u.exec(text2)?.[0].length ?? 0;
  }
  return __toCommonJS(entry_exports);
})();
